internal class ResourceCustomizerLocator
{
	internal static readonly ResourceCustomizer Customizer = new ResourceCustomizer();

	private ResourceCustomizerLocator()
	{
	}
}
internal sealed class SR
{
	private static global::SR loader;

	[ThreadStatic]
	private static Dictionary<DynamicResourceString.ID, DynamicResourceString> _dynamicResourceStrings;

	private ResourceManager[] resources;

	private SR()
	{
		string[] array = "Infragistics.Controls.Editors.strings".Split(';');
		int num = 0;
		string[] array2 = array;
		foreach (string text in array2)
		{
			if (text != null && text.Length > 0)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		resources = new ResourceManager[num];
		Assembly assembly = GetType().Assembly;
		int num2 = 0;
		array2 = array;
		foreach (string text2 in array2)
		{
			if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
			{
				resources[num2++] = new ResourceManager(text2, assembly);
			}
		}
	}

	public static DynamicResourceString GetDynamicResourceString(string name, object[] args, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		lock (typeof(global::SR))
		{
			DynamicResourceString.ID key = new DynamicResourceString.ID(culture, name, args);
			DynamicResourceString value = null;
			if (_dynamicResourceStrings == null)
			{
				_dynamicResourceStrings = new Dictionary<DynamicResourceString.ID, DynamicResourceString>();
			}
			else
			{
				_dynamicResourceStrings.TryGetValue(key, out value);
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				value = new InternalDynamicResourceString(ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer, culture, name, args);
				_dynamicResourceStrings.Add(key, value);
			}
			return value;
		}
	}

	private static global::SR GetLoader()
	{
		if (loader == null)
		{
			lock (typeof(global::SR))
			{
				if (loader == null)
				{
					loader = new global::SR();
				}
			}
		}
		return loader;
	}

	public static string GetString(string name)
	{
		return GetString(null, name);
	}

	public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetString(null, name, args);
	}

	public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
	{
		return GetString(culture, name, null);
	}

	public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: true, culture, name, args);
	}

	private static string GetStringHelper(bool allowCustomizedStrings, CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
	{
		string text = null;
		if (allowCustomizedStrings)
		{
			text = ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer.GetCustomizedString(name);
		}
		if (text == null)
		{
			global::SR sR = GetLoader();
			if (sR == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			text = sR.GetString(name, culture);
			if (text == null)
			{
				text = string.Empty;
			}
		}
		if (args != null && args.Length != 0 && text.Length > 2)
		{
			try
			{
				text = string.Format(null, text, args);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
		return text;
	}

	private string GetString(string name, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		ResourceManager[] array = resources;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			string text = array[i].GetString(name, culture);
			if (text != null)
			{
				return text;
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name)
	{
		return GetUncustomizedString(null, name);
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetUncustomizedString(null, name, args);
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
	{
		return GetUncustomizedString(culture, name, null);
	}

	public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
	{
		return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: false, culture, name, args);
	}

	public static object GetObject(string name)
	{
		return GetObject(null, name);
	}

	public static object GetObject(CultureInfo culture, string name)
	{
		return GetLoader()?.GetObject(name, culture);
	}

	private object GetObject(string name, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		ResourceManager[] array = resources;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			object obj = array[i].GetObject(name, culture);
			if (obj != null)
			{
				return obj;
			}
		}
		return null;
	}
}
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
internal sealed class LocalizedCategoryAttribute : CategoryAttribute
{
	public LocalizedCategoryAttribute(string category)
		: base(category)
	{
	}

	protected override string GetLocalizedString(string value)
	{
		return global::SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, value);
	}
}
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
internal sealed class LocalizedDescriptionAttribute : DescriptionAttribute
{
	private bool loaded;

	public override string Description
	{
		get
		{
			if (!loaded)
			{
				loaded = true;
				base.DescriptionValue = global::SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, base.Description);
			}
			return base.Description;
		}
	}

	public LocalizedDescriptionAttribute(string description)
		: base(description)
	{
	}
}
internal sealed class InternalDynamicResourceString : DynamicResourceString
{
	internal InternalDynamicResourceString(ResourceCustomizer customizer, CultureInfo cultureInfo, string resourceName, object[] args)
		: base(customizer, cultureInfo, resourceName, args)
	{
	}

	protected override string GetStringFromResource(string resourceName, CultureInfo culture)
	{
		return global::SR.GetString(culture, resourceName, null);
	}
}

			: base(owner)

		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;

namespace Infragistics.Windows.Ime
{
	internal interface IImeEditor
	{
		FrameworkElement EditorElement { get; }

		ImeMessageHookMode ImeMessageHookMode { get; }

		Rect PreferredCompositionWindowRect { get; }

		ImeEditorHelperState ImeEditorHelperState { get; }

		int SelectionLength { get; }

		int SelectionStart { get; }

		int SelectionEnd { get; }

		string DebugId { get; }

		Rect GetCandidateWindowExclusionRect(int compositionStartOffset, int compositionEndOffset);

		Tuple<int, int> SetSelection(int start, int length);

		void DeleteRange(int start, int length);

		void InsertText(int offset, string text, bool advanceCaret, ImeClauseAdornmentStyle adornmentStyle);

		void EnsureCaretIsVisible();

		void OnCompositionStarting();

		void OnCompositionEnding(bool compositionDiscarded);
	}
	internal class ImeEditorHelper
	{
		private HwndSourceHelper _hwndSourceHelper;

		private bool _avoidHwndSourceHelper;

		private bool _avoidTsfThreadManager;

		private bool _imeMessageProcessingActive;

		[SecurityCritical]
		private ImeNativeWindowMethods.ITfThreadMgr _tsfThreadManager;

		private int _compositionStartOffset = -1;

		private int _compositionLength;

		private IntPtr _inputContext;

		private IntPtr _previousInputContext;

		private int _caretOffsetResolved;

		private bool _imeMessageBeingProcessed;

		private bool _endCompositionMessageReceived;

		internal const string CULTURE_KOREAN = "ko-KR";

		private PropertyValueTracker _isKeyBoardFocusedTracker;

		private int CompositionStartOffset
		{
			get
			{
				if (_compositionStartOffset == -1)
				{
					return ImeEditor.SelectionStart;
				}
				return _compositionStartOffset;
			}
		}

		private HwndSourceHelper HwndSourceHelper
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hwndSourceHelper == null && !_avoidHwndSourceHelper)
				{
					try
					{
						_hwndSourceHelper = new HwndSourceHelper(WndProc);
					}
					catch (SecurityException)
					{
						_avoidHwndSourceHelper = false;
					}
				}
				return _hwndSourceHelper;
			}
		}

		private IImeEditor ImeEditor { get; set; }

		private ImeEditorHelperState ImeState => ImeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState;

		private bool IsInputContextValid => IntPtr.Zero != GetInputContext();

		static ImeEditorHelper()
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.SetImeDebugOn();
		}

		internal ImeEditorHelper(IImeEditor imeEditor)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(imeEditor, "imeEditor");
			ImeEditor = imeEditor;
			SetInputMethodProperties();
			if (imeEditor.ImeMessageHookMode == ImeMessageHookMode.ImeMessageHookModeAutomaticOnKeyboardFocus && imeEditor is UIElement)
			{
				_isKeyBoardFocusedTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(ImeEditor.EditorElement, UIElement.IsKeyboardFocusedProperty, OnImeEditorElementIsKeyboardFocusedChanged);
				((UIElement)imeEditor).GotKeyboardFocus += OnImeEditorElementGotKeyboardFocus;
				((UIElement)imeEditor).LostKeyboardFocus += OnImeEditorElementLostKeyboardFocus;
				((UIElement)imeEditor).GotFocus += OnImeEditorElementGotFocus;
				((UIElement)imeEditor).LostFocus += OnImeEditorElementLostFocus;
			}
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		internal void CompleteComposition(bool compositionDiscarded = false)
		{
			IntPtr inputContext = GetInputContext();
			if (inputContext != IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmNotifyIME(inputContext, 21, 1, 0);
			}
			_compositionStartOffset = -1;
			_compositionLength = 0;
			ImeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition = false;
			ImeEditor.OnCompositionEnding(compositionDiscarded);
		}

		internal void Dispose()
		{
			if (ImeEditor.ImeMessageHookMode == ImeMessageHookMode.ImeMessageHookModeAutomaticOnKeyboardFocus && ImeEditor is UIElement)
			{
				((UIElement)ImeEditor).GotKeyboardFocus -= OnImeEditorElementGotKeyboardFocus;
				((UIElement)ImeEditor).LostKeyboardFocus -= OnImeEditorElementLostKeyboardFocus;
				((UIElement)ImeEditor).GotFocus -= OnImeEditorElementGotFocus;
				((UIElement)ImeEditor).LostFocus -= OnImeEditorElementLostFocus;
			}
			ClearCompositionInEditor();
			CompleteComposition();
			StopProcessingImeMessages();
			ClearInputContext();
			ImeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.ResetState();
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		internal void StartProcessingImeMessages()
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   In StartProcessingImeMessages", ImeEditor.DebugId);
			if (HwndSourceHelper != null && !_imeMessageProcessingActive)
			{
				SetInputMethodProperties();
				GetInputContext();
				HwndSourceHelper.AddElement(ImeEditor.EditorElement);
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       Added Message Hook", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				_imeMessageProcessingActive = true;
				if (_tsfThreadManager == null)
				{
					InitializeTsfThreadManager();
				}
				else if (!_avoidTsfThreadManager)
				{
					_tsfThreadManager.SetFocus(IntPtr.Zero);
				}
				PositionCandidateWindow();
			}
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		internal void StopProcessingImeMessages()
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   In StopProcessingImeMessages", ImeEditor.DebugId);
			if (HwndSourceHelper != null && _imeMessageProcessingActive)
			{
				CompleteComposition();
				HwndSourceHelper.RemoveElement(ImeEditor.EditorElement);
				_imeMessageProcessingActive = false;
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       Removed Message Hook", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				ClearInputContext();
			}
		}

		private void ClearCompositionInEditor()
		{
			if (_compositionStartOffset != -1 && _compositionLength > 0)
			{
				ImeEditor.DeleteRange(CompositionStartOffset, _compositionLength);
			}
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private void ClearInputContext()
		{
			if (_inputContext != IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmAssociateContext(GetHwnd(), _previousInputContext);
				ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmDestroyContext(_inputContext);
				_inputContext = IntPtr.Zero;
				_previousInputContext = IntPtr.Zero;
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       Cleared InputContext", ImeEditor.DebugId);
			}
		}

		private string GetCombinedResultAndCompositionString(char[] resultChars, char[] compositionChars, out int resultLength)
		{
			string text = new string(resultChars);
			string text2 = new string(compositionChars);
			_ = text2.Length;
			resultLength = text.Length;
			string text3 = null;
			text3 = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) ? text2 : ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2)) ? (text + text2) : text));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && text3.Length >= 1)
			{
				return text3;
			}
			return null;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private IntPtr GetHwnd()
		{
			return GetHwndSource()?.Handle ?? IntPtr.Zero;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private HwndSource GetHwndSource()
		{
			return PresentationSource.FromVisual(ImeEditor.EditorElement) as HwndSource;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private IntPtr GetInputContext()
		{
			if (_inputContext == IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				_inputContext = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmCreateContext();
				_previousInputContext = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmAssociateContext(GetHwnd(), _inputContext);
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       Creating and associating InputContext", ImeEditor.DebugId);
			}
			return _inputContext;
		}

		private bool GetIsKoreanIme()
		{
			return InputLanguageManager.Current.CurrentInputLanguage.Name == "ko-KR";
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private bool GetIsLegacyChineseIME()
		{
			int num = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetProperty(new HandleRef(this, ImeNativeWindowMethods.GetKeyboardLayout(0)), 4);
			if ((num & 0x10000) != 0)
			{
				return (num & 0x20000) != 0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void ImeEditorElement_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   OnLostFocus", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private void InitializeTsfThreadManager()
		{
			if (_avoidTsfThreadManager)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_tsfThreadManager == null)
			{
				ImeNativeWindowMethods.TF_CreateThreadMgr(out _tsfThreadManager);
				if (_tsfThreadManager == null)
				{
					_avoidTsfThreadManager = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			_tsfThreadManager.SetFocus(IntPtr.Zero);
		}

		private void OnCurrentInputLanguageChanging(object sender, InputLanguageEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("&&&&&&&  >>>>>  InputLanguage Changing from '" + e.PreviousLanguage.Name + "' to '" + e.NewLanguage.Name + "' !!!", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		private void OnCurrentInputLanguageChanged(object sender, InputLanguageEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("&&&&&&&  >>>>>  InputLanguage Changed from '" + e.PreviousLanguage.Name + "' to '" + e.NewLanguage.Name + "' !!!", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		private void OnImeEditorElementGotKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   OnGotKeyboardFocus", ImeEditor.DebugId);
			StartProcessingImeMessages();
		}

		private void OnImeEditorElementLostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   OnLostKeyboardFocus", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		private void OnImeEditorElementIsKeyboardFocusedChanged()
		{
			if (!ImeEditor.EditorElement.IsKeyboardFocused)
			{
				StopProcessingImeMessages();
			}
		}

		private void OnImeEditorElementGotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   OnGotFocus", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		private void OnImeEditorElementLostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====   OnLostFocus", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		public void PositionCandidateWindow()
		{
			if (IsInputContextValid)
			{
				ImeNativeWindowMethods.CANDIDATEFORM lpCandidate = default(ImeNativeWindowMethods.CANDIDATEFORM);
				if (GetIsLegacyChineseIME())
				{
					lpCandidate.dwIndex = 0;
					lpCandidate.dwStyle = 0;
					lpCandidate.rcArea.left = 0;
					lpCandidate.rcArea.right = 0;
					lpCandidate.rcArea.top = 0;
					lpCandidate.rcArea.bottom = 0;
					lpCandidate.ptCurrentPos = new ImeNativeWindowMethods.POINT(0, 0);
				}
				else
				{
					Rect candidateWindowExclusionRect = ImeEditor.GetCandidateWindowExclusionRect(CompositionStartOffset, CompositionStartOffset + _compositionLength);
					lpCandidate.dwIndex = 0;
					lpCandidate.dwStyle = 128;
					lpCandidate.rcArea.left = Convert.ToInt32(candidateWindowExclusionRect.Left);
					lpCandidate.rcArea.right = Convert.ToInt32(candidateWindowExclusionRect.Right);
					lpCandidate.rcArea.top = Convert.ToInt32(candidateWindowExclusionRect.Top);
					lpCandidate.rcArea.bottom = Convert.ToInt32(candidateWindowExclusionRect.Bottom);
					lpCandidate.ptCurrentPos = new ImeNativeWindowMethods.POINT(Convert.ToInt32(candidateWindowExclusionRect.Left), Convert.ToInt32(candidateWindowExclusionRect.Top));
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       Setting CandidateWindowPosition rect: " + candidateWindowExclusionRect.ToString() + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				}
				if (!ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmSetCandidateWindow(GetInputContext(), ref lpCandidate))
				{
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("XXXX       ImmSetCandidateWindow FAILED!! ", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				}
			}
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private bool PositionCompositionWindow()
		{
			ImeNativeWindowMethods.COMPOSITIONFORM lpCandidate = new ImeNativeWindowMethods.COMPOSITIONFORM
			{
				dwStyle = 1
			};
			Rect preferredCompositionWindowRect = ImeEditor.PreferredCompositionWindowRect;
			lpCandidate.rcArea = ImeNativeWindowMethods.RECT.FromRectangle(preferredCompositionWindowRect);
			lpCandidate.ptCurrentPos = new ImeNativeWindowMethods.POINT((int)preferredCompositionWindowRect.Left, (int)preferredCompositionWindowRect.Top);
			if (IsInputContextValid)
			{
				return ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmSetCompositionWindow(GetInputContext(), ref lpCandidate);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings(char[] resultChars, char[] compositionChars, int caretOffset, int deltaStart, int[] clauseOffsets, byte[] clauseCharsAttributes)
		{
			if (_imeMessageBeingProcessed)
			{
				return;
			}
			_imeMessageBeingProcessed = true;
			bool flag = false;
			try
			{
				int resultLength;
				string text = GetCombinedResultAndCompositionString(resultChars, compositionChars, out resultLength);
				if (text == null)
				{
					if (ImeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition)
					{
						ClearCompositionInEditor();
					}
					return;
				}
				ResolveCaretOffset(caretOffset, clauseCharsAttributes, text.Length);
				bool flag2 = false;
				IImeEditor imeEditor = ImeEditor;
				if (!imeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition)
				{
					imeEditor.OnCompositionStarting();
					imeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition = true;
					_compositionStartOffset = imeEditor.SelectionStart;
				}
				else if (resultChars != null)
				{
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: About to clear composition in editor (1) (start: '" + CompositionStartOffset + "', length: '" + _compositionLength + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
					ClearCompositionInEditor();
					flag = true;
					string text2 = new string(resultChars);
					imeEditor.InsertText(CompositionStartOffset, text2, advanceCaret: false, ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.None);
					imeEditor.OnCompositionEnding(compositionDiscarded: false);
					imeEditor.OnCompositionStarting();
					_compositionStartOffset += text2.Length;
					resultChars = null;
					text = new string(compositionChars);
					if (compositionChars == null)
					{
						flag2 = true;
						_compositionLength = 0;
						ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: Composition is ending (1)!!  ('" + (resultChars == null) + "," + (compositionChars == null) + ", combinedstring: " + text + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
					}
				}
				if (!flag && !flag2)
				{
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: About to clear composition in editor (2) (start: '" + CompositionStartOffset + "', length: '" + _compositionLength + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
					ClearCompositionInEditor();
				}
				imeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.CurrentCompositionClauses = null;
				if (clauseOffsets != null && clauseCharsAttributes != null)
				{
					int num = 0;
					int num2 = CompositionStartOffset;
					string text3 = new string(compositionChars);
					ImeClauseInfo[] array = new ImeClauseInfo[clauseOffsets.Length - 1];
					ImeClauseAdornmentStyle imeClauseAdornmentStyle = ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.None;
					for (int i = 0; i < clauseOffsets.Length - 1; i++)
					{
						switch (clauseCharsAttributes[clauseOffsets[i]])
						{
						case 0:
							imeClauseAdornmentStyle = ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.DottedLine;
							break;
						case 1:
							imeClauseAdornmentStyle = ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.SolidThickLine;
							break;
						case 2:
							imeClauseAdornmentStyle = ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.SolidLine;
							break;
						case 3:
							imeClauseAdornmentStyle = ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.SolidLine;
							break;
						}
						int num3 = clauseOffsets[i + 1] - clauseOffsets[i];
						string text4 = text3.Substring(num, num3);
						imeEditor.InsertText(num2, text4, advanceCaret: false, imeClauseAdornmentStyle);
						array[i] = new ImeClauseInfo(text4, imeClauseAdornmentStyle, num2, num3);
						num += num3;
						num2 += num3;
					}
					_compositionLength = text3.Length;
					int start = CompositionStartOffset + _caretOffsetResolved;
					imeEditor.SetSelection(start, 0);
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: Set caret offset.  CompStart: '" + CompositionStartOffset + "', caretOffsetResolved: '" + _caretOffsetResolved + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
					imeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.CurrentCompositionClauses = array;
				}
				else if (text.Length > 0)
				{
					imeEditor.InsertText(CompositionStartOffset, text, advanceCaret: false, ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.None);
					_compositionLength = text.Length;
					int num4 = CompositionStartOffset + _caretOffsetResolved;
					imeEditor.SetSelection(num4, 0);
					if (text.Trim().Length == 0 && resultChars != null && compositionChars == null)
					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: Set caret offset to '" + num4 + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				}
				else if (imeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: Composition is ending (2)!!  ('" + (resultChars == null) + "," + (compositionChars == null) + ", combinedstring: " + text + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				}
				else
				{
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings: NO COMPOSITION STRINGS OR CLAUSES!", ImeEditor.DebugId);
				}
				if (GetIsKoreanIme())
				{
					if (flag2 && _endCompositionMessageReceived)
					{
						CompleteComposition();
					}
				}
				else if (flag2)
				{
					CompleteComposition();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_imeMessageBeingProcessed = false;
				_endCompositionMessageReceived = false;
			}
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private bool ProcessWmImeChar(IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam)
		{
			if (_imeMessageBeingProcessed)
			{
				return false;
			}
			_imeMessageBeingProcessed = true;
			try
			{
				if (GetCombinedResultAndCompositionString(null, new char[1] { (char)(int)wParam }, out var _) != null)
				{
					_caretOffsetResolved = 1;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_imeMessageBeingProcessed = false;
			}
			if (GetIsLegacyChineseIME())
			{
				PositionCompositionWindow();
			}
			return true;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private bool ProcessWmImeComposition(IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam)
		{
			IntPtr inputContext = GetInputContext();
			int caretOffset = 0;
			int deltaStart = 0;
			char[] array = null;
			char[] array2 = null;
			int[] array3 = null;
			byte[] array4 = null;
			int num = (int)lParam.ToInt64();
			PositionCandidateWindow();
			if (GetIsLegacyChineseIME())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!IsInputContextValid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((num & 0x80) != 0)
			{
				caretOffset = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 128, IntPtr.Zero, 0);
			}
			if ((num & 0x800) != 0)
			{
				int num2 = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 2048, IntPtr.Zero, 0);
				if (num2 > 0)
				{
					array = new char[num2 / Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(short))];
					ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 2048, array, num2);
				}
			}
			if ((num & 8) != 0)
			{
				int num2 = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 8, IntPtr.Zero, 0);
				if (num2 > 0)
				{
					array2 = new char[num2 / Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(short))];
					ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 8, array2, num2);
					if ((num & 0x100) != 0)
					{
						deltaStart = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 256, IntPtr.Zero, 0);
					}
					if ((num & 0x20) != 0)
					{
						num2 = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 32, IntPtr.Zero, 0);
						if (num2 > 0)
						{
							array3 = new int[num2 / Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(int))];
							ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 32, array3, num2);
						}
					}
					if ((num & 0x10) != 0)
					{
						num2 = ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 16, IntPtr.Zero, 0);
						if (num2 > 0)
						{
							array4 = new byte[num2 / Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(byte))];
							ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmGetCompositionString(inputContext, 16, array4, num2);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (array3 != null)
			{
				int[] array5 = array3;
				foreach (int value in array5)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			if (array4 != null)
			{
				byte[] array6 = array4;
				foreach (byte value2 in array6)
				{
					stringBuilder2.Append(value2);
					stringBuilder2.Append(",");
				}
			}
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ProcessWM_IME_COMPOSITION: about to call ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings result: '" + new string(array) + "', composition: '" + new string(array2) + "', clauseInfo: '" + stringBuilder.ToString() + "', attribs: '" + stringBuilder2.ToString() + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
			ProcessCompositionAndResultStrings(array, array2, caretOffset, deltaStart, array3, array4);
			return true;
		}

		private void SetInputMethodProperties()
		{
			InputMethod.SetIsInputMethodSuspended(ImeEditor.EditorElement, value: true);
		}

		private void ResolveCaretOffset(int caretOffset, byte[] clauseCharsAttributes, int compositionLength)
		{
			if (clauseCharsAttributes == null)
			{
				_caretOffsetResolved = compositionLength;
			}
			else
			{
				byte b = byte.MaxValue;
				if (caretOffset >= 0 && caretOffset < clauseCharsAttributes.Length)
				{
					b = clauseCharsAttributes[caretOffset];
				}
				byte b2 = byte.MaxValue;
				if (caretOffset > 0 && caretOffset - 1 < clauseCharsAttributes.Length)
				{
					b2 = clauseCharsAttributes[caretOffset - 1];
				}
				if (b == 0 || b == 2 || b == 1 || b == 3 || b2 == 0 || b2 == 2 || b2 == 1 || b2 == 3)
				{
					_caretOffsetResolved = caretOffset;
				}
				else
				{
					_caretOffsetResolved = compositionLength;
				}
			}
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("====       ResolveCaretOffset: '" + _caretOffsetResolved + "'", ImeEditor.DebugId);
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private IntPtr WndProc(IntPtr hwnd, int message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam, ref bool handled)
		{
			handled = false;
			string winMessageAsString = ImeDebugHelpers.GetWinMessageAsString(hwnd, message, wParam, lParam);
			switch (message)
			{
			case 81:
			case 643:
			case 644:
			case 645:
			case 656:
			case 657:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				break;
			case 646:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				handled = ProcessWmImeChar(wParam, lParam);
				break;
			case 271:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				handled = ProcessWmImeComposition(wParam, lParam);
				break;
			case 269:
			case 270:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				if (message == 269)
				{
					_compositionStartOffset = ImeEditor.SelectionStart;
				}
				else
				{
					_endCompositionMessageReceived = true;
					if (!GetIsKoreanIme())
					{
						CompleteComposition();
					}
				}
				PositionCandidateWindow();
				if (!GetIsLegacyChineseIME())
				{
					handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case 642:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				if (wParam.ToInt64() == 5)
				{
					PositionCandidateWindow();
				}
				break;
			case 641:
			{
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				uint num = (uint)lParam.ToInt64();
				num &= 0x7FFFFFFF;
				lParam = new IntPtr((int)num);
				ImeNativeWindowMethods.ImmIsUIMessage(IntPtr.Zero, message, wParam, lParam);
				handled = true;
				break;
			}
			case 8:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				break;
			case 513:
				CompleteComposition();
				break;
			case 256:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				PositionCandidateWindow();
				break;
			case 257:
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(winMessageAsString, ImeEditor.DebugId);
				break;
			}
			return IntPtr.Zero;
		}
	}
	internal class ImeEditorHelperState : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private bool _isInComposition;

		private ImeClauseInfo[] _currentImeClauses;

		internal ImeClauseInfo[] CurrentCompositionClauses
		{
			get
			{
				return _currentImeClauses;
			}
			set
			{
				_currentImeClauses = value;
				ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("^^^^   \u3000\u3000\u3000\u3000ImeState.CurrentCompositionClauses set to '" + ImeClauseInfo.ArrayToString(value) + "'");
				RaisePropertyChanged("CurrentCompositionClauses");
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInComposition
		{
			get
			{
				return _isInComposition;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isInComposition != value)
				{
					_isInComposition = value;
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("^^^^   \u3000\u3000\u3000\u3000ImeState.IsInComposition set to '" + value + "'");
					if (!value)
					{
						CurrentCompositionClauses = null;
					}
					RaisePropertyChanged("IsInComposition");
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		internal void ResetState()
		{
			_isInComposition = false;
			_currentImeClauses = null;
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}
	}

		internal struct MSG
		{
			public IntPtr hwnd;

			public int message;

			public IntPtr wParam;

			public IntPtr lParam;

			public int time;

			public POINT pt;
		}

		internal struct POINT
		{
			internal int X;

			internal int Y;

			internal Point Point => new Point(X, Y);

			internal POINT(int x, int y)
			{
				X = x;
				Y = y;
			}

			internal POINT(Point point)
			{
				X = (int)point.X;
				Y = (int)point.Y;
			}

			internal static POINT FromPoint(Point point)
			{
				return new POINT((int)point.X, (int)point.Y);
			}
		}

		internal struct RECONVERTSTRING
		{
			public int dwSize;

			public int dwVersion;

			public int dwStrLen;

			public int dwStrOffset;

			public int dwCompStrLen;

			public int dwCompStrOffset;

			public int dwTargetStrLen;

			public int dwTargetStrOffset;
		}

		internal struct RECT
		{
			public int left;

			public int top;

			public int right;

			public int bottom;

			public Rect Rect => new Rect(left, top, right - left, bottom - top);

			public RECT(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
			{
				this.left = left;
				this.top = top;
				this.right = right;
				this.bottom = bottom;
			}

			public RECT(Rect rect)
			{
				left = (int)rect.Left;
				top = (int)rect.Top;
				right = (int)rect.Right;
				bottom = (int)rect.Bottom;
			}

			public static RECT FromXYWH(int x, int y, int width, int height)
			{
				return new RECT(x, y, x + width, y + height);
			}

			public static RECT FromRectangle(Rect rect)
			{
				return new RECT((int)rect.Left, (int)rect.Top, (int)rect.Right, (int)rect.Bottom);
			}
		}

		{
			string text = hwnd.ToInt64().ToString();
			string text2 = "??";
			string text3 = "n/a";

			switch (message)
			{
			case 642:
			{
				text2 = "WM_IME_NOTIFY";
				long num = wParam.ToInt64();
				long num4 = num - 1;
				if ((ulong)num4 > 12uL)
				{
					goto IL_01b0;
				}
				switch (num4)
				{
				case 2L:
					break;
				case 3L:

		internal static void SetImeDebugOn()
		{
			s_imeDebugOn = true;
		}

		internal static void SetImeDebugOff()
		{
			s_imeDebugOn = false;
		}

		internal static void WriteImeDebugMessage(string message, string debugId = null)
		{
			if (s_imeDebugOn)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(s_DebugMessagePrefix);
				if (s_includeTickCountInDebugMessage)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("  [");
					stringBuilder.Append(DateTime.Now.ToString("hh:mm:ss:fffff"));
					stringBuilder.Append("]");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append("  ");
				stringBuilder.Append(message);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(debugId))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(" [DebugId： '");
					stringBuilder.Append(debugId);
					stringBuilder.Append("']");
				}
			}
		}
	}

	internal enum ImeClauseAdornmentStyle
	{
		None,
		SolidLine,
		SolidThickLine,
		DottedLine,
		WavyLine
	}
	internal class ImeClauseInfo
	{
		private string _clauseText;

		private ImeClauseAdornmentStyle _adornmentStyle;

		private int _offset;

		private int _length;

		public ImeClauseAdornmentStyle AdornmentStyle => _adornmentStyle;

		public string ClauseText => _clauseText;

		public int Length => _length;

		public int Offset => _offset;

		public ImeClauseInfo(string clauseText, ImeClauseAdornmentStyle textStyle, int offset, int length)
		{
			_clauseText = clauseText;
			_adornmentStyle = textStyle;
			_offset = offset;
			_length = length;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("Text: '");
			stringBuilder.Append(ClauseText);
			stringBuilder.Append("', Style: '");
			stringBuilder.Append(Enum.GetName(typeof(ImeClauseAdornmentStyle), AdornmentStyle));
			stringBuilder.Append("'");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		internal static string ArrayToString(ImeClauseInfo[] clauseInfos)
		{
			if (clauseInfos == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (int i = 0; i < clauseInfos.Length; i++)
			{
				ImeClauseInfo imeClauseInfo = clauseInfos[i];
				stringBuilder.Append("[");
				stringBuilder.Append(i);
				stringBuilder.Append("] '");
				stringBuilder.Append(imeClauseInfo.ToString());
				stringBuilder.Append("'");
				if (i < clauseInfos.Length - 1)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(", ");
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}
