		internal RichDocumentView(XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor, RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle layoutStyle = RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle.WebLayout)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(richTextEditor, "richTextEditor");
			RichTextEditor = richTextEditor;
			_layoutStyle = layoutStyle;
			_editorHorizontalScrollBarVisibilityTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(RichTextEditor, DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(XamRichTextEditor.HorizontalScrollBarVisibilityProperty), UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars);
			_editorVerticalScrollBarVisibilityTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(RichTextEditor, DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(XamRichTextEditor.VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty), UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars);
		}

		public void Activate()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView != this)
			{
				RichTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView = this;
			}
		}

		internal void BringDocumentOffsetIntoView(int documentOffset)
		{
			if (GetIsDocumentOffsetInView(documentOffset))
			{
				EnsureLineContainingOffsetIsFullyInView(documentOffset);
			}
			else if (GetIsDocumentOffsetBeforeView(documentOffset))
			{
				Point? point = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
				if (point.HasValue)
				{
					VerticalViewOffset = point.Value.Y;
				}
			}
			else if (GetIsDocumentOffsetAfterView(documentOffset))
			{
				Point? point2 = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
				if (point2.HasValue)
				{
					double num = 16.0;
					VerticalViewOffset = point2.Value.Y - Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight + num;
				}
			}
		}

		internal CaretLayoutInfo? CaretLayoutInfoFromTextAreaPoint(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			int? num = DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				return GetCaretLayoutInfo(num.Value);
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal void ClearCaretLayoutInfoCache()
		{
			CaretLayoutInfoCache.Clear();
		}

		internal void ClearPresenter()
		{
			_presenter = null;
		}

		internal bool CloseContextMenuIfOpen()
		{
			if (_contextMenu != null && _contextMenu.IsOpen)
			{
				_contextMenu.IsOpen = false;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void CreateAdornmentGeneratorsIfNecessary()
		{
			if (AdornmentGenerators.Count < 1)
			{
				AddAdornmentGenerators();
			}
		}

		private XamMenuItem CreateContextMenuItemWithCommand(string header, RichTextEditorCommandType commandType, bool isEnabled = true)
		{
			XamMenuItem obj = new XamMenuItem
			{
				Header = header,
				IsEnabled = isEnabled
			};
			obj.SetValue(value: new RichTextEditorCommandSource
			{
				EventName = "Click",
				CommandType = commandType,
				Parameter = RichTextEditor
			}, dp: Commanding.CommandProperty);
			return obj;
		}

		private XamMenuItem CreateContextMenuItemWithEvent(string header, EventHandler clickHandler, Tuple<int, int> rowsCols)
		{
			XamMenuItem xamMenuItem = new XamMenuItem();
			xamMenuItem.Header = header;
			xamMenuItem.Click += clickHandler;
			xamMenuItem.Tag = rowsCols;
			return xamMenuItem;
		}

		internal void DiscardLayoutProviderForContentElementHost(IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			if (LayoutProviders.ContainsKey(contentElementHost))
			{
				LayoutProviders[contentElementHost].Dispose();
				LayoutProviders.Remove(contentElementHost);
			}
		}

		internal int? DocumentOffsetFromLayoutPoint(Point layoutPoint, bool includeListNumbers = false, bool includeParagraphMarks = false)
		{
			return LayoutManager.GetDocumentOffsetFromLayoutPoint(RichTextEditorUtilities.AdjustPointForYCusp(layoutPoint), includeListNumbers, includeParagraphMarks);
		}

		internal int? DocumentOffsetFromPointInDocumentText(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			if (Presenter != null)
			{
				RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = Presenter.TextArea;
				if (textArea == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (!textArea.ContainsPoint(pointInTextAreaCoordinates))
				{
					return null;
				}
				int? result = DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
				if (!result.HasValue)
				{
					return null;
				}
				foreach (LineElementProxy item in LinesFromTextAreaOffsetY(pointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y))
				{
					if (item.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates.Contains(pointInTextAreaCoordinates))
					{
						return result;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal int? DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates, bool includeListNumbers = false, bool includeParagraphMarks = false)
		{
			return DocumentOffsetFromLayoutPoint(LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(pointInTextAreaCoordinates), includeListNumbers, includeParagraphMarks);
		}

		internal void EnsureCaretAtOffsetIsFullyInView(int documentOffset)
		{
			int aggregateOffsetCount = Document.RootNode.AggregateOffsetCount;
			if (documentOffset >= aggregateOffsetCount)
			{
				documentOffset = Math.Max(0, aggregateOffsetCount - 1);
			}
			double? num = null;
			LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, out var layoutBlock, out var line);
			if (line != null)
			{
				double num2 = layoutBlock.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels + line.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels;
				num = num2 + line.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels;
			}
			CaretLayoutInfo? caretLayoutInfo = GetCaretLayoutInfo(documentOffset);
			if (!caretLayoutInfo.HasValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			Point overallTopLeftCaretPoint = caretLayoutInfo.Value.OverallTopLeftCaretPoint;
			double actualHeight = Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight;
			double caretHeight = caretLayoutInfo.Value.CaretHeight;
			double num3 = overallTopLeftCaretPoint.Y + caretHeight;
			double num4 = (num.HasValue ? num.Value : num3) - (VerticalViewOffset + actualHeight);
			double num5 = num3 - caretLayoutInfo.Value.ContentHeight;
			if (caretHeight > actualHeight)
			{
				VerticalViewOffset = num3 - caretHeight;
				return;
			}
			if (num4 > 0.0)
			{
				VerticalViewOffset += num4;
				return;
			}
			double num6 = VerticalViewOffset - num5 + Extent.LayoutPaddingY.LogicalPixels;
			if (num6 > 0.0)
			{
				VerticalViewOffset -= num6;
			}
		}

		internal bool EnsureLineContainingOffsetIsFullyInView(int documentOffset)
		{
			int aggregateOffsetCount = Document.RootNode.AggregateOffsetCount;
			if (documentOffset >= aggregateOffsetCount)
			{
				documentOffset = Math.Max(0, aggregateOffsetCount - 1);
			}
			LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = LineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (lineElementProxy == null)
			{
				LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, out var layoutBlock, out var line);
				if (line != null)
				{
					double num = layoutBlock.ContentOverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels + line.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels;
					if (documentOffset <= FirstVisibleDocumentOffset)
					{
						VerticalViewOffset = num;
					}
					else
					{
						double val = num - Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight + line.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels;
						VerticalViewOffset = Math.Max(0.0, val);
					}
					return true;
				}
			}
			return EnsureLineIsFullyInView(lineElementProxy);
		}

		internal bool EnsureLineIsFullyInView(LineElementProxy lineElementProxy)
		{
			if (lineElementProxy == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(0.0, lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY) || lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY <= 0.0)
			{
				VerticalViewOffset += lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY;
				return true;
			}
			double actualHeight = Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight;
			if (lineElementProxy.OverallHeight > actualHeight)
			{
				VerticalViewOffset = lineElementProxy.LayoutOffsetY;
			}
			else
			{
				double num = lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY + lineElementProxy.OverallHeight - Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight;
				if (num > 0.0 && num < actualHeight)
				{
					VerticalViewOffset += num;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void EnsureXOffsetIsInView(double xOffset, bool includePadding, bool includeViewportWidthPercentageInPadding = false)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (includePadding)
			{
				num = 8.0;
			}
			double actualWidth = Presenter.TextArea.ActualWidth;
			if (includeViewportWidthPercentageInPadding)
			{
				LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = LineFromDocumentOffset(Caret.DocumentOffset);
				num = ((lineElementProxy == null) ? (num + actualWidth / 3.0) : (num + Math.Min(lineElementProxy.OverallWidth - HorizontalViewOffset, actualWidth / 3.0)));
			}
			num = Math.Max(0.0, num);
			if (xOffset < HorizontalViewOffset)
			{
				HorizontalViewOffset = Math.Max(0.0, xOffset - num * 2.0);
			}
			else if (xOffset > Presenter.TextArea.ActualWidth + HorizontalViewOffset && xOffset - HorizontalViewOffset >= actualWidth)
			{
				HorizontalViewOffset = xOffset - actualWidth + num;
			}
		}

		internal void ForceImeCompositionEnd()
		{
			if (Presenter != null)
			{
				Presenter.ForceImeCompositionEnd();
			}
		}

		internal void ForceLayoutProvidersToRebuildLineContentOnNextLayoutPass()
		{
			foreach (LayoutProvider value in LayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				value.ForceLineContentRebuildOnNextLayoutPass();
			}
		}

		internal List<LineElementProxy> GetAllVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes()
		{
			List<LineElementProxy> list = new List<LineElementProxy>(50);
			foreach (LayoutProvider value in LayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				value.AddVisibleLinesToList(list);
			}
			return list;
		}

		internal bool GetAreDocumentOffsetsInSameParagraph(int documentOffset1, int documentOffset2, bool includeParagraphMark)
		{
			if (documentOffset1 == documentOffset2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset1, out var layoutBlock, out var line) && LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset2, out var layoutBlock2, out line))
			{
				if (layoutBlock == layoutBlock2)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (!includeParagraphMark)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (layoutBlock is ParagraphLayoutNode)
				{
					int documentOffset3 = ((ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock).DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset();
					int aggregateOffsetCount = ((ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock).DocumentNode.AggregateOffsetCount;
					if (documentOffset2 == documentOffset3 + aggregateOffsetCount)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetAreDocumentOffsetsOnSameLine(int documentOffset1, int documentOffset2)
		{
			if (documentOffset1 == documentOffset2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset1, out var layoutBlock, out var line) && LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset2, out layoutBlock, out var line2))
			{
				return line == line2;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal CaretLayoutInfo? GetCaretLayoutInfo(int documentOffset, CharacterSettings settingsOverride = null, LayoutManagerBase.CaretContext caretContext = LayoutManagerBase.CaretContext.NoSelection)
		{
			if (settingsOverride == null && Selection.PendingCharacterSettings != null)
			{
				settingsOverride = Selection.PendingCharacterSettings;
			}
			LayoutManagerProcessStatus status = LayoutManager.Status;
			if (status == LayoutManagerProcessStatus.BuildingStructureNotStarted || status == LayoutManagerProcessStatus.BuildingStructure)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (Document == null || Document.AggregateOffsetCount < 1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (settingsOverride != null)
			{
				return LayoutManager.GetCaretLayoutFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, settingsOverride, caretContext);
			}
			CaretLayoutInfoCacheKey key = new CaretLayoutInfoCacheKey(documentOffset, caretContext);
			if (CaretLayoutInfoCache.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = LayoutManager.GetCaretLayoutFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, null, caretContext);
			if (value.HasValue)
			{
				try
				{
					CaretLayoutInfoCache.Add(new CaretLayoutInfoCacheKey(documentOffset, caretContext), value);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			else
			{
				RichTextEditorUtilities.LogDebuggerError(1, "DocumentView", "CaretLayoutInfo not available!");
			}
			return value;
		}

		internal bool GetDoesParagraphAtOffsetHaveContent(int documentOffset)
		{
			ParagraphLayoutNode paragraphAtDocumentOffset = LayoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (paragraphAtDocumentOffset != null)
			{
				return ((ParagraphNode)paragraphAtDocumentOffset.DocumentNode).HasChildNodes;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetDoesParagraphAtOffsetHaveCustomTabStops(int documentOffset)
		{
			return LayoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(documentOffset)?.SettingsResolved.HasTabStops ?? false;
		}

		internal bool GetDoesTextAreaContainMouseClickPoint(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Presenter != null && Presenter.TextArea != null)
			{
				return GetDoesTextAreaContainPoint(e.GetPosition(Presenter.TextArea));
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetDoesTextAreaContainPoint(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			if (Presenter != null)
			{
				RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = Presenter.TextArea;
				if (textArea != null)
				{
					return textArea.ContainsPoint(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAfterParagraphMark(int documentOffset)
		{
			if (LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, out var layoutBlock, out var _) && layoutBlock is ParagraphLayoutNode)
			{
				int documentOffset2 = ((ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock).DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset();
				if (documentOffset == documentOffset2)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAfterView(int documentOffset)
		{
			return documentOffset > LastVisibleDocumentOffset;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOrEndOfLine(bool beginning, int documentOffset, bool backupBeforeTabsIfBeginning = true)
		{
			documentOffset = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(documentOffset, Document);
			if (beginning && documentOffset < 1)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!beginning && documentOffset >= Document.RootNode.AggregateOffsetCount)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!beginning)
			{
				int num = RichTextEditorUtilities.AdjustDocumentOffsetForParagraphMark(documentOffset, Document);
				documentOffset = ((documentOffset == num) ? RichTextEditorUtilities.AdjustDocumentOffsetForNewLine(documentOffset, this) : num);
			}
			if (beginning && backupBeforeTabsIfBeginning)
			{
				RichTextDocument document = Document;
				while (documentOffset > 0 && document.GetTextInSpan(new DocumentSpan(documentOffset - 1, 1)) == "\t")
				{
					documentOffset--;
				}
			}
			if (LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, out var layoutBlock, out var line))
			{
				int num2 = ((!beginning) ? 1 : (-1));
				if (beginning && layoutBlock is LayoutNodeBase)
				{
					num2 -= ((LayoutNodeBase)layoutBlock).DocumentNode.PrefixOffsetCount;
				}
				if (LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset + num2, out layoutBlock, out var line2))
				{
					return line != line2;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfLineInList(int documentOffset)
		{
			if (GetIsDocumentOffsetInList(documentOffset))
			{
				return GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOrEndOfLine(beginning: true, documentOffset);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfList(int documentOffset)
		{
			IEnumerable<ParagraphNode> paragraphsInListAtOffset = Document.GetParagraphsInListAtOffset(documentOffset);
			if (paragraphsInListAtOffset != null)
			{
				ParagraphNode paragraphNode = paragraphsInListAtOffset.First();
				if (documentOffset != paragraphNode.GetDocumentOffset())
				{
					return documentOffset == paragraphNode.GetDocumentOffset() + paragraphNode.PrefixOffsetCount;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfParagraph(int documentOffset, bool includeListNumbers = false)
		{
			if (documentOffset < 1)
			{
				return true;
			}
			documentOffset = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(documentOffset, Document);
			ParagraphLayoutNode paragraphAtDocumentOffset = LayoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (paragraphAtDocumentOffset == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(paragraphAtDocumentOffset.DocumentNode is ParagraphNode paragraphNode))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (includeListNumbers)
			{
				return paragraphNode.GetDocumentOffset() == documentOffset;
			}
			return paragraphNode.GetDocumentOffset() + paragraphNode.PrefixOffsetCount == documentOffset;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetAtListIndicator(int documentOffset)
		{
			if (GetIsDocumentOffsetInList(documentOffset))
			{
				return GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfParagraph(documentOffset, includeListNumbers: true);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetBeforeView(int documentOffset)
		{
			return documentOffset < FirstVisibleDocumentOffset;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetInFirstParagraphOfList(int documentOffset)
		{
			return Document.GetParagraphsInListAtOffset(documentOffset)?.First().GetDocumentSpan(includePrefix: true, includePostfix: true).Contains(documentOffset) ?? false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetInList(int documentOffset)
		{
			documentOffset = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(documentOffset, Document);
			ParagraphLayoutNode paragraphAtDocumentOffset = LayoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (paragraphAtDocumentOffset != null && paragraphAtDocumentOffset.DocumentNode is ParagraphNode paragraphNode)
			{
				return paragraphNode.PrefixOffsetCount > 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsDocumentOffsetInView(int documentOffset, bool fullyInView = false)
		{
			bool flag = !GetIsDocumentOffsetBeforeView(documentOffset) && !GetIsDocumentOffsetAfterView(documentOffset);
			if (!fullyInView)
			{
				return flag;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return false;
			}
			LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = LineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (lineElementProxy == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			double actualHeight = Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight;
			if (lineElementProxy.OverallHeight > actualHeight)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY + lineElementProxy.OverallHeight <= actualHeight)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetIsFirstVisibleLineTheFirstDocumentLine(out LineElementProxy firstVisibleLine)
		{
			firstVisibleLine = null;
			List<LineElementProxy> allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes = GetAllVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes();
			if (allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes.Count < 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes[0];
			bool doesLineContainDocumentOffset = lineElementProxy.GetDoesLineContainDocumentOffset(0);
			if (doesLineContainDocumentOffset)
			{
				firstVisibleLine = lineElementProxy;
			}
			return doesLineContainDocumentOffset;
		}

		internal bool GetIsLastVisibleLineTheLastDocumentLine(out LineElementProxy lastVisibleLine)
		{
			lastVisibleLine = null;
			List<LineElementProxy> allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes = GetAllVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes();
			if (allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes.Count < 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes[allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes.Count - 1];
			bool doesLineContainDocumentOffset = lineElementProxy.GetDoesLineContainDocumentOffset(Document.AggregateOffsetCount - 1);
			if (doesLineContainDocumentOffset)
			{
				lastVisibleLine = lineElementProxy;
			}
			return doesLineContainDocumentOffset;
		}

		internal bool GetIsLineFullyInView(LineElementProxy lineElementProxy)
		{
			if (lineElementProxy == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY < 0.0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			double num = lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY + lineElementProxy.OverallHeight - Presenter.TextArea.ActualHeight;
			if (!RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(0.0, num))
			{
				return num <= 0.0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal LayoutProvider GetLayoutProviderForContentElementHost(IContentElementHost contentElementHost, LayoutPlane layoutPlane)
		{
			if (LayoutProviders.ContainsKey(contentElementHost))
			{
				return LayoutProviders[contentElementHost];
			}
			LayoutProvider layoutProvider = new LayoutProvider(this, contentElementHost, layoutPlane);
			LayoutProviders.Add(contentElementHost, layoutProvider);
			return layoutProvider;
		}

		internal Rect GetOverallLayoutRectOfTextLayerViewport()
		{
			foreach (LayoutProvider value in LayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				if (value.ContentElementHost.LayoutPlane == LayoutPlane.Middle)
				{
					return new Rect(new Point(0.0, VerticalViewOffset), value.LastMeasureSize);
				}
			}
			return Rect.Empty;
		}

		internal LineElementProxy[] GetVisibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray()
		{
			return new List<LineElementProxy>(VisibleLinesInMiddleLayer).ToArray();
		}

		internal void InitializeFrom(RichDocumentView view)
		{
			VerticalViewOffset = view.VerticalViewOffset;
		}

		internal bool LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(int documentOffset, out ILayoutBlock layoutBlock, out IRichTextLine line)
		{
			layoutBlock = LayoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (layoutBlock != null)
			{
				line = layoutBlock.GetLineAtDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
				if (line != null)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			line = null;
			return false;
		}

		internal Point LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return new Point(pointInTextAreaCoordinates.X, pointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y + VerticalViewOffset);
		}

		internal Point? LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(int documentOffset, bool isDocumentOffsetAtSelectionEnd = false)
		{
			LayoutManagerBase.CaretContext caretContext = (isDocumentOffsetAtSelectionEnd ? LayoutManagerBase.CaretContext.EndOfSelection : LayoutManagerBase.CaretContext.NoSelection);
			CaretLayoutInfo? caretLayoutInfo = GetCaretLayoutInfo(documentOffset, null, caretContext);
			if (caretLayoutInfo.HasValue)
			{
				return caretLayoutInfo.Value.OverallTopLeftCaretPoint;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal LineElementProxy LineFromDocumentOffset(int documentOffset)
		{
			foreach (LineElementProxy allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlane in GetAllVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlanes())
			{
				if (allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlane.GetDoesLineContainDocumentOffset(documentOffset))
				{
					return allVisibleLinesInAllLayoutPlane;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal LineElementProxy LineFromTextAreaOffsetY(double textAreaOffsetY)
		{
			if (VisibleLinesInMiddleLayerCount < 1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			foreach (LineElementProxy item in VisibleLinesInMiddleLayer)
			{
				if (item.GetDoesLineContainTextAreaOffsetY(textAreaOffsetY))
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal IEnumerable<LineElementProxy> LinesFromTextAreaOffsetY(double textAreaOffsetY)
		{
			if (VisibleLinesInMiddleLayerCount < 1)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (LineElementProxy item in VisibleLinesInMiddleLayer)
			{
				if (item.GetDoesLineContainTextAreaOffsetY(textAreaOffsetY))
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void MakeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout()
		{
			_makeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout = true;
		}

		internal void OnDocumentContentChanged(object sender, DocumentContentChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ClearCaretLayoutInfoCache();
			if (Document.CurrentVersion.IsCompatibleWith(_lastDocumentVersion))
			{
				Selection.EnsureAllRangesAreCurrent(raisePropertyChangeNotification: false);
				Selection.OnDocumentContentChanged();
				Caret.TranslateDocumentOffsetToCurrentVersion(OffsetTrackingMode.Positive);
				RefreshLayersLayout();
				SelectionManager.RaiseSelectionChangedEvents();
			}
			else
			{
				ProcessDocumentContentsReplacement();
			}
			RichTextEditor.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars();
			});
		}

		internal void OnDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange()
		{
			if (Presenter != null)
			{
				Presenter.OnDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange();
			}
		}

		internal void OnImeStateChanged()
		{
			if (Presenter != null)
			{
				Presenter.OnImeStateChanged();
			}
		}

		internal void OnIsMultiSelectEnabledChanged(bool newValue)
		{
			if (!newValue)
			{
				Selection.ClearMultipleSelections();
			}
		}

		internal void OnTextAreaInitialized()
		{
			foreach (RichTextAdornmentGeneratorBase value in AdornmentGenerators.Values)
			{
				value.OnTextAreaInitialized();
			}
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.Source = RichTextEditor;
			binding.Path = new PropertyPath("DocumentBackground");
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			Presenter.TextArea.SetBinding(Panel.BackgroundProperty, binding);
		}

		internal void OnVerticalViewOffsetChangedByUser()
		{
			_updateCachedPositionInfoAfterNextLayout = true;
		}

		internal RichTextHitTestResult[] PerformHitTestOnDocumentOffset(int documentOffset, bool includeAllOverlappingHits = false)
		{
			Point? point = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (point.HasValue)
			{
				return LayoutManager.HitTest(point.Value, includeAllOverlappingHits);
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal RichTextHitTestResult[] PerformHitTestOnLayoutPoint(Point layoutPoint, bool includeAllOverlappingHits = false)
		{
			return LayoutManager.HitTest(layoutPoint, includeAllOverlappingHits);
		}

		internal RichTextHitTestResult[] PerformHitTestOnTextAreaPoint(Point textAreaPoint, bool includeAllOverlappingHits = false)
		{
			return LayoutManager.HitTest(LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(textAreaPoint), includeAllOverlappingHits);
		}

		internal void ProcessTemplateChange()
		{
			UnloadAdornmentGenerators();
			DisposeLayoutProviders();
			_cursorHelper = null;
		}

		internal void RecreateAdornmentGenerators()
		{
			if (AdornmentGenerators.Count == 0)
			{
				UnloadAdornmentGenerators();
			}
			AddAdornmentGenerators();
			RefreshLayersLayout();
		}

		internal void ShowContextMenu()
		{
			ShowContextMenu(Caret.PixelLocation);
		}

		internal void ShowContextMenu(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ShowContextMenu(e.GetPosition(RichTextEditor.RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel));
		}

		internal void ShowContextMenu(Point point)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			UIElement richDocumentViewLayoutPanel = richTextEditor.RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel;
			_contextMenu = new XamContextMenu();
			_contextMenu.Placement = Infragistics.Controls.Menus.PlacementMode.Manual;
			_contextMenu.PlacementTarget = richDocumentViewLayoutPanel;
			_contextMenu.VerticalOffset = point.Y;
			_contextMenu.HorizontalOffset = point.X;
			_contextMenu.FontFamily = RichTextEditor.FontFamily;
			ContextMenuManager contextMenuManager = Infragistics.Controls.Menus.ContextMenuService.GetManager(richDocumentViewLayoutPanel);
			if (contextMenuManager == null)
			{
				contextMenuManager = new ContextMenuManager();
			}
			contextMenuManager.OpenMode = OpenMode.None;
			Infragistics.Controls.Menus.ContextMenuService.SetManager(richDocumentViewLayoutPanel, contextMenuManager);
			contextMenuManager.ContextMenu = _contextMenu;
			_contextMenu.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithCommand(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_Cut"), RichTextEditorCommandType.Cut, !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved));
			_contextMenu.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithCommand(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_Copy"), RichTextEditorCommandType.Copy));
			_contextMenu.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithCommand(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_Paste"), RichTextEditorCommandType.Paste, !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved));
			XamMenuSeparator newItem = new XamMenuSeparator();
			_contextMenu.Items.Add(newItem);
			XamMenuItem insertTableMenuItem = new XamMenuItem();
			insertTableMenuItem.Header = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_InsertTable");
			insertTableMenuItem.IsEnabled = !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved;
			_contextMenu.Items.Add(insertTableMenuItem);
			EventHandler insertTableSubMenuItemClicked = null;
			insertTableSubMenuItemClicked = delegate(object sender, EventArgs args)
			{
				Tuple<int, int> tuple = ((XamMenuItem)sender).Tag as Tuple<int, int>;
				TableExtentBehavior extentBehavior = TableExtentBehavior.FixedWidthColumns;
				Extent? fixedColumnWidth = null;
				richTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
				string error = null;
				TableNode tableNode = RichTextEditor.Document.InsertTable(RichTextEditor.Selection.Start, tuple.Item2, tuple.Item1, out error, extentBehavior, null, fixedColumnWidth);
				int num = tableNode.GetDocumentOffset();
				ParagraphNode nodeAtDocumentOffset = NodeUtils.GetNodeAtDocumentOffset<ParagraphNode>(tableNode.Document, num);
				if (nodeAtDocumentOffset != null)
				{
					num += nodeAtDocumentOffset.PrefixOffsetCount;
				}
				Caret.SetDocumentOffset(num, syncSelection: true);
				foreach (XamMenuItem item in (IEnumerable)insertTableMenuItem.Items)
				{
					item.Click -= insertTableSubMenuItemClicked;
				}
			};
			insertTableMenuItem.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithEvent(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_InsertTable_1x1"), insertTableSubMenuItemClicked, new Tuple<int, int>(1, 1)));
			insertTableMenuItem.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithEvent(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_InsertTable_2x2"), insertTableSubMenuItemClicked, new Tuple<int, int>(2, 2)));
			insertTableMenuItem.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithEvent(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_InsertTable_3x3"), insertTableSubMenuItemClicked, new Tuple<int, int>(3, 3)));
			insertTableMenuItem.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithEvent(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_InsertTable_4x4"), insertTableSubMenuItemClicked, new Tuple<int, int>(4, 4)));
			insertTableMenuItem.Items.Add(CreateContextMenuItemWithEvent(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MI_InsertTable_5x5"), insertTableSubMenuItemClicked, new Tuple<int, int>(5, 5)));
			ContextMenuOpeningEventArgs e = new ContextMenuOpeningEventArgs(_contextMenu);
			richTextEditor.RaiseContextMenuOpening(e);
			if (!e.Cancel)
			{
				EventHandler<OpeningEventArgs> contextMenuOpening = null;
				contextMenuOpening = delegate
				{
					CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
					_contextMenu.Opening -= contextMenuOpening;
				};
				EventHandler<CancellableEventArgs> contextMenuClosing = null;
				contextMenuClosing = delegate
				{
					ContextMenuClosingEventArgs args2 = new ContextMenuClosingEventArgs(_contextMenu);
					richTextEditor.RaiseContextMenuClosing(args2);
				};
				EventHandler<EventArgs> contextMenuClosed = null;
				contextMenuClosed = delegate
				{
					_contextMenu.PopupParent = null;
					_contextMenu.Closed -= contextMenuClosed;
					_contextMenu.Closing -= contextMenuClosing;
					richTextEditor.SetFocusToActiveViewPresenter();
					_contextMenu = null;
				};
				_contextMenu.Opening += contextMenuOpening;
				_contextMenu.Closing += contextMenuClosing;
				_contextMenu.Closed += contextMenuClosed;
				_contextMenu.PopupParent = richDocumentViewLayoutPanel as Panel;
				_contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			}
		}

		internal Point? TextAreaPointFromDocumentOffset(int documentOffset, bool isDocumentOffsetAtSelectionEnd = false)
		{
			Point? point = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, isDocumentOffsetAtSelectionEnd);
			if (!point.HasValue)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return TextAreaPointFromLayoutPoint(point.Value);
		}

		internal Point TextAreaPointFromLayoutPoint(Point layoutPoint)
		{
			return new Point(layoutPoint.X, layoutPoint.Y - VerticalViewOffset);
		}

		internal void SetCursor(RichTextHitTestResultArea hitTestResultArea, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			RichTextCursorType richTextCursorType = RichTextCursorType.IBeam;
			richTextCursorType = ((hitTestResultArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.Hyperlink && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control)) ? RichTextCursorType.Hyperlink : (hitTestResultArea switch
			{
				RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellHorizontalResizeBorder => RichTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved ? RichTextCursorType.IBeam : RichTextCursorType.SizeNS, 
				RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder => RichTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved ? RichTextCursorType.IBeam : RichTextCursorType.SizeWE, 
				RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellMarginAreaLeft => RichTextCursorType.SelectCell, 
				RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableColumnPrefixArea => RichTextCursorType.SelectColumn, 
				RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableRowPrefixArea => RichTextCursorType.SelectRow, 
				RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableResizeCorner => RichTextCursorType.SizeNWSE, 
				_ => RichTextCursorType.IBeam, 
			}));
			UpdateCursorHelperWithCursorType(richTextCursorType, e);
		}

		internal void UpdateCursorHelperWithCursorType(RichTextCursorType cursorType, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			CustomCursorHelper cursorHelper = CursorHelper;
			if (RichTextCursorManager.InitializeCursorHelper(cursorType, cursorHelper))
			{
				cursorHelper.ShowTemplate();
				cursorHelper.ProcessMouseMove(e);
			}
			else
			{
				cursorHelper.ProcessMouseMove(e);
			}
			Presenter.OnCursorChanged(Presenter.TextArea.Cursor);
		}

		internal void UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars()
		{
			ScrollManager scrollManager = ScrollManager;
			Visibility verticalScrollBarVisibility = VerticalScrollBarVisibility;
			Visibility horizontalScrollBarVisibility = HorizontalScrollBarVisibility;
			Visibility visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			if (RichTextEditor.IsInInfiniteHeightContainer)
			{
				visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else
			{
				switch (RichTextEditor.VerticalScrollBarVisibility)
				{
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Auto:
					visibility = ((!scrollManager.GetIsVerticalScrollBarNeeded()) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
					break;
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled:
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Hidden:
					visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					break;
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Visible:
					visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					break;
				}
			}
			Visibility visibility2 = Visibility.Visible;
			switch (RichTextEditor.HorizontalScrollBarVisibility)
			{
			case ScrollBarVisibility.Auto:
				visibility2 = ((!scrollManager.GetIsHorizontalScrollBarNeeded()) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
				break;
			case ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled:
			case ScrollBarVisibility.Hidden:
				visibility2 = Visibility.Collapsed;
				break;
			case ScrollBarVisibility.Visible:
				visibility2 = Visibility.Visible;
				break;
			}
			if (!RichTextEditor.IsInInfiniteHeightContainer && visibility == Visibility.Collapsed && RichTextEditor.VerticalScrollBarVisibility == ScrollBarVisibility.Auto && visibility2 == Visibility.Visible && horizontalScrollBarVisibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
			{
				visibility = ((!scrollManager.GetIsVerticalScrollBarNeeded(visibility2 == Visibility.Visible)) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			}
			if (visibility2 == Visibility.Collapsed && RichTextEditor.HorizontalScrollBarVisibility == ScrollBarVisibility.Auto && visibility == Visibility.Visible && verticalScrollBarVisibility == Visibility.Collapsed)
			{
				visibility2 = ((!scrollManager.GetIsHorizontalScrollBarNeeded(visibility == Visibility.Visible)) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			}
			VerticalScrollBarVisibility = visibility;
			if (!IsResizeOperationInProgress)
			{
				HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = visibility2;
			}
		}

		internal void VerifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout()
		{
			_verifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout = true;
		}

		internal void VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout()
		{
			_verifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout = true;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDocumentChanged(RichTextDocument oldDocument, RichTextDocument newDocument)
		{
			ProcessDocumentContentsReplacement();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnLoaded()
		{
			_updateCachedPositionInfoAfterNextLayout = true;
			if (RichTextEditor != null)
			{
				RichTextEditor.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					InitializeDocumentVersion();
					CreateAdornmentGenerators();
				});
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMouseLeftControl()
		{
			SelectionManager.HyperlinkToolTip.IsOpen = false;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnUnloaded()
		{
			UnloadAdornmentGenerators();
			if (_layoutManager != null)
			{
				_layoutManager.PropertyChanged -= LayoutManager_PropertyChanged;
			}
			DisposeLayoutProviders();
		}

		protected internal void RefreshLayerLayout(AdornmentLayerInfo layerInfo)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layerInfo, "layerInfo");
			if (Presenter != null && Presenter.TextArea != null)
			{
				Presenter.TextArea.AdornmentLayersManager.InvalidateLayer(layerInfo);
			}
		}

		protected internal void RefreshLayersLayout()
		{
			if (Presenter != null && Presenter.TextArea != null)
			{
				Presenter.TextArea.AdornmentLayersManager.InvalidateAllLayers();
			}
		}

		private void AddAdornmentGenerators()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor != null && RichTextEditor.Document != null)
			{
				CaretAdornmentGeneratorProvider caretAdornmentGeneratorProvider = new CaretAdornmentGeneratorProvider();
				AdornmentGenerators.Add("Caret", caretAdornmentGeneratorProvider.CreateAdornmentGenerator(this));
				SelectionAdornmentGeneratorProvider selectionAdornmentGeneratorProvider = new SelectionAdornmentGeneratorProvider();
				AdornmentGenerators.Add("Selection", selectionAdornmentGeneratorProvider.CreateAdornmentGenerator(this));
				EditGlyphsAdornmentGeneratorProvider editGlyphsAdornmentGeneratorProvider = new EditGlyphsAdornmentGeneratorProvider();
				AdornmentGenerators.Add("EditGlyphs", editGlyphsAdornmentGeneratorProvider.CreateAdornmentGenerator(this));
			}
		}

		private void BringCaretIntoView()
		{
			double y = Caret.PixelLocation.Y;
			Rect targetRectangle = new Rect
			{
				Y = y,
				X = 0.0,
				Height = Caret.Height,
				Width = Caret.Width
			};
			Presenter.TextArea.BringIntoView(targetRectangle);
		}

		private void CreateAdornmentGenerators()
		{
			UnloadAdornmentGenerators();
			AddAdornmentGenerators();
		}

		private void DisposeLayoutProviders()
		{
			foreach (LayoutProvider value in LayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				value.Dispose();
			}
			LayoutProviders.Clear();
		}

		private void InitializeDocumentVersion()
		{
			_lastDocumentVersion = null;
			if (Document != null)
			{
				_lastDocumentVersion = Document.CurrentVersion;
			}
		}

		private void LayoutManager_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.PropertyName)
			{
			case "LayoutHeight":
			case "LayoutWidth":
			{
				ClearCaretLayoutInfoCache();
				ScrollManager.UpdateScrollBarRanges();
				Point? point = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset) ?? new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				if (e.PropertyName == "LayoutWidth" && point.Value.Y != _cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint.Y)
				{
					int num = 0;
					if (Document != null && Document.GetNodeAtDocumentOffset(0) is ParagraphNode paragraphNode)
					{
						num = paragraphNode.PrefixOffsetCount;
					}
					if (_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset == 0 || _cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset == 1 || _cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset == num)
					{
						VerticalViewOffset = 0.0;
					}
					else
					{
						VerticalViewOffset = point.Value.Y;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					Point? point2 = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(_cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffset) ?? new Point(0.0, 0.0);
					if (point.Value.X != _cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint.X || point2.Value.X != _cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint.X)
					{
						RefreshLayersLayout();
					}
					else
					{
						double value = _cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint.Y - _cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint.Y;
						double value2 = point2.Value.Y - point.Value.Y;
						if (!RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(value, value2))
						{
							RefreshLayersLayout();
						}
					}
				}
				if (e.PropertyName == "LayoutHeight" && !_initialLayoutCompleted)
				{
					RichTextEditor.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars();
					});
				}
				break;
			}
			case "Status":
				switch (LayoutManager.Status)
				{
				case LayoutManagerProcessStatus.BuildingStructureNotStarted:
				case LayoutManagerProcessStatus.BuildingStructure:
					_initialLayoutCompleted = false;
					break;
				case LayoutManagerProcessStatus.Complete:
					_initialLayoutCompleted = true;
					RefreshLayersLayout();
					if (_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint == new Point(0.0, 0.0) && _cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint == new Point(0.0, 0.0))
					{
						_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset) ?? new Point(0.0, 0.0);
						_cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(_cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffset) ?? new Point(0.0, 0.0);
					}
					RichTextEditor.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars();
					});
					break;
				case LayoutManagerProcessStatus.ProcessingLayout:
				case LayoutManagerProcessStatus.RefreshingSettings:
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void OnResizeTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ResizeTimer.Stop();
			RefreshLayersLayout();
			Presenter.TextArea.UpdateLayout();
		}

		private void ProcessDocumentContentsReplacement()
		{
			DisposeLayoutProviders();
			_layoutManager = null;
			ClearCaretLayoutInfoCache();
			Caret.InitializeLocation();
			_selection = null;
			if (_selectionManager != null)
			{
				_selectionManager.OnDispose();
				_selectionManager = null;
			}
			SelectionManager.UpdateSelectionWithCaretLocation();
			Caret.UpdateVisibility();
			CreateAdornmentGenerators();
			VerticalViewOffset = 0.0;
			InitializeDocumentVersion();
			_initialLayoutCompleted = false;
			_updateCachedPositionInfoAfterNextLayout = true;
		}

		private void ProcessOnLayoutChanged()
		{
			if (_makeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout)
			{
				_makeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout = false;
				Caret.PreferredCaretOffsetX = Caret.PixelLocation.X;
			}
			VersionedDocumentOffset documentOffset = Caret.DocumentOffset;
			if (_verifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout)
			{
				_verifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout = false;
				if (!GetIsDocumentOffsetInView(documentOffset, fullyInView: true))
				{
					EnsureLineContainingOffsetIsFullyInView(documentOffset);
					VerifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout();
					RichTextEditor.RefreshAllViews();
				}
				else
				{
					Point? point = TextAreaPointFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
					if (point.HasValue && (point.Value.X < HorizontalViewOffset || point.Value.X > Presenter.TextArea.ActualWidth + HorizontalViewOffset))
					{
						VerifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout();
						RichTextEditor.RefreshAllViews();
					}
				}
				BringCaretIntoView();
			}
			else if (_verifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout)
			{
				_verifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout = false;
				EnsureXOffsetIsInView(Caret.PixelLocation.X, includePadding: true);
			}
			if (_updateCachedPositionInfoAfterNextLayout)
			{
				LayoutManagerProcessStatus status = LayoutManager.Status;
				if (status != LayoutManagerProcessStatus.BuildingStructure && status != LayoutManagerProcessStatus.BuildingStructureNotStarted)
				{
					_updateCachedPositionInfoAfterNextLayout = false;
					_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset = FirstVisibleDocumentOffset ?? 0;
					_cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffset = LastVisibleDocumentOffset ?? 0;
					_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(_cachedFirstVisibleDocumentOffset) ?? new Point(0.0, 0.0);
					_cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffsetLayoutPoint = LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(_cachedLastVisibleDocumentOffset) ?? new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private void RefreshAdornmentGenerators()
		{
			foreach (RichTextAdornmentGeneratorBase value in AdornmentGenerators.Values)
			{
				value.OnRefreshAdornments();
			}
		}

		private void UnloadAdornmentGenerators()
		{
			foreach (RichTextAdornmentGeneratorBase value in AdornmentGenerators.Values)
			{
				value.OnUnloaded();
			}
			AdornmentGenerators.Clear();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnActivated()
		{
			RichTextEditor.Caret = Caret;
			RichTextEditor.Selection = Selection;
			this.Activated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDeactivated()
		{
			this.Deactivated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayoutChanged(RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			this.LayoutChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
			ProcessOnLayoutChanged();
		}

		internal void RaiseLayoutChanged(RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnLayoutChanged(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged(RichTextSelectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			this.SelectionChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		internal void RaiseSelectionChanged(RichTextSelectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnSelectionChanged(args);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnFocusReceived()
		{
			this.FocusReceived?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnFocusLost()
		{
			this.FocusLost?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnClipboardOperationExecuted(RichTextClipboardOperationEventArgs args)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor != null)
			{
				RichTextEditor.RaiseClipboardOperationExecuted(args);
			}
			VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			if (args.ClipboardOperation != ClipboardOperation.Cut)
			{
				_ = args.ClipboardOperation;
				_ = 2;
			}
			if (_clipboardOperationTransaction != null)
			{
				if (!_clipboardOperationTransaction.IsClosed && (args.ClipboardOperation == ClipboardOperation.Cut || args.ClipboardOperation == ClipboardOperation.Paste))
				{
					_clipboardOperationTransaction.Commit();
				}
				_clipboardOperationTransaction = null;
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnClipboardOperationExecuting(RichTextClipboardOperationCancelEventArgs args)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor != null)
			{
				RichTextEditor.RaiseClipboardOperationExecuting(args);
			}
			if (!args.Cancel && (args.ClipboardOperation == ClipboardOperation.Cut || args.ClipboardOperation == ClipboardOperation.Paste))
			{
				if (ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition)
				{
					int start = Selection.Start;
					int end = Selection.End;
					ForceImeCompositionEnd();
					Selection.SetRange(start, end);
				}
				Caret.TranslateDocumentOffsetToCurrentVersion(OffsetTrackingMode.Positive);
				VersionedDocumentOffset documentOffset = Caret.DocumentOffset;
				RichTextDocument document = Document;
				Selection selection = Selection;
				_clipboardOperationTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, this, documentOffset, selection.GetArrayOfClonedRanges(), selection.IsSelectionInverted);
				document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(_clipboardOperationTransaction);
			}
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnSelectionReplacingText(DocumentSpan documentSpanAffected)
		{
			Caret.TranslateDocumentOffsetToCurrentVersion(OffsetTrackingMode.Positive);
			VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			MakeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout();
			RichTextDocument document = Document;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			_selectionReplacedTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, this, Caret.DocumentOffset, selection.GetArrayOfClonedRanges(), selection.IsSelectionInverted);
			document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(_selectionReplacedTransaction);
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnSelectionReplacedText(string error)
		{
			if (_selectionReplacedTransaction != null)
			{
				if (!_selectionReplacedTransaction.IsClosed)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
					{
						_selectionReplacedTransaction.Commit();
					}
					else
					{
						_selectionReplacedTransaction.Rollback();
					}
				}
				_selectionReplacedTransaction = null;
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnSelectionModifyingSelectedContent()
		{
			VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			RichTextDocument document = Document;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			_selectionModifiedTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, this, Caret.DocumentOffset, selection.GetArrayOfClonedRanges(), selection.IsSelectionInverted);
			document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(_selectionModifiedTransaction);
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnSelectionModifiedSelectedContent(string error, bool preserveSelection)
		{
			if (_selectionModifiedTransaction != null)
			{
				if (!_selectionModifiedTransaction.IsClosed)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
					{
						_selectionModifiedTransaction.Commit();
						if (!preserveSelection)
						{
							Selection.Collapse();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						_selectionModifiedTransaction.Rollback();
					}
				}
				_selectionModifiedTransaction = null;
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnSelectionNavigating(int oldDocumentOffset)
		{
			Caret.PixelLocationOverrideMode = CaretPixelLocationOverrideMode.None;
			_preNavigationOffset = oldDocumentOffset;
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.OnSelectionNavigated(int newDocumentOffset, bool offsetXUpdated, bool navigatedToStartOfLine, bool navigatedToEndOfLine)
		{
			if (navigatedToStartOfLine || navigatedToEndOfLine)
			{
				Caret.PixelLocationOverrideMode = (navigatedToStartOfLine ? CaretPixelLocationOverrideMode.ForceLocationToStartOfLine : CaretPixelLocationOverrideMode.ForceLocationToEndOfLine);
				Caret.PixelLocationOverridePreNavigationOffset = _preNavigationOffset;
				Caret.VerifyPixelLocation();
			}
			EnsureCaretAtOffsetIsFullyInView(newDocumentOffset);
			EnsureXOffsetIsInView(Caret.PixelLocation.X, includePadding: true, offsetXUpdated);
			if (offsetXUpdated)
			{
				newDocumentOffset = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(newDocumentOffset, Document);
				if (!GetIsDocumentOffsetAtListIndicator(newDocumentOffset))
				{
					Point? point = TextAreaPointFromDocumentOffset(newDocumentOffset, Selection.Length > 0);
					if (point.HasValue)
					{
						Caret.PreferredCaretOffsetX = point.Value.X;
					}
				}
			}
			BringCaretIntoView();
		}

		void ISelectionOwner.RefreshCommandsCanExecute()
		{
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}
	}

			: base(lines)

		}

		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(lineElementProxy, "lineElementProxy");
			LineElementProxy = lineElementProxy;
		}

		SelectDownByPage,
		SelectUpByPage,
		SelectPreviousCharacter,
		SelectNextCharacter,
		SelectPreviousWord,
		SelectNextWord,
		SelectToStartOfDocument,
		SelectToEndOfDocument,
		SelectToStartOfLine,
		SelectToEndOfLine,
		ToggleInsert,
		ToggleSubscript,
		ToggleSuperscript,
		Backspace,
		ToggleBullets,
		ToggleNumbering
	}

		}

	internal class AdornmentLayersManager
	{
		private RichDocumentViewTextArea _viewTextArea;

		private RichDocumentView _documentView;

		private XamRichTextEditor _richTextEditor;

		private Dictionary<AdornmentLayerInfo, AdornmentLayer> _layers;

		private Dictionary<AdornmentLayerInfo, AdornmentLayer> Layers
		{
			get
			{
				if (_layers == null)
				{
					_layers = new Dictionary<AdornmentLayerInfo, AdornmentLayer>(4);
				}
				return _layers;
			}
		}

		internal AdornmentLayersManager(RichDocumentViewTextArea viewTextArea)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(viewTextArea, "viewTextArea");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(viewTextArea.DocumentView, "viewTextArea.DocumentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(viewTextArea.DocumentView.RichTextEditor, "viewTextArea.DocumentView.RichTextEditor");
			_viewTextArea = viewTextArea;
			_documentView = viewTextArea.DocumentView;
			_richTextEditor = viewTextArea.DocumentView.RichTextEditor;
			VerifyLayers();
		}

		internal void AddAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayer adornmentLayerToAdd)
		{
			if (Layers.ContainsKey(adornmentLayerToAdd.AdornmentLayerInfo))
			{
				return;
			}
			Layers.Add(adornmentLayerToAdd.AdornmentLayerInfo, adornmentLayerToAdd);
			List<ISupportPositioning> list = new List<ISupportPositioning>(Layers.Count);
			foreach (AdornmentLayerInfo key in Layers.Keys)
			{
				list.Add(key);
			}
			PositionSorter.Sort(list, out var isValid);
			if (!isValid)
			{
				RichTextEditorUtilities.LogDebuggerError(1, "Layout", "Invalid ISupportPositioning info specified for one or more AdornmentLayers.  AdornmentLayer order will be undefined.");
			}
			UIElementCollection children = _viewTextArea.Children;
			int count = children.Count;
			int num = list.IndexOf(adornmentLayerToAdd.AdornmentLayerInfo);
			if (num > -1)
			{
				if (num >= count)
				{
					children.Add(adornmentLayerToAdd);
				}
				else
				{
					children.Insert(num, adornmentLayerToAdd);
				}
			}
		}

		internal AdornmentLayer GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo adornmentLayerInfo)
		{
			if (Layers.ContainsKey(adornmentLayerInfo))
			{
				return Layers[adornmentLayerInfo];
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal void InvalidateAllLayers()
		{
			foreach (AdornmentLayer value in Layers.Values)
			{
				value.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		internal void InvalidateLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo layerInfo)
		{
			if (Layers.ContainsKey(layerInfo))
			{
				Layers[layerInfo].InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayer adornmentLayerToRemove)
		{
			if (_viewTextArea.Children.Contains(adornmentLayerToRemove))
			{
				_viewTextArea.Children.Remove(adornmentLayerToRemove);
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveAllAdornmentLayers()
		{
			_viewTextArea.Children.Clear();
		}

		internal void VerifyLayers()
		{
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.BackgroundContent))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.BackgroundContent);
			}
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.SpellingError))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.SpellingError);
			}
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.MiddleContent))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.MiddleContent);
			}
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.ForegroundContent))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.ForegroundContent);
			}
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.Selection))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.Selection);
			}
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.Caret))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.Caret);
			}
			if (!Layers.ContainsKey(AdornmentLayerInfo.EditGlyphs))
			{
				AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo.EditGlyphs);
			}
		}

		private void AddLayerPrivate(AdornmentLayerInfo layerInfo)
		{
			AdornmentLayer adornmentLayerToAdd = ((layerInfo.Key == "BackgroundContent") ? new BackgroundContentAdornmentLayer(_documentView, layerInfo) : ((layerInfo.Key == "MiddleContent") ? new MiddleContentAdornmentLayer(_documentView, layerInfo) : ((!(layerInfo.Key == "ForegroundContent")) ? new AdornmentLayer(_documentView, layerInfo) : new ForegroundContentAdornmentLayer(_documentView, layerInfo))));
			AddAdornmentLayer(adornmentLayerToAdd);
		}
	}
	public class FindReplaceManager : IEnumerator<FindMatch>, IDisposable, IEnumerator, IEnumerable<FindMatch>, IEnumerable, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private class FindMatchSpanComparer : IComparer<FindMatch>
		{
			internal FindMatchSpanComparer()
			{
			}

			int IComparer<FindMatch>.Compare(FindMatch x, FindMatch y)
			{
				return x.Span.Start - y.Span.Start;
			}
		}

		private List<FindMatch> _findMatches;

		private List<FindMatch> _highlightMatches;

		private string _lastTextToFind;

		private DocumentSpan[] _lastSpansToSearch;

		private FindCriteria _lastFindCriteria;

		private bool? _lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive;

		private FindOperator? _lastFindCriteriaOperator;

		private int _currentMatchIndex;

		private bool _isFindReplaceDialogActive;

		private bool _bypassContentChangedProcessing;

		private bool _selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager;

		private bool _lastSelectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager;

		private bool _isSelectionChangeProcessingPending;

		public int FindMatchesCount => FindMatches.Count;

		public int HighlightMatchesCount => HighlightMatches.Count;

		public bool IsFindReplaceDialogActive
		{
			get
			{
				return _isFindReplaceDialogActive;
			}
			set
			{
				_isFindReplaceDialogActive = value;
				RichTextEditor.IsFindReplaceDialogActive = value;
			}
		}

		public XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; private set; }

		internal List<FindMatch> HighlightMatches
		{
			get
			{
				if (_highlightMatches == null)
				{
					_highlightMatches = new List<FindMatch>();
				}
				return _highlightMatches;
			}
		}

		private List<FindMatch> FindMatches
		{
			get
			{
				if (_findMatches == null)
				{
					_findMatches = new List<FindMatch>();
				}
				return _findMatches;
			}
		}

		public FindMatch Current
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentMatchIndex > -1 && _currentMatchIndex < FindMatchesCount)
				{
					return FindMatches[_currentMatchIndex];
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		object IEnumerator.Current => Current;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		internal FindReplaceManager(XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor)
		{
			RichTextEditor = richTextEditor;
			RichTextEditor.DocumentContentChanged += RichTextEditor_DocumentContentChanged;
			RichTextEditor.DocumentChanged += RichTextEditor_DocumentChanged;
			RichTextEditor.SelectionChanged += RichTextEditor_SelectionChanged;
		}

		public void ClearHighlighting()
		{
			_highlightMatches = null;
			RaisePropertyChanged("HighlightMatchesCount");
		}

		public void ClearResults()
		{
			_findMatches = null;
			_lastTextToFind = null;
			_lastSpansToSearch = null;
			_lastFindCriteria = null;
			_lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive = null;
			_lastFindCriteriaOperator = null;
			_currentMatchIndex = -1;
			RaisePropertyChanged("FindMatchesCount");
		}

		public int FindInDocument(string textToFind, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria = null)
		{
			return FindInSpan(textToFind, RichTextEditor.Document.RootNode.GetDocumentSpan(), out error, findCriteria);
		}

		public int FindInSpan(string textToFind, DocumentSpan spanToSearch, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria = null)
		{
			return FindInSpans(textToFind, new DocumentSpan[1] { spanToSearch }, out error, findCriteria);
		}

		public int FindInSpans(string textToFind, DocumentSpan[] spansToSearch, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria = null)
		{
			return FindInSpans(textToFind, spansToSearch, out error, findCriteria, force: false);
		}

		public int FindInSelection(string textToFind, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria = null)
		{
			return FindInSpans(textToFind, GetSpansFromSelectionRanges(), out error, findCriteria);
		}

		public int Highlight(string textToHighlight, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria = null)
		{
			error = null;
			RichTextDocument document = RichTextEditor.Document;
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			DocumentSpan[] spans = ((RichTextEditor.Selection.Length <= 0) ? new DocumentSpan[1] { document.RootNode.GetDocumentSpan() } : GetSpansFromSelectionRanges());
			IEnumerable<FindMatch> matches = document.FindAll(spans, textToHighlight, out error, findCriteria, allowOverlappingMatches: true).Matches;
			if (error != null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			HighlightMatches.AddRange(matches);
			HighlightMatches.Sort(new FindMatchSpanComparer());
			int i = 0;
			for (List<FindMatch> highlightMatches = HighlightMatches; i < highlightMatches.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i < highlightMatches.Count - 1)
				{
					DocumentSpan documentSpan = highlightMatches[i].Span;
					DocumentSpan documentSpan2 = highlightMatches[i + 1].Span;
					if (documentSpan == documentSpan2 && highlightMatches[i].Text == highlightMatches[i + 1].Text)
					{
						highlightMatches.RemoveAt(i + 1);
					}
				}
			}
			RaisePropertyChanged("HighlightMatchesCount");
			return matches.Count();
		}

		public bool MoveToNearestMatchFollowingOffset(int offset)
		{
			List<FindMatch> findMatches = FindMatches;
			int count = findMatches.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (offset <= (int)findMatches[i].Span.Start)
				{
					_currentMatchIndex = i;
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool MoveToNearestMatchPreceedingOffset(int offset)
		{
			List<FindMatch> findMatches = FindMatches;
			for (int num = findMatches.Count - 1; num > -1; num--)
			{
				if (offset >= (int)findMatches[num].Span.Start)
				{
					_currentMatchIndex = num;
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool ReplaceCurrent(string replacementText, out string error)
		{
			error = null;
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			Selection selection = richTextEditor.Selection;
			if (selection == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Current == null || _isSelectionChangeProcessingPending)
			{
				MoveToNearestMatchFollowingOffset(selection.Start);
				_isSelectionChangeProcessingPending = false;
			}
			if (Current != null)
			{
				RichDocumentView documentView = richTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView;
				DocumentSpan span = Current.Span.Span;
				if (span.Start != selection.Start || span.Length != selection.Length)
				{
					SelectCurrentMatch();
					return true;
				}
				_bypassContentChangedProcessing = true;
				DocumentSpan documentSpan = Current.Span;
				ReplaceWithinSpans(new DocumentSpan[1] { documentSpan }, _lastTextToFind, replacementText, out error, _lastFindCriteria, clearFindResultsAfterReplace: false);
				RemoveMatchesThatIntersectSpan(documentSpan);
				TranslateAllMatchSpans();
				richTextEditor.RefreshAllViews();
				EventHandler<RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs> layoutChanged = null;
				layoutChanged = delegate
				{
					if (FindMatches.Count > 0 && Current != null)
					{
						SelectSpanAndScrollIntoViewIfNecessary(Current.Span);
					}
					documentView.LayoutChanged -= layoutChanged;
				};
				documentView.LayoutChanged += layoutChanged;
				return string.IsNullOrEmpty(error);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public int ReplaceAll(string textToFind, string replacementText, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria = null)
		{
			error = null;
			DocumentSpan[] spansToReplace = ((RichTextEditor.Selection == null || RichTextEditor.Selection.Length <= 0) ? new DocumentSpan[1] { RichTextEditor.Document.RootNode.GetDocumentSpan() } : GetSpansFromSelectionRanges());
			return ReplaceWithinSpans(spansToReplace, textToFind, replacementText, out error, findCriteria, clearFindResultsAfterReplace: true);
		}

		public int SelectAllMatches()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (FindMatches.Count > 0)
			{
				_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
				RichTextEditor.Selection.SetSelectionWithFindMatches(FindMatches);
				return FindMatches.Count;
			}
			RichTextEditor.Selection.Collapse();
			return 0;
		}

		public bool SelectCurrentMatch()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (FindMatches.Count > 0 && Current != null)
			{
				_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
				RichTextEditor.Selection.SetRange(Current.Span.Start, Current.Span.End);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool SelectNextMatch()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (FindMatches.Count > 0)
			{
				int num = RichTextEditor.Selection.Start;
				if (RichTextEditor.Selection.Length > 0 && _lastSelectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager)
				{
					num++;
				}
				MoveToNearestMatchFollowingOffset(num);
				if (Current != null)
				{
					SelectSpanAndScrollIntoViewIfNecessary(Current.Span);
				}
				return Current != null;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool SelectPreviousMatch()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (FindMatches.Count > 0)
			{
				int start = RichTextEditor.Selection.Start;
				if (RichTextEditor.Selection.Length > 0 && _lastSelectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager)
				{
					start--;
					Math.Max(0, start);
				}
				MoveToNearestMatchPreceedingOffset(RichTextEditor.Selection.Start - 1);
				if (Current != null)
				{
					SelectSpanAndScrollIntoViewIfNecessary(Current.Span);
				}
				return Current != null;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private int FindInSpans(string textToFind, DocumentSpan[] spansToSearch, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria, bool force)
		{
			error = null;
			if (!force && textToFind == _lastTextToFind && GetIsOperatorSameAsLast(findCriteria) && GetIsCaseSensitiveSameAsLast(findCriteria) && GetAreSpanArraysEqual(spansToSearch, _lastSpansToSearch))
			{
				return FindMatches.Count;
			}
			ClearResults();
			IEnumerable<FindMatch> matches = RichTextEditor.Document.FindAll(spansToSearch, textToFind, out error, findCriteria, allowOverlappingMatches: true).Matches;
			if (error != null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			FindMatches.AddRange(matches);
			RaisePropertyChanged("FindMatchesCount");
			_lastTextToFind = textToFind;
			_lastSpansToSearch = spansToSearch;
			if (findCriteria == null)
			{
				_lastFindCriteria = null;
				_lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive = null;
				_lastFindCriteriaOperator = null;
			}
			else
			{
				_lastFindCriteria = findCriteria;
				_lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive = findCriteria.CaseSensitive;
				_lastFindCriteriaOperator = findCriteria.Operator;
			}
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection != null)
			{
				MoveToNearestMatchFollowingOffset(RichTextEditor.Selection.Start);
			}
			return FindMatches.Count;
		}

		private bool GetAreSpanArraysEqual(DocumentSpan[] spanArray1, DocumentSpan[] spanArray2)
		{
			if (spanArray1 == null || spanArray2 == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (spanArray1.Length != spanArray2.Length)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < spanArray1.Length; i++)
			{
				if (spanArray1[i] != spanArray2[i])
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool GetIsCaseSensitiveSameAsLast(FindCriteria findCriteria)
		{
			if (findCriteria == null)
			{
				return !_lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive.HasValue;
			}
			if (!_lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive.HasValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return findCriteria.CaseSensitive == _lastFindCriteriaCaseSensitive.Value;
		}

		private bool GetIsOperatorSameAsLast(FindCriteria findCriteria)
		{
			if (findCriteria == null)
			{
				return !_lastFindCriteriaOperator.HasValue;
			}
			if (!_lastFindCriteriaOperator.HasValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return findCriteria.Operator == _lastFindCriteriaOperator.Value;
		}

		private DocumentSpan[] GetSpansFromSelectionRanges()
		{
			if (RichTextEditor.Selection == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Selection selection = RichTextEditor.Selection;
			DocumentSpan[] array = new DocumentSpan[selection.Ranges.Count];
			for (int i = 0; i < selection.Ranges.Count; i++)
			{
				array[i] = selection.Ranges[i].DocumentSpan;
			}
			return array;
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private void RemoveMatch(DocumentSpan spanToRemove)
		{
			List<FindMatch> findMatches = FindMatches;
			int count = findMatches.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (findMatches[i].Span.Span == spanToRemove)
				{
					findMatches.RemoveAt(i);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void RemoveMatchesThatIntersectSpan(DocumentSpan span)
		{
			List<FindMatch> findMatches = FindMatches;
			for (int num = findMatches.Count - 1; num > -1; num--)
			{
				if (findMatches[num].Span.IntersectsWith(span))
				{
					findMatches.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
		}

		private int ReplaceWithinSpans(DocumentSpan[] spansToReplace, string textToFind, string replacementText, out string error, FindCriteria findCriteria, bool clearFindResultsAfterReplace)
		{
			error = null;
			if (spansToReplace == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(textToFind))
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (replacementText == null)
			{
				replacementText = "";
			}
			_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
			ReplaceResult replaceResult = RichTextEditor.Document.ReplaceAll(spansToReplace, textToFind, replacementText, out error, findCriteria);
			if (clearFindResultsAfterReplace)
			{
				ClearResults();
			}
			return replaceResult.Replacements.Count();
		}

		private void RichTextEditor_DocumentChanged(object sender, DocumentChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ClearResults();
			ClearHighlighting();
		}

		private void RichTextEditor_DocumentContentChanged(object sender, DocumentContentChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_bypassContentChangedProcessing)
			{
				ClearResults();
			}
			else
			{
				_bypassContentChangedProcessing = false;
			}
			ClearHighlighting();
		}

		private void RichTextEditor_SelectionChanged(object sender, RichTextSelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			bool selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = _selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager;
			if (!_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager)
			{
				_isSelectionChangeProcessingPending = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = false;
			}
			_lastSelectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager;
		}

		private void SelectSpanAndScrollIntoViewIfNecessary(VersionedDocumentSpan span)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			RichDocumentView activeDocumentView = richTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView;
			EventHandler<RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs> layoutChanged = null;
			layoutChanged = delegate
			{
				if (RichTextEditor.Selection != null)
				{
					Selection selection2 = richTextEditor.Selection;
					_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
					selection2.Collapse();
					_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
					selection2.SetRange(span.Start, span.End);
				}
				activeDocumentView.LayoutChanged -= layoutChanged;
			};
			if (!activeDocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetInView(span.Start, fullyInView: true))
			{
				activeDocumentView.LayoutChanged += layoutChanged;
				activeDocumentView.BringDocumentOffsetIntoView(Current.Span.Start);
			}
			else if (RichTextEditor.Selection != null)
			{
				Selection selection = RichTextEditor.Selection;
				_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
				selection.Collapse();
				_selectionChangeTriggeredByFindReplaceManager = true;
				selection.SetRange(span.Start, span.End);
			}
		}

		private void TranslateAllMatchSpans()
		{
			RichTextDocument document = RichTextEditor.Document;
			IEnumerable<FindMatch> source = FindMatches.Select((FindMatch findMatch) => new FindMatch(findMatch.Span.TranslateTo(document.CurrentVersion, SpanTrackingMode.EdgePositive), findMatch.Text));
			source = source.ToArray();
			FindMatches.Clear();
			FindMatches.AddRange(source);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
		}

		public bool MoveNext()
		{
			if (_currentMatchIndex < FindMatchesCount - 1)
			{
				_currentMatchIndex++;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool MovePrevious()
		{
			if (_currentMatchIndex > 0)
			{
				_currentMatchIndex--;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			_currentMatchIndex = -1;
		}

		public IEnumerator<FindMatch> GetEnumerator()
		{
			_currentMatchIndex = -1;
			return this;
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			_currentMatchIndex = -1;
			return this;
		}
	}
	internal class LayoutProvider : DependencyObject, IDisposable
	{
		private class LineOffsetYComparer : IComparer<LineElementProxy>
		{
			internal LineOffsetYComparer()
			{
			}

			int IComparer<LineElementProxy>.Compare(LineElementProxy x, LineElementProxy y)
			{
				double textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime = x.TextAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime;
				double textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime2 = y.TextAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime;
				if (RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime, textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime2))
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime > textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime2)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				return -1;
			}
		}

		private enum BorderSide
		{
			Left,
			Top,
			Right,
			Bottom
		}

		internal enum SpecialElementType
		{
			None,
			LayoutBlockHighlight,
			Border,

			SearchHighlight,
			ImageOuterContainer
		}

		private struct BorderCornerBevelInfo
		{
			internal bool TopLeftCornerNeedsBevel;

			internal bool TopRightCornerNeedsBevel;

			internal bool BottomLeftCornerNeedsBevel;

			internal bool BottomRightCornerNeedsBevel;

			internal BorderCornerBevelInfo(bool topLeftCornerNeedsBevel, bool topRightCornerNeedsBevel, bool bottomLeftCornerNeedsBevel, bool bottomRightCornerNeedsBevel)
			{
				TopLeftCornerNeedsBevel = topLeftCornerNeedsBevel;
				TopRightCornerNeedsBevel = topRightCornerNeedsBevel;
				BottomLeftCornerNeedsBevel = bottomLeftCornerNeedsBevel;
				BottomRightCornerNeedsBevel = bottomRightCornerNeedsBevel;
			}
		}

			{
				X1Indent = x1Indent;
				X2Indent = x2Indent;
				Y1Indent = y1Indent;
				Y2Indent = y2Indent;
			}
		}
