		internal LayoutProvider(RichDocumentView documentView, IContentElementHost contentElementHost, LayoutPlane layoutPlane, ILayoutBlock associatedLayoutBlock = null, LayoutProvider parentLayoutProvider = null)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(contentElementHost, "contentElementHost");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			ContentElementHost = contentElementHost;
			LayoutPlane = layoutPlane;
			AssociatedLayoutBlock = associatedLayoutBlock;
			ParentLayoutProvider = parentLayoutProvider;
			LastLayoutViewportRect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			LastMeasureSize = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			_visibleLines = new ObservableCollectionExtended<LineElementProxy>();
			_visibleLinesReadOnly = new VisibleLineCollection(_visibleLines);
		}

		internal static bool GetIsBorderElementNewlyRealized(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(IsBorderElementNewlyRealizedProperty);
		}

		internal static void SetIsBorderElementNewlyRealized(DependencyObject target, bool value)
		{
			target.SetValue(IsBorderElementNewlyRealizedPropertyKey, value);
		}

		internal static SpecialElementType GetSpecialElementType(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (SpecialElementType)d.GetValue(SpecialElementTypeProperty);
		}

		internal static void SetSpecialElementType(DependencyObject target, SpecialElementType value)
		{
			target.SetValue(SpecialElementTypePropertyKey, value);
		}

		internal void AddVisibleLinesToList(List<LineElementProxy> list)
		{
			list.AddRange(VisibleLines);
			foreach (LayoutProvider value in ChildLayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				value.AddVisibleLinesToList(list);
			}
		}

		internal Size ArrangeContentElements(Size finalSize)
		{
			_ = DocumentView;
			double contentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetContentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment(ContentElementHost, DocumentView, subtractVerticalViewOffset: false);
			foreach (Visual lineVisual in ContentElementHost.GetLineVisuals())
			{
				if (lineVisual is DocumentViewLineVisual)
				{
					DocumentViewLineVisual documentViewLineVisual = lineVisual as DocumentViewLineVisual;
					if (!((TranslateTransform)documentViewLineVisual.Transform).IsFrozen)
					{
						LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = documentViewLineVisual.LineElementProxy;
						((TranslateTransform)documentViewLineVisual.Transform).X = lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetX;
						((TranslateTransform)documentViewLineVisual.Transform).Y = lineElementProxy.GetTextAreaOffsetYResolved();
					}
				}
				else if (lineVisual is ContentElementClippingHost)
				{
					ContentElementClippingHost contentElementClippingHost = lineVisual as ContentElementClippingHost;
					contentElementClippingHost.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, contentElementClippingHost.DesiredSize.Width, contentElementClippingHost.DesiredSize.Height));
					((TranslateTransform)contentElementClippingHost.RenderTransform).X = TransformOverallOffsetXToLocalCoordinates(contentElementClippingHost.LayoutBlock.OverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels);
					((TranslateTransform)contentElementClippingHost.RenderTransform).Y = TransformOverallOffsetYToLocalCoordinates(contentElementClippingHost.LayoutBlock.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels);
				}
				else if (GetSpecialElementType(lineVisual) == SpecialElementType.ImageOuterContainer)
				{
					ArrangeImageOuterContainer(lineVisual as UIElement, contentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment);
				}
				else if (GetSpecialElementType(lineVisual) == SpecialElementType.UnderlineStrikethrough)
				{
					Shape shape = lineVisual as Shape;
					shape.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, shape.DesiredSize.Width, shape.DesiredSize.Height));
					if (shape.Tag is Tuple<LineElementProxy, LineElementProxy.LineContentProxy> tuple)
					{
						((TranslateTransform)shape.RenderTransform).X = tuple.Item1.TextAreaOffsetX + tuple.Item2.OffsetX;
						((TranslateTransform)shape.RenderTransform).Y = tuple.Item1.TextAreaOffsetY - contentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment;
					}
				}
				else if (lineVisual is BorderRenderingSurface)
				{
					UIElement uIElement = lineVisual as UIElement;
					uIElement.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, uIElement.DesiredSize.Width, uIElement.DesiredSize.Height));
				}
				else
				{
					UIElement uIElement2 = lineVisual as UIElement;
					uIElement2.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, uIElement2.DesiredSize.Width, uIElement2.DesiredSize.Height));
				}
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		internal Size CreateAndMeasureContentElements(Size availableSize)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			LayoutManagerBase layoutManager = documentView.LayoutManager;
			ClearLayoutBlockHighlightElements();
			RemoveRectangleBorderElements();
			BorderElementProxysVertical.Clear();
			BorderElementProxysHorizontal.Clear();
			UnderlineStrikethroughElementProxys.Clear();
			foreach (LayoutProvider value in ChildLayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				value.IsInUse = false;
			}
			List<Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>> list = new List<Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>>(30);
			Rect rect;
			if (IsRootLayoutProvider)
			{
				if (LastMeasureSize.Width != 0.0 && LastMeasureSize.Height != 0.0 && LastMeasureSize != availableSize)
				{
					documentView.ResizeTimer.Start();
				}
				if (!documentView.IsResizeOperationInProgress && documentView.LayoutStyle == RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle.WebLayout)
				{
					XamRichTextEditor.SetLayoutTargetWidth(RichTextEditor, (WebLayoutManager)layoutManager, RichTextEditor.GetSmallestTextAreaWidthInAllViews(documentView, availableSize.Width));
				}
				layoutManager.ValidateStructure();
				rect = ((!documentView.IsResizeOperationInProgress) ? new Rect(0.0, documentView.VerticalViewOffset, availableSize.Width, availableSize.Height) : LastLayoutViewportRect);
				foreach (ILayoutBlock item in layoutManager.GetBlocksInViewPort(rect, LayoutPlane))
				{
					ProcessBlock(item, rect, list);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				rect = AssociatedLayoutBlock.OverallRect.Value;
				foreach (ILayoutBlock item2 in AssociatedLayoutBlock.GetChildBlocksInViewPort(rect))
				{
					ProcessBlock(item2, rect, list);
				}
			}
			List<LineElementProxy> list2 = new List<LineElementProxy>(_visibleLines.Count);
			using (LineElementGenerator lineElementGenerator = new LineElementGenerator(DocumentView, list, list2, ContentElementHost))
			{
				lineElementGenerator.GenerateElements(rect, _forceLineContentRebuildOnNextLayoutPass);
			}
			_forceLineContentRebuildOnNextLayoutPass = false;
			_visibleLines.BeginUpdate();
			_visibleLines.Clear();
			list2.Sort(new LineOffsetYComparer());
			_visibleLines.AddRange(list2);
			_visibleLines.EndUpdate();
			List<Rectangle> layoutBlockHighlightElements = LayoutBlockHighlightElements;
			for (int num = layoutBlockHighlightElements.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = layoutBlockHighlightElements[num];
				ContentElementHost.InsertChild(0, rectangle);
				rectangle.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
			}
			CombineBorderElementProxysIfPossible();
			foreach (FrameworkElement borderElement in BorderElements)
			{
				if (GetIsBorderElementNewlyRealized(borderElement))
				{
					ContentElementHost.AddChild(borderElement);
					SetIsBorderElementNewlyRealized(borderElement, value: false);
				}
				else
				{
					ContentElementHost.RemoveChild(borderElement);
					ContentElementHost.AddChild(borderElement);
				}
				borderElement.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
			}
			List<LayoutProvider> list3 = new List<LayoutProvider>(ChildLayoutProviders.Count);
			foreach (LayoutProvider value2 in ChildLayoutProviders.Values)
			{
				if (!value2.IsInUse)
				{
					list3.Add(value2);
				}
			}
			RemoveUnusedChildLayoutProviders(list3);
			if (IsPrimaryLayoutProvider)
			{
				documentView.ScrollManager.UpdateScrollBarRanges();
			}
			LastLayoutViewportRect = rect;
			LastMeasureSize = availableSize;
			Size result = availableSize;
			result = ((!double.IsNaN(availableSize.Height) && !double.IsPositiveInfinity(availableSize.Height)) ? new Size(availableSize.Width, Math.Min(availableSize.Height, layoutManager.LayoutHeight + 2.0 * Extent.LayoutPaddingY.LogicalPixels)) : new Size(availableSize.Width, layoutManager.LayoutHeight + 2.0 * Extent.LayoutPaddingY.LogicalPixels));
			return result;
		}

		internal void ForceLineContentRebuildOnNextLayoutPass()
		{
			_forceLineContentRebuildOnNextLayoutPass = true;
		}

		private void ArrangeImageOuterContainer(UIElement element, double offsetYContainerAdjustment)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = element as FrameworkElement;
			frameworkElement.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Width, frameworkElement.DesiredSize.Height));
			if (!(frameworkElement.Tag is Tuple<LineElementProxy, LineElementProxy.LineContentProxy> tuple))
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = tuple.Item1.TextAreaOffsetX + tuple.Item2.OffsetX;
			double num2 = tuple.Item1.TextAreaOffsetY + tuple.Item2.OffsetY - offsetYContainerAdjustment;
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = null;
			Transform renderTransform = frameworkElement.RenderTransform;
			if (renderTransform == null)
			{
				translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)(frameworkElement.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform
				{
					X = 0.0,
					Y = 0.0
				});
			}
			else if (renderTransform is TranslateTransform)
			{
				translateTransform = renderTransform as TranslateTransform;
			}
			else if (renderTransform is TransformGroup && renderTransform is TransformGroup transformGroup)
			{
				foreach (Transform child in transformGroup.Children)
				{
					if (child is TranslateTransform)
					{
						translateTransform = child as TranslateTransform;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (translateTransform == null)
				{
					translateTransform = new TranslateTransform
					{
						X = 0.0,
						Y = 0.0
					};
					transformGroup.Children.Add(translateTransform);
				}
			}
			if (translateTransform != null)
			{
				translateTransform.X = num;
				translateTransform.Y = num2;
			}
			else if (renderTransform is MatrixTransform)
			{
				Matrix matrix = ((MatrixTransform)renderTransform).Matrix;
				Matrix matrix2 = new Matrix(matrix.M11, matrix.M12, matrix.M21, matrix.M22, num, num2);
				MatrixTransform matrixTransform = new MatrixTransform();
				matrixTransform.Matrix = matrix2;
				frameworkElement.RenderTransform = matrixTransform;
			}
		}

		private bool CanCreateBordersUsingRectangles(BlockNodeBorders borders)
		{
			if (borders.Left == null || borders.Top == null || borders.Right == null || borders.Bottom == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (borders.Left.BorderType == borders.Top.BorderType && borders.Right.BorderType == borders.Bottom.BorderType && borders.Left.BottomRightComplement == borders.Right.BorderType && borders.Top.BottomRightComplement == borders.Bottom.BorderType && borders.Left.BottomRightComplement != borders.Left.BorderType && borders.Top.BottomRightComplement != borders.Top.BorderType)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ClearBorderElements()
		{
			foreach (FrameworkElement borderElement in BorderElements)
			{
				ContentElementHost.RemoveChild(borderElement);
			}
			BorderElements.Clear();
		}

		private void ClearLayoutBlockHighlightElements()
		{
			foreach (Rectangle layoutBlockHighlightElement in LayoutBlockHighlightElements)
			{
				ContentElementHost.RemoveChild(layoutBlockHighlightElement);
			}
			LayoutBlockHighlightElements.Clear();
		}

		private void CombineBorderElementProxys(List<BorderElementProxy> borderElementProxys, bool isVertical)
		{
			_ = BorderElements;
			_ = ContentElementHost;
			int num = borderElementProxys.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < num && i < num - 1; i++)
			{
				BorderElementProxy borderElementProxy = borderElementProxys[i];
				BorderElementProxy borderElementProxy2 = borderElementProxys[i + 1];
				if (borderElementProxy.Color != borderElementProxy2.Color || borderElementProxy.StrokeThickness != borderElementProxy2.StrokeThickness || borderElementProxy.OtherDimensionOffsetInTextAreaCoords != borderElementProxy2.OtherDimensionOffsetInTextAreaCoords)
				{
					continue;
				}
				double num2 = borderElementProxy.EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords - borderElementProxy2.StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords;
				if (num2 >= 0.0 && num2 <= 2.0)
				{
					double num3 = borderElementProxy2.Length - num2;
					if (isVertical)
					{
						borderElementProxy.Y2 += num3;
						borderElementProxy.EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords += num3;
					}
					else
					{
						borderElementProxy.X2 += num3;
						borderElementProxy.EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords += num3;
					}
					borderElementProxys.RemoveAt(i + 1);
					num--;
					i--;
				}
			}
		}

		private void CombineBorderElementProxysIfPossible()
		{
			_borderElementProxysVertical = (from p in BorderElementProxysVertical
				orderby p.OtherDimensionOffsetInTextAreaCoords, p.StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords
				select p).ToList();
			CombineBorderElementProxys(_borderElementProxysVertical, isVertical: true);
			_borderElementProxysHorizontal = (from p in BorderElementProxysHorizontal
				orderby p.OtherDimensionOffsetInTextAreaCoords, p.StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords
				select p).ToList();
			CombineBorderElementProxys(_borderElementProxysHorizontal, isVertical: false);
		}

		private void CreateBorders(BlockNodeBorders borders, Rect borderRect)
		{
			Rect borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates = new Rect(borderRect.Left, borderRect.Top - DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset, borderRect.Width, borderRect.Height);
			RichTextDocument document = DocumentView.Document;
			if (CanCreateBordersUsingRectangles(borders) && borders.Left.BorderType != RichTextBorderType.None)
			{
				CreateBordersUsingRectangles(borders, borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, document);
				return;
			}
			BorderCornerBevelInfo bevelInfo = new BorderCornerBevelInfo(borders.Left != null && borders.Top != null && ShouldBorderTypesBeBeveled(borders.Left.BorderType, borders.Top.BorderType), borders.Right != null && borders.Top != null && ShouldBorderTypesBeBeveled(borders.Right.BorderType, borders.Top.BorderType), borders.Left != null && borders.Bottom != null && ShouldBorderTypesBeBeveled(borders.Left.BorderType, borders.Bottom.BorderType), borders.Right != null && borders.Bottom != null && ShouldBorderTypesBeBeveled(borders.Right.BorderType, borders.Bottom.BorderType));
			ProcessBorderSide(borders.Left, BorderSide.Left, borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, bevelInfo, borders, document);
			ProcessBorderSide(borders.Top, BorderSide.Top, borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, bevelInfo, borders, document);
			ProcessBorderSide(borders.Right, BorderSide.Right, borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, bevelInfo, borders, document);
			ProcessBorderSide(borders.Bottom, BorderSide.Bottom, borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, bevelInfo, borders, document);
		}

		private void CreateBordersUsingRectangles(BlockNodeBorders borders, Rect borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, RichTextDocument document)
		{
			DoubleCollection dashArray = null;
			double[] lineThickness = new double[0];
			double[] spaceBeforeLine = new double[0];
			ColorInfo[] lineColor = null;
			GetBorderLineMetricsAndColors(borders.Left, out lineThickness, out spaceBeforeLine, out dashArray, out lineColor);
			for (int i = 0; i < lineThickness.Length; i++)
			{
				double num = spaceBeforeLine[i];
				OffsetRect(ref borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, num, num);
				InflateRect(ref borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, 0.0 - num, 0.0 - num);
				Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle();
				rectangle.Width = borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Width;
				rectangle.Height = borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Height;
				rectangle.Fill = TransparentBrush;
				rectangle.StrokeThickness = lineThickness[i];
				rectangle.Stroke = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo(lineColor[i], document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Borders);
				rectangle.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform
				{
					X = borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Left,
					Y = borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Top
				};
				rectangle.UseLayoutRounding = true;
				if (dashArray != null)
				{
					rectangle.StrokeDashArray = dashArray;
				}
				SetIsBorderElementNewlyRealized(rectangle, value: true);
				BorderElements.Add(rectangle);
				double num2 = lineThickness[i];
				OffsetRect(ref borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, num2, num2);
				InflateRect(ref borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, 0.0 - num2, 0.0 - num2);
			}
		}

		private BorderElementProxy CreateBorderElementProxy(double thickness, ColorInfo color, double textAreaOffsetX, double textAreaOffsetY, LineIndents lineIndents, Rect borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, BorderSide borderSide, DoubleCollection dashArray, bool isVertical, bool isHorizontal, RichTextDocument document)
		{
			double x1Indent = lineIndents.X1Indent;
			double x = (isVertical ? 0.0 : (borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Width - lineIndents.X2Indent));
			double y1Indent = lineIndents.Y1Indent;
			double y = (isHorizontal ? 0.0 : (borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Height - lineIndents.Y2Indent));
			Brush brushFromColorInfo = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo(color, document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Borders);
			return new BorderElementProxy(x1Indent, x, y1Indent, y, thickness, color, brushFromColorInfo, dashArray, textAreaOffsetX, textAreaOffsetY, isVertical);
		}

		private void GetBorderLineMetricsAndColors(RichTextBorder border, out double[] lineThickness, out double[] spaceBeforeLine, out DoubleCollection dashArray, out ColorInfo[] lineColor)
		{
			dashArray = null;
			Extent[] lineSegmentExtents = border.LineSegmentExtents;
			int num = lineSegmentExtents.Length;
			lineThickness = new double[num];
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				lineThickness[i] = lineSegmentExtents[i].LogicalPixels;
			}
			Extent[] lineSegmentSpacings = border.LineSegmentSpacings;
			num = lineSegmentSpacings.Length;
			spaceBeforeLine = new double[num];
			for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
			{
				spaceBeforeLine[j] = lineSegmentSpacings[j].LogicalPixels;
			}
			lineColor = border.GetLineSegmentColors(DocumentView.Document.RootNode);
			switch (border.BorderType)
			{
			case RichTextBorderType.Dotted:
				dashArray = new DoubleCollection();
				dashArray.Add(1.0);
				dashArray.Add(3.0);
				break;
			case RichTextBorderType.DotDash:
				dashArray = new DoubleCollection();
				dashArray.Add(1.0);
				dashArray.Add(3.0);
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				break;
			case RichTextBorderType.DotDotDash:
				dashArray = new DoubleCollection();
				dashArray.Add(1.0);
				dashArray.Add(3.0);
				dashArray.Add(1.0);
				dashArray.Add(3.0);
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				break;
			case RichTextBorderType.Dashed:
				dashArray = new DoubleCollection();
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				dashArray.Add(6.0);
				break;
			case RichTextBorderType.DashSmallGap:
				dashArray = new DoubleCollection();
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				break;
			case RichTextBorderType.DashDotStroked:
				dashArray = new DoubleCollection();
				dashArray.Add(4.0);
				dashArray.Add(2.0);
				dashArray.Add(1.0);
				dashArray.Add(2.0);
				break;
			case RichTextBorderType.Double:
			case RichTextBorderType.Triple:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickSmallGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThickThinSmallGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickThinSmallGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickMediumGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThickThinMediumGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickThinMediumGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickLargeGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThickThinLargeGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickThinLargeGap:
			case RichTextBorderType.Wave:
			case RichTextBorderType.DoubleWave:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEmboss:
			case RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEngrave:
			case RichTextBorderType.Outset:
			case RichTextBorderType.Inset:
				break;
			}
		}

		private LineIndents GetBorderLineIndents(BorderSide borderSide, BorderCornerBevelInfo bevelInfo, double indentAmount, BlockNodeBorders borders)
		{
			return borderSide switch
			{
				BorderSide.Left => new LineIndents(0.0, 0.0, bevelInfo.TopLeftCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : ((borders.Top != null) ? borders.Top.BorderThickness.LogicalPixels : 0.0), bevelInfo.BottomLeftCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : ((borders.Bottom != null) ? borders.Bottom.BorderThickness.LogicalPixels : 0.0)), 
				BorderSide.Top => new LineIndents(bevelInfo.TopLeftCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : 0.0, bevelInfo.TopRightCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : 0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 
				BorderSide.Right => new LineIndents(0.0, 0.0, bevelInfo.TopRightCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : ((borders.Top != null) ? borders.Top.BorderThickness.LogicalPixels : 0.0), bevelInfo.BottomRightCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : ((borders.Bottom != null) ? borders.Bottom.BorderThickness.LogicalPixels : 0.0)), 
				BorderSide.Bottom => new LineIndents(bevelInfo.BottomLeftCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : 0.0, bevelInfo.BottomRightCornerNeedsBevel ? indentAmount : 0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 
				_ => new LineIndents(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0), 
			};
		}

		private double GetContentElementHostOffsetXAdjustment()
		{
			if (ContentElementHost is ContentElementClippingHost contentElementClippingHost)
			{
				return contentElementClippingHost.LayoutBlock.OverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		private LayoutProvider GetUltimateParentLayoutProvider()
		{
			if (ParentLayoutProvider == null)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return ParentLayoutProvider.GetUltimateParentLayoutProvider();
		}

		private void InflateRect(ref Rect rect, double width, double height)
		{
			rect = new Rect(rect.X, rect.Y, rect.Width + 2.0 * width, rect.Height + 2.0 * height);
		}

		private void OffsetRect(ref Rect rect, double x, double y)
		{
			rect = new Rect(rect.X + x, rect.Y + y, rect.Width, rect.Height);
		}

		private void ProcessBorderSide(RichTextBorder border, BorderSide borderSide, Rect borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, BorderCornerBevelInfo bevelInfo, BlockNodeBorders borders, RichTextDocument document)
		{
			if (border == null || border.BorderType == RichTextBorderType.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = borderSide == BorderSide.Left || borderSide == BorderSide.Right;
			bool isHorizontal = borderSide == BorderSide.Top || borderSide == BorderSide.Bottom;
			double num = ((borderSide == BorderSide.Right) ? borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Right : borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Left);
			double num2 = ((borderSide == BorderSide.Bottom) ? borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Bottom : borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates.Top);
			DoubleCollection dashArray = null;
			double[] lineThickness = new double[0];
			double[] spaceBeforeLine = new double[0];
			ColorInfo[] lineColor = null;
			GetBorderLineMetricsAndColors(border, out lineThickness, out spaceBeforeLine, out dashArray, out lineColor);
			if (borderSide == BorderSide.Right || borderSide == BorderSide.Bottom)
			{
				Array.Reverse(lineThickness);
				Array.Reverse(lineColor);
			}
			double num3 = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < lineThickness.Length; i++)
			{
				num3 += spaceBeforeLine[i];
				double num4 = ((borderSide == BorderSide.Bottom || borderSide == BorderSide.Right) ? (spaceBeforeLine[i] * -1.0) : spaceBeforeLine[i]);
				if (flag)
				{
					num += num4;
				}
				else
				{
					num2 += num4;
				}
				double num5 = ((borderSide == BorderSide.Bottom || borderSide == BorderSide.Right) ? (lineThickness[i] * -1.0) : lineThickness[i]);
				if (borderSide == BorderSide.Bottom || borderSide == BorderSide.Right)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						num += num5;
					}
					else
					{
						num2 += num5;
					}
				}
				BorderElementProxy item = CreateBorderElementProxy(lineThickness[i], lineColor[i], num, num2, GetBorderLineIndents(borderSide, bevelInfo, num3, borders), borderRectInTextAreaCoordinates, borderSide, dashArray, flag, isHorizontal, document);
				if (flag)
				{
					BorderElementProxysVertical.Add(item);
				}
				else
				{
					BorderElementProxysHorizontal.Add(item);
				}
				if (borderSide == BorderSide.Left || borderSide == BorderSide.Top)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						num += num5;
					}
					else
					{
						num2 += num5;
					}
				}
				num3 += lineThickness[i];
			}
		}

		private void ProcessBlock(ILayoutBlock layoutBlock, Rect viewPortRect, List<Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>> lines)
		{
			Rect value = layoutBlock.OverallRect.Value;
			if (layoutBlock.IsContentClipped)
			{
				Dictionary<ILayoutBlock, LayoutProvider> childLayoutProviders = ChildLayoutProviders;
				LayoutProvider value2 = null;
				childLayoutProviders.TryGetValue(layoutBlock, out value2);
				if (value2 == null)
				{
					ContentElementClippingHost contentElementClippingHost = new ContentElementClippingHost(layoutBlock);
					contentElementClippingHost.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform
					{
						X = 0.0,
						Y = 0.0
					};
					value2 = new LayoutProvider(DocumentView, contentElementClippingHost, LayoutPlane, layoutBlock, this);
					contentElementClippingHost.Initialize(value2);
					childLayoutProviders.Add(layoutBlock, value2);
					ContentElementHost.AddChild(contentElementClippingHost);
				}
				value2.IsInUse = true;
				FrameworkElement obj = value2.ContentElementHost as FrameworkElement;
				obj.Width = value.Width;
				obj.Height = value.Height;
				obj.Clip = new RectangleGeometry
				{
					Rect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, value.Width, value.Height)
				};
				obj.InvalidateMeasure();
				obj.Measure(new Size(value.Width, value.Height));
				return;
			}
			Rect rect = new Rect(value.Left, viewPortRect.Top, value.Width, viewPortRect.Height);
			if (layoutBlock.Borders != null && layoutBlock.BorderRect.HasValue)
			{
				CreateBorders(layoutBlock.Borders, layoutBlock.BorderRect.Value);
			}
			if (layoutBlock.Shading.HasValue)
			{
				Rect? shadingRect = layoutBlock.ShadingRect;
				if (shadingRect.HasValue)
				{
					Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle();
					rectangle.Fill = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo(layoutBlock.Shading.Value.Background, DocumentView.Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Background);
					rectangle.Width = shadingRect.Value.Width;
					rectangle.Height = shadingRect.Value.Height;
					rectangle.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform
					{
						X = shadingRect.Value.X - GetContentElementHostOffsetXAdjustment(),
						Y = shadingRect.Value.Y - DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset - RichTextEditorUtilities.GetContentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment(ContentElementHost, DocumentView)
					};
					SetSpecialElementType(rectangle, SpecialElementType.LayoutBlockHighlight);
					LayoutBlockHighlightElements.Add(rectangle);
				}
			}
			if (layoutBlock.LineCount > 0)
			{
				Shading backgroundShading = AutomaticForegroundColorUtilities.GetBackgroundShading(DocumentView.Document.RootNode, layoutBlock);
				foreach (IRichTextLine line in layoutBlock.GetLines(rect))
				{
					lines.Add(new Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>(layoutBlock, line, backgroundShading));
				}
			}
			if (!layoutBlock.HasChildBlocks)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ILayoutBlock item in layoutBlock.GetChildBlocksInViewPort(rect))
			{
				ProcessBlock(item, rect, lines);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveRectangleBorderElements()
		{
			List<FrameworkElement> borderElements = BorderElements;
			for (int num = borderElements.Count - 1; num > -1; num--)
			{
				FrameworkElement frameworkElement = borderElements[num];
				if (frameworkElement is Rectangle)
				{
					borderElements.RemoveAt(num);
					ContentElementHost.RemoveChild(frameworkElement);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RemoveUnusedChildLayoutProviders(List<LayoutProvider> unusedLayoutProviders)
		{
			foreach (LayoutProvider unusedLayoutProvider in unusedLayoutProviders)
			{
				ChildLayoutProviders.Remove(unusedLayoutProvider.AssociatedLayoutBlock);
				ContentElementHost.RemoveChild(unusedLayoutProvider.ContentElementHost as DependencyObject);
				unusedLayoutProvider.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldBorderTypesBeBeveled(RichTextBorderType borderType1, RichTextBorderType borderType2)
		{
			switch (borderType1)
			{
			case RichTextBorderType.ThickThinLargeGap:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThickThinLargeGap)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThinThickLargeGap;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThickThinMediumGap:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThickThinMediumGap)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThinThickMediumGap;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThickThinSmallGap:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThickThinSmallGap)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThinThickSmallGap;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickLargeGap:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThinThickLargeGap)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThickThinLargeGap;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickMediumGap:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThinThickMediumGap)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThickThinMediumGap;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThinThickSmallGap:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThinThickSmallGap)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThickThinSmallGap;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEmboss:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEmboss)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEngrave;
				}
				return true;
			case RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEngrave:
				if (borderType2 != RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEngrave)
				{
					return borderType2 == RichTextBorderType.ThreeDEmboss;
				}
				return true;
			default:
				return borderType1 == borderType2;
			}
		}

		private Point TransformOverallPointToLocalCoordinates(Point pointInOverallCoordinates)
		{
			return TransformTextAreaPointToLocalCoordinates(new Point(pointInOverallCoordinates.X, pointInOverallCoordinates.Y - DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset));
		}

		private double TransformTextAreaOffsetXToLocalCoordinates(double textAreaOffsetX)
		{
			return ContainingAdornmentLayerTransform.Transform(new Point(textAreaOffsetX, 0.0)).X;
		}

		private double TransformTextAreaOffsetYToLocalCoordinates(double textAreaOffsetY)
		{
			return ContainingAdornmentLayerTransform.Transform(new Point(0.0, textAreaOffsetY)).Y;
		}

		private double TransformOverallOffsetXToLocalCoordinates(double overallOffsetX)
		{
			return ContainingAdornmentLayerTransform.Transform(new Point(overallOffsetX, 0.0)).X;
		}

		private double TransformOverallOffsetYToLocalCoordinates(double overallOffsetY)
		{
			return ContainingAdornmentLayerTransform.Transform(new Point(0.0, overallOffsetY - DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset)).Y;
		}

		private Point TransformTextAreaPointToLocalCoordinates(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return ContainingAdornmentLayerTransform.Transform(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			ClearLayoutBlockHighlightElements();
			ClearBorderElements();
			_visibleLines.Clear();
			ContentElementHost.ClearChildren();
			RemoveUnusedChildLayoutProviders(ChildLayoutProviders.Values.ToList());
		}
	}
	internal class BorderElementProxy
	{
		internal double StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords { get; private set; }

		internal double EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords { get; set; }

		internal double OtherDimensionOffsetInTextAreaCoords { get; private set; }

		internal double Length { get; private set; }

		internal ColorInfo Color { get; private set; }

		internal double X1InTextAreaCoords => X1 + TextAreaOffsetX;

		internal double X2InTextAreaCoords => X2 + TextAreaOffsetX;

		internal double Y1InTextAreaCoords => Y1 + TextAreaOffsetY;

		internal double Y2InTextAreaCoords => Y2 + TextAreaOffsetY;

		internal double X1 { get; set; }

		internal double X2 { get; set; }

		internal double Y1 { get; set; }

		internal double Y2 { get; set; }

		internal double StrokeThickness { get; set; }

		internal Brush Stroke { get; set; }

		internal DoubleCollection StrokeDashArray { get; set; }

		internal double TextAreaOffsetX { get; private set; }

		internal double TextAreaOffsetY { get; private set; }

		internal bool IsVertical { get; private set; }

		internal BorderElementProxy(double x1, double x2, double y1, double y2, double strokeThickness, ColorInfo color, Brush stroke, DoubleCollection dashArray, double textAreaOffsetX, double textAreaOffsetY, bool isVertical)
		{
			X1 = x1;
			X2 = x2;
			Y1 = y1;
			Y2 = y2;
			StrokeThickness = strokeThickness;
			Stroke = stroke;
			StrokeDashArray = dashArray;
			StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords = (isVertical ? y1 : x1);
			StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords += (isVertical ? textAreaOffsetY : textAreaOffsetX);
			EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords = (isVertical ? y2 : x2);
			EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords += (isVertical ? textAreaOffsetY : textAreaOffsetX);
			OtherDimensionOffsetInTextAreaCoords = (isVertical ? (x1 + textAreaOffsetX) : (y1 + textAreaOffsetY));
			Length = EndOffsetInTextAreaCoords - StartOffsetInTextAreaCoords;
			Color = color;
			TextAreaOffsetX = textAreaOffsetX;
			TextAreaOffsetY = textAreaOffsetY;
			IsVertical = isVertical;
		}
	}
	internal class LineElementGenerator : IDisposable
	{
		private Dictionary<IRichTextLine, LineElementProxy> _lineElementPoolDictionary;

		private List<LineElementProxy> _lineElementPoolList;

		private List<Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>> _iRichTextLines;

		private List<LineElementProxy> _visibleLines;

		private int _totalNewlyRealizedElementsGenerated;

		private int _totalElementsReusedAsIs;

		private int _totalElementsReusedWithNewContents;

		private IContentElementHost ContentElementHost { get; set; }

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; set; }

		internal LineElementGenerator(RichDocumentView documentView, List<Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>> iRichTextLines, List<LineElementProxy> visibleLines, IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(contentElementHost, "contentElementHost");
			_iRichTextLines = iRichTextLines;
			_visibleLines = visibleLines;
			ContentElementHost = contentElementHost;
			DocumentView = documentView;
			InitializeElementPool();
		}

		internal void GenerateElements(Rect viewPortRect, bool forceLineContentRebuild)
		{
			List<Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading>> iRichTextLines = _iRichTextLines;
			Dictionary<IRichTextLine, LineElementProxy> lineElementPoolDictionary = _lineElementPoolDictionary;
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (!forceLineContentRebuild)
			{
				for (int num = iRichTextLines.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading> tuple = iRichTextLines[num];
					if (lineElementPoolDictionary.TryGetValue(tuple.Item2, out var value))
					{
						iRichTextLines.RemoveAt(num);
						lineElementPoolDictionary.Remove(tuple.Item2);
						_totalElementsReusedAsIs++;
						value.ResetCachedValues();
						value.RefreshUnderlineAndStrikeThroughElements();
						value.RefreshLayoutBlockResolvedShading(tuple.Item3);
						_visibleLines.Add(value);
					}
				}
			}
			_lineElementPoolList = new List<LineElementProxy>(_lineElementPoolDictionary.Values);
			List<LineElementProxy> lineElementPoolList = _lineElementPoolList;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < iRichTextLines.Count; i++)
			{
				Tuple<ILayoutBlock, IRichTextLine, Shading> tuple2 = iRichTextLines[i];
				LineElementProxy value;
				if (lineElementPoolList.Count > 0)
				{
					flag = false;
					value = _lineElementPoolList[0];
					lineElementPoolList.RemoveAt(0);
					value.SetupForReuse(tuple2.Item1, tuple2.Item2, tuple2.Item3, documentView, ContentElementHost);
					_totalElementsReusedWithNewContents++;
				}
				else
				{
					value = new LineElementProxy(tuple2.Item1, tuple2.Item2, tuple2.Item3, documentView, ContentElementHost);
					flag = true;
					_totalNewlyRealizedElementsGenerated++;
				}
				double layoutWidth = documentView.LayoutManager.LayoutWidth;
				bool flag2 = documentView.RichTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode == HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DoNotDisplay;
				ILayoutBlock item = tuple2.Item1;
				double offset = Math.Max(0.0, (item != null && item.ContentRect.HasValue) ? (viewPortRect.Left - item.ContentRect.Value.Left) : viewPortRect.Left);
				foreach (RichTextLineContent item2 in tuple2.Item2.GetLineContent(offset, layoutWidth))
				{
					if (!(item2.IsParagraphMark && flag2) && (item2.Text != null || item2.TrailingSpaces > 0 || item2.Content is ImageLayoutNode))
					{
						value.AddLineContent(item2);
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					ContentElementHost.AddChild(value.Element);
				}
				value.FinalizeContent();
				_visibleLines.Add(value);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeElementPool()
		{
			_lineElementPoolDictionary = new Dictionary<IRichTextLine, LineElementProxy>();
			foreach (Visual lineVisual in ContentElementHost.GetLineVisuals())
			{
				if (lineVisual is DocumentViewLineVisual documentViewLineVisual)
				{
					_lineElementPoolDictionary.Add(documentViewLineVisual.LineElementProxy.RichTextLine, documentViewLineVisual.LineElementProxy);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			foreach (LineElementProxy lineElementPool in _lineElementPoolList)
			{
				lineElementPool.Dispose(ContentElementHost);
			}
			_lineElementPoolList.Clear();
			_lineElementPoolList = null;
		}
	}
	internal class LineElementProxy
	{
		internal class LineContentProxy
		{
			internal string Text => TextBuilder.ToString();

			internal ColorInfo Color { get; private set; }

			internal double ContentWidth { get; private set; }

			internal HighlightColor? HighlightColor { get; private set; }

			internal Shading? Shading { get; private set; }

			internal FontSpecification FontSpecification { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsJustified { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsNewLine { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsParagraphMark { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsNewLineOrParagraphMark { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsImage { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsTab { get; private set; }

			internal bool IsInListNumberGroup { get; private set; }

			internal double OffsetX { get; private set; }

			internal double OffsetY { get; private set; }

			internal double OverallWidth { get; set; }

			internal double TrailingSpacesWidth { get; set; }

			internal double Height { get; private set; }

			internal UnderlineType? UnderlineType { get; private set; }

			internal ColorInfo UnderlineColor { get; private set; }

			internal bool? StrikeThrough { get; private set; }

			internal bool? DoubleStrikeThrough { get; private set; }

			internal object Content { get; private set; }

			internal bool ProcessedForUnderlineLength { get; set; }

			internal bool ProcessedForStrikethroughLength { get; set; }

			private StringBuilder TextBuilder { get; set; }

			internal LineContentProxy(RichTextLineContent lineContent)
			{
				TextBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				TextBuilder.Append((lineContent.ContentType == RichTextLineContentType.Tab) ? "" : lineContent.Text);
				TextBuilder.Append("".PadRight(lineContent.TrailingSpaces));
				CharacterSettings characterSettings = lineContent.CharacterSettings;
				Color = characterSettings.Color;
				HighlightColor = characterSettings.HighlightColor;
				Shading = characterSettings.Shading;
				FontSpecification = lineContent.Font;
				OffsetX = lineContent.RelativeOffsetX.LogicalPixels;
				OffsetY = lineContent.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels;
				OverallWidth = lineContent.OverallWidth.LogicalPixels;
				ContentWidth = lineContent.ContentWidth.LogicalPixels;
				TrailingSpacesWidth = lineContent.TrailingSpaceWidth.LogicalPixels;
				Height = lineContent.Height.LogicalPixels;
				IsJustified = lineContent.IsJustified;
				IsNewLine = lineContent.IsNewLine;
				IsParagraphMark = lineContent.IsParagraphMark;
				IsNewLineOrParagraphMark = lineContent.IsNewLineOrParagraphMark;
				IsTab = lineContent.IsTab;
				IsInListNumberGroup = lineContent.IsInListNumberGroup;
				IsImage = lineContent.Content is ImageLayoutNode;
				UnderlineType = characterSettings.UnderlineType;
				UnderlineColor = characterSettings.UnderlineColor;
				StrikeThrough = characterSettings.StrikeThrough;
				DoubleStrikeThrough = characterSettings.DoubleStrikeThrough;
				Content = lineContent.Content;
			}

			internal bool IsCompatibleWith(RichTextLineContent lineContent)
			{
				CharacterSettings characterSettings = lineContent.CharacterSettings;
				if (RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(OffsetX + OverallWidth, lineContent.RelativeOffsetX.LogicalPixels) && Color == characterSettings.Color && HighlightColor == characterSettings.HighlightColor)
				{
					Shading? shading = Shading;
					Shading? shading2 = characterSettings.Shading;
					if (shading.HasValue == shading2.HasValue && (!shading.HasValue || shading.GetValueOrDefault() == shading2.GetValueOrDefault()) && FontSpecification == lineContent.Font)
					{
						UnderlineType? underlineType = UnderlineType;
						UnderlineType? underlineType2 = characterSettings.UnderlineType;
						if (underlineType.GetValueOrDefault() == underlineType2.GetValueOrDefault() && underlineType.HasValue == underlineType2.HasValue && UnderlineColor == characterSettings.UnderlineColor && IsParagraphMark == lineContent.IsParagraphMark && IsNewLine == lineContent.IsNewLine && !lineContent.IsTab && !IsTab && !lineContent.IsJustified && !lineContent.IsInListNumberGroup && !IsInListNumberGroup && !(lineContent.Content is ImageLayoutNode) && !IsImage)
						{
							string text = lineContent.Text;
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								TextBuilder.Append(text);
							}
							TextBuilder.Append("".PadRight(lineContent.TrailingSpaces));
							double logicalPixels = lineContent.OverallWidth.LogicalPixels;
							OverallWidth += logicalPixels;
							ContentWidth = OverallWidth - (logicalPixels - lineContent.ContentWidth.LogicalPixels);
							TrailingSpacesWidth += lineContent.TrailingSpaceWidth.LogicalPixels;
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private DocumentViewLineVisual _element;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static WeakReference s_textDecorationsCache;

		private List<LineContentProxy> _lineContents;

		internal List<Line> _underlineAndStrikethroughElements;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static SolidColorBrush s_searchHighlightsBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, 220, 0));

		private Dictionary<ImageLayoutNode, ImageContainer> _imageElementMap;

		private List<ImageLayoutNodeBinder> _imageLayoutNodeBinders;

		private double? _smallestContentItemOffsetY;

		private double? _textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime;

		internal Rect BoundsInLayoutCoordinates => new Rect(LayoutOffsetX, LayoutOffsetY, OverallWidth, OverallHeight);

		internal Rect BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates => new Rect(TextAreaOffsetX, TextAreaOffsetY, RichTextLine.Width.LogicalPixels, RichTextLine.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels);

		internal int CachedOffsetForSelectionAdornmentGenerator { get; set; }

		internal IContentElementHost ContentElementHost { get; private set; }

		internal double ContentHeight => RichTextLine.ContentHeight.LogicalPixels;

		internal RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; private set; }

		internal DependencyObject Element => _element;

		internal double FirstContentItemOffsetY
		{
			get
			{
				List<LineContentProxy> lineContents = LineContents;
				if (lineContents.Count > 0)
				{
					return lineContents[0].OffsetY;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
		}

		internal double FirstLineContentOffsetX
		{
			get
			{
				if (LineContents.Count < 1)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return LineContents[0].OffsetX;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasContent => RichTextLine.HasContent;

		internal bool IsLayoutBlockZombied
		{
			get
			{
				if (LayoutBlock is LayoutNodeBase layoutNodeBase)
				{
					return layoutNodeBase.DocumentNode == null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal ILayoutBlock LayoutBlock { get; private set; }

		internal Shading LayoutBlockResolvedShading { get; private set; }

		internal double LayoutOffsetX
		{
			get
			{
				if (LayoutBlock.ParentBlock != null && LayoutBlock.ParentBlock.IsContentClipped)
				{
					return ((ParagraphLayoutNode)LayoutBlock).RelativeOffsetX.LogicalPixels;
				}
				return LayoutBlock.ContentOverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels;
			}
		}

		internal double LayoutOffsetY
		{
			get
			{
				if (LayoutBlock.ParentBlock != null && LayoutBlock.ParentBlock.IsContentClipped)
				{
					return DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset + RichTextLine.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels;
				}
				return LayoutBlock.ContentOverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels + RichTextLine.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels;
			}
		}

		internal double OverallHeight => RichTextLine.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels;

		internal double OverallWidth => RichTextLine.Width.LogicalPixels + TextAreaOffsetX;

		internal XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; private set; }

		internal IRichTextLine RichTextLine { get; private set; }

		internal double SmallestContentItemOffsetY
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_smallestContentItemOffsetY.HasValue)
				{
					double num = double.MaxValue;
					foreach (LineContentProxy lineContent in LineContents)
					{
						if (!lineContent.IsImage)
						{
							num = Math.Min(num, lineContent.OffsetY);
						}
					}
					if (num != double.MaxValue)
					{
						_smallestContentItemOffsetY = num;
					}
					else
					{
						_smallestContentItemOffsetY = 0.0;
					}
				}
				return _smallestContentItemOffsetY.Value;
			}
		}

		internal double TextAreaOffsetX => LayoutOffsetX;

		internal double TextAreaOffsetY
		{
			get
			{
				if (ContentElementHost is ContentElementClippingHost)
				{
					return RichTextLine.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels + LayoutBlock.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels - DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset;
				}
				return LayoutOffsetY - DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset;
			}
		}

		internal double TextAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime.HasValue)
				{
					_textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime = TextAreaOffsetY;
				}
				return _textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime.Value;
			}
		}

				return _imageElementMap;

				return _imageLayoutNodeBinders;
			}
		}

		private List<LineContentProxy> LineContents
		{
			get

				if (_lineContents == null)

					_lineContents = new List<LineContentProxy>(30);

				return _lineContents;
			}
		}

		private DocumentViewLineVisual LineVisual => _element;

		private static Dictionary<string, TextDecorationCollection> TextDecorationsCache
		{
			get
			{
				if (s_textDecorationsCache == null || !s_textDecorationsCache.IsAlive)
				{
					s_textDecorationsCache = new WeakReference(new Dictionary<string, TextDecorationCollection>(5));
				}
				return CoreUtilities.GetWeakReferenceTargetSafe(s_textDecorationsCache) as Dictionary<string, TextDecorationCollection>;
			}
		}

		internal LineElementProxy(ILayoutBlock layoutBlock, IRichTextLine richTextLine, Shading layoutBlockResolvedShading, RichDocumentView documentView, IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutBlock, "layoutBlock");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(richTextLine, "richTextLine");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutBlockResolvedShading, "layoutBlockResolvedShading");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			LayoutBlock = layoutBlock;
			RichTextLine = richTextLine;
			LayoutBlockResolvedShading = layoutBlockResolvedShading;
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			ContentElementHost = contentElementHost;
			Init();
		}

		internal void AddLineContent(RichTextLineContent lineContent)
		{
			int count = LineContents.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				LineContents.Add(new LineContentProxy(lineContent));
			}
			else if (!LineContents[count - 1].IsCompatibleWith(lineContent))
			{
				LineContents.Add(new LineContentProxy(lineContent));
			}
		}

		internal void Dispose(IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			CleanupImageElements(contentElementHost);
			contentElementHost.RemoveChild(Element);
		}

		internal void FinalizeContent()
		{
			List<LineContentProxy> lineContents = LineContents;
			List<LineContentProxy> list = new List<LineContentProxy>(2);
			IContentElementHost contentElementHost = ContentElementHost;
			DrawingContext drawingContext = LineVisual.RenderOpen();
			int count = lineContents.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				LineContentProxy lineContentProxy = lineContents[i];
				Brush brush = null;
				if (!lineContentProxy.IsNewLineOrParagraphMark)
				{
					brush = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromHighlightColor(lineContentProxy.HighlightColor);
				}
				if (brush == null)
				{
					brush = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromShading(lineContentProxy.Shading, DocumentView.Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Background);
				}
				if (brush != null)
				{
					drawingContext.DrawRectangle(brush, null, new Rect(lineContentProxy.OffsetX, 0.0, lineContentProxy.ContentWidth, ContentHeight));
				}
			}
			ProcessSearchHighlights(contentElementHost, drawingContext);
			for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
			{
				LineContentProxy lineContentProxy2 = lineContents[j];
				if (lineContentProxy2.IsImage)
				{
					list.Add(lineContentProxy2);
				}
				if (!lineContentProxy2.IsImage)
				{
					FormattedText formattedTextHelper = GetFormattedTextHelper(lineContents, j, lineContentProxy2);
					drawingContext.DrawText(formattedTextHelper, new Point(lineContentProxy2.OffsetX, lineContentProxy2.OffsetY));
				}
				AddUnderlineAndStrikeThroughElements(lineContents, j, contentElementHost, lineContentProxy2);
			}
			drawingContext.Close();
			ProcessImageContent(contentElementHost, list);
		}

		internal bool GetDoesLineContainDocumentOffset(int documentOffset)
		{
			if (documentOffset >= GetLineStartDocumentOffset(includeListNumbers: true))
			{
				return documentOffset <= GetLineEndDocumentOffset();
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool GetDoesLineContainTextAreaOffsetY(double textAreaOffsetY)
		{
			double textAreaOffsetY2 = TextAreaOffsetY;
			textAreaOffsetY2 = (RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(textAreaOffsetY, textAreaOffsetY2) ? textAreaOffsetY : textAreaOffsetY2);
			double num = TextAreaOffsetY + RichTextLine.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels;
			num = (RichTextEditorUtilities.DoublesAreClose(textAreaOffsetY, num) ? textAreaOffsetY : num);
			if (textAreaOffsetY >= textAreaOffsetY2)
			{
				return textAreaOffsetY < num;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal Size GetElementSize()
		{
			return new Size(RichTextLine.Width.LogicalPixels, RichTextLine.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels);
		}

		internal int GetLineEndDocumentOffset(bool includeListNumbers = false, bool includeNewLine = false)
		{
			if (LayoutBlock is ILayoutNodeWithLines layoutNodeWithLines)
			{
				return GetLineStartDocumentOffset() + layoutNodeWithLines.GetDocumentOffsetCountOfLine(RichTextLine, includeListNumbers, includeNewLine);
			}
			return GetLineStartDocumentOffset();
		}

		internal int GetLineStartDocumentOffset(bool includeListNumbers = false, bool includeParagraphMarks = false)
		{
			int? num = null;
			if (LayoutBlock is ILayoutNodeWithLines layoutNodeWithLines)
			{
				num = layoutNodeWithLines.GetDocumentOffsetOfLine(RichTextLine, includeListNumbers);
				if (num.HasValue)
				{
					return num.Value;
				}
			}
			num = DocumentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(new Point(TextAreaOffsetX + FirstLineContentOffsetX, TextAreaOffsetY + 1.0), includeListNumbers, includeParagraphMarks);
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				return num.Value;
			}
			return 0;
		}

		internal double GetTextAreaOffsetYResolved()
		{
			if (ContentElementHost is ContentElementClippingHost)
			{
				double contentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetContentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment(ContentElementHost, DocumentView, subtractVerticalViewOffset: false);
				return RichTextLine.RelativeOffsetY.LogicalPixels + LayoutBlock.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels - contentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment;
			}
			return TextAreaOffsetY;
		}

		private void ProcessImageContent(IContentElementHost contentElementHost, List<LineContentProxy> lineImageContents)
		{
			foreach (LineContentProxy lineImageContent in lineImageContents)
			{
				ImageLayoutNode imageLayoutNode = lineImageContent.Content as ImageLayoutNode;
				Image image = new Image();
				image.Stretch = Stretch.None;
				image.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
				image.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
				ImageContainer imageContainer = new ImageContainer(clipToBounds: true);
				imageContainer.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
				imageContainer.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
				imageContainer.Children.Add(image);
				ImageContainer imageContainer2 = new ImageContainer(clipToBounds: false);
				imageContainer2.Tag = new Tuple<LineElementProxy, LineContentProxy>(this, lineImageContent);
				imageContainer2.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform
				{
					X = 0.0,
					Y = 0.0
				};
				imageContainer2.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
				imageContainer2.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
				imageContainer2.Children.Add(imageContainer);
				LayoutProvider.SetSpecialElementType(imageContainer2, LayoutProvider.SpecialElementType.ImageOuterContainer);
				contentElementHost.AddChild(imageContainer2);
				ImageElementMap.Add(imageLayoutNode, imageContainer2);
				ImageLayoutNodeBinders.Add(new ImageLayoutNodeBinder(DocumentView, imageLayoutNode, imageContainer, imageContainer2, image));
				imageContainer2.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
			}
		}

		internal void ProcessSearchHighlights(IContentElementHost contentElementHost, DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			List<FindMatch> highlightMatches = RichTextEditor.FindReplaceManager.HighlightMatches;
			if (highlightMatches.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int lineStartDocumentOffset = GetLineStartDocumentOffset(includeListNumbers: true);
			int lineEndDocumentOffset = GetLineEndDocumentOffset();
			foreach (FindMatch item in highlightMatches)
			{
				if ((int)item.Span.End < lineStartDocumentOffset)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if ((int)item.Span.Start >= lineEndDocumentOffset)
				{
					break;
				}
				int num = 0;
				num = (((int)item.Span.Start >= lineStartDocumentOffset) ? ((int)item.Span.Start) : lineStartDocumentOffset);
				int num2 = 0;
				bool flag = false;
				if ((int)item.Span.End >= lineEndDocumentOffset)
				{
					num2 = lineEndDocumentOffset;
					flag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					num2 = item.Span.End;
				}
				RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
				Point? point = documentView.TextAreaPointFromDocumentOffset(num);
				Point? point2 = null;
				if (flag)
				{
					Rect? lineBounds = LayoutBlock.GetLineBounds(RichTextLine, includeParagraphMarks: false);
					if (lineBounds.HasValue)
					{
						point2 = new Point(lineBounds.Value.Right, lineBounds.Value.Top);
					}
				}
				if (!point2.HasValue)
				{
					point2 = documentView.TextAreaPointFromDocumentOffset(num2);
				}
				if (point.HasValue && point2.HasValue)
				{
					Point point3 = new Point(point.Value.X, TextAreaOffsetY);
					Point point4 = new Point(point2.Value.X, TextAreaOffsetY + RichTextLine.OverallHeight.LogicalPixels - 2.0);
					drawingContext.DrawRectangle(s_searchHighlightsBrush, null, new Rect(point3.X - TextAreaOffsetX, 0.0, point4.X - point3.X, point4.Y - point3.Y));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void RefreshLayoutBlockResolvedShading(Shading layoutBlockResolvedShading)
		{
			LayoutBlockResolvedShading = layoutBlockResolvedShading;
		}

		internal void RefreshUnderlineAndStrikeThroughElements()
		{
			List<LineContentProxy> lineContents = LineContents;
			int count = lineContents.Count;
			IContentElementHost contentElementHost = ContentElementHost;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				LineContentProxy lineContentProxy = lineContents[i];
				lineContentProxy.ProcessedForStrikethroughLength = false;
				lineContentProxy.ProcessedForUnderlineLength = false;
				AddUnderlineAndStrikeThroughElements(lineContents, i, contentElementHost, lineContentProxy);
			}
		}

		internal void ResetCachedValues()
		{
			_textAreaOffsetYAtGenerationTime = null;
		}

		internal void SetupForReuse(ILayoutBlock layoutBlock, IRichTextLine richTextLine, Shading layoutBlockResolvedShading, RichDocumentView documentView, IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutBlock, "layoutBlock");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(richTextLine, "richTextLine");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutBlockResolvedShading, "layoutBlockResolvedShading");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			LayoutBlock = layoutBlock;
			RichTextLine = richTextLine;
			LayoutBlockResolvedShading = layoutBlockResolvedShading;
			DocumentView = documentView;
			ContentElementHost = contentElementHost;
			_smallestContentItemOffsetY = null;
			LineContents.Clear();
			ResetCachedValues();
			CleanupImageElements(contentElementHost);
		}

		private void CleanupImageElements(IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			foreach (ImageContainer value in ImageElementMap.Values)
			{
				contentElementHost.RemoveChild(value);
			}
			ImageElementMap.Clear();
			foreach (ImageLayoutNodeBinder imageLayoutNodeBinder in ImageLayoutNodeBinders)
			{
				imageLayoutNodeBinder.Dispose();
			}
			ImageLayoutNodeBinders.Clear();
		}

		private FormattedText GetFormattedTextHelper(List<LineContentProxy> lineContents, int currentLineContentsIndex, LineContentProxy currentLineContent)
		{
			FontSpecification fontSpecification = currentLineContent.FontSpecification;
			bool flag = DocumentView.RichTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode == HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DisplayAllHiddenSymbols;
			bool flag2 = currentLineContent.IsTab && flag;
			FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(flag2 ? "➞" : currentLineContent.Text, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, GetTypefaceFromFontSpecification(fontSpecification, flag2), flag2 ? 12.0 : fontSpecification.FontSize.LogicalPixels, XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo(currentLineContent.Color, DocumentView.Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Foreground, LayoutBlockResolvedShading, currentLineContent.Shading));
			if (!flag2)
			{
				formattedText.SetFontStyle(RichTextEditorUtilities.FontStyleFromIsItalic(fontSpecification.Italic));
				formattedText.SetFontWeight(RichTextEditorUtilities.FontWeightFromIsBold(fontSpecification.Bold));
			}
			return formattedText;
		}

		private bool GetIsContentJustified(List<LineContentProxy> lineContents, int currentLineContentsIndex, LineContentProxy currentLineContent)
		{
			if (currentLineContent.IsJustified)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (currentLineContentsIndex > lineContents.Count - 2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return lineContents[currentLineContentsIndex + 1].IsJustified;
		}

		private Typeface GetTypefaceFromFontSpecification(FontSpecification fontSpecification, bool displayingTab)
		{
			return new Typeface(new FontFamily(displayingTab ? "Calibri" : fontSpecification.FontName), RichTextEditorUtilities.FontStyleFromIsItalic(!displayingTab && fontSpecification.Italic), RichTextEditorUtilities.FontWeightFromIsBold(!displayingTab && fontSpecification.Bold), FontStretches.Normal);
		}

		private void SetTextDecorations(FormattedText formattedText, LineContentProxy lineContentProxy)
		{
			if (lineContentProxy.IsNewLineOrParagraphMark)
			{
				return;
			}
			TextDecorationCollection textDecorationCollection = null;
			TextDecorationCollection textDecorationCollection2 = null;
			if (lineContentProxy.StrikeThrough.HasValue || lineContentProxy.DoubleStrikeThrough.HasValue)
			{
				if (true == lineContentProxy.StrikeThrough)
				{
					textDecorationCollection = TextDecorations.Strikethrough;
				}
				else if (true == lineContentProxy.DoubleStrikeThrough)
				{
					Brush brushFromColorInfo = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo(lineContentProxy.Color, DocumentView.Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Foreground, LayoutBlockResolvedShading, lineContentProxy.Shading);
					string key = "1" + brushFromColorInfo.ToString();
					if (TextDecorationsCache.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						textDecorationCollection = TextDecorationsCache[key];
					}
					else
					{
						textDecorationCollection = new TextDecorationCollection();
						Pen pen = new Pen(brushFromColorInfo, 1.0);
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Strikethrough, pen, -1.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection.Add(value);
						value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Strikethrough, pen, 1.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection.Add(value);
						TextDecorationsCache.Add(key, textDecorationCollection);
					}
				}
			}
			if (lineContentProxy.UnderlineType.HasValue && lineContentProxy.UnderlineType.Value != UnderlineType.None)
			{
				string underlineTypeNumber = GetUnderlineTypeNumber(lineContentProxy.UnderlineType.Value);
				Brush brushFromColorInfo = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo((null != lineContentProxy.UnderlineColor && lineContentProxy.UnderlineColor.Color.HasValue) ? lineContentProxy.UnderlineColor : lineContentProxy.Color, DocumentView.Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Foreground, LayoutBlockResolvedShading, lineContentProxy.Shading);
				string key = underlineTypeNumber + brushFromColorInfo.ToString();
				if (TextDecorationsCache.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					textDecorationCollection2 = TextDecorationsCache[key];
				}
				else
				{
					textDecorationCollection2 = new TextDecorationCollection();
					Pen pen = new Pen(brushFromColorInfo, 1.0);
					switch (underlineTypeNumber)
					{
					case "2":
					case "4":
					{
						if (underlineTypeNumber == "4")
						{
							pen.Thickness = 2.0;
						}
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 0.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					case "3":
					{
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, -1.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 1.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					case "5":
					case "6":
					{
						if (underlineTypeNumber == "6")
						{
							pen.Thickness = 2.0;
						}
						pen.DashStyle = DashStyles.Dot;
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 0.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					case "7":
					case "8":
					{
						if (underlineTypeNumber == "8")
						{
							pen.Thickness = 2.0;
						}
						pen.DashStyle = DashStyles.Dash;
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 0.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					case "9":
					case "10":
					{
						if (underlineTypeNumber == "10")
						{
							pen.Thickness = 2.0;
						}
						DashStyle dashStyle = new DashStyle(new double[2] { 3.0, 2.0 }, 0.0);
						pen.DashStyle = dashStyle;
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 0.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					case "11":
					case "12":
					{
						if (underlineTypeNumber == "12")
						{
							pen.Thickness = 2.0;
						}
						pen.DashStyle = DashStyles.DashDot;
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 0.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					case "13":
					case "14":
					{
						if (underlineTypeNumber == "14")
						{
							pen.Thickness = 2.0;
						}
						pen.DashStyle = DashStyles.DashDotDot;
						TextDecoration value = new TextDecoration(TextDecorationLocation.Underline, pen, 0.0, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel, TextDecorationUnit.Pixel);
						textDecorationCollection2.Add(value);
						break;
					}
					}
					TextDecorationsCache.Add(key, textDecorationCollection2);
				}
			}
			if ((textDecorationCollection != null && textDecorationCollection.Count > 0) || (textDecorationCollection2 != null && textDecorationCollection2.Count > 0))
			{
				TextDecorationCollection textDecorationCollection3 = new TextDecorationCollection();
				if (textDecorationCollection != null)
				{
					textDecorationCollection3.Add(textDecorationCollection);
				}
				if (textDecorationCollection2 != null)
				{
					textDecorationCollection3.Add(textDecorationCollection2);
				}
				formattedText.SetTextDecorations(textDecorationCollection3);
			}
		}

		private void AddUnderlineAndStrikeThroughElements(List<LineContentProxy> lineContents, int currentLineContentsIndex, IContentElementHost contentElementHost, LineContentProxy currentLineContent)
		{
			if (currentLineContent.IsNewLineOrParagraphMark)
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = 1.0;
			double num2 = 2.0;
			double logicalPixels = currentLineContent.FontSpecification.FontSize.LogicalPixels;
			if (logicalPixels <= 6.0)
			{
				num2 = 1.5;
			}
			else if (logicalPixels > 28.0)
			{
				num = 2.0;
				num2 = 3.0;
			}
			else if (logicalPixels > 18.0)
			{
				num = 1.5;
				num2 = 2.5;
			}
			if (currentLineContent.StrikeThrough.HasValue || currentLineContent.DoubleStrikeThrough.HasValue)
			{
				double lineLength = GetLineLength(lineContents, currentLineContentsIndex, isForUnderline: false, currentLineContent);
				if (lineLength > 0.0)
				{
					double num3 = currentLineContent.OffsetY + currentLineContent.Height * 0.55;
					double num4 = num;
					double num5 = num4 + 0.25;
					if (currentLineContent.FontSpecification.Bold)
					{
						num4 = num2;
						num5 = num4 + 0.25;
					}
					if (true == currentLineContent.StrikeThrough)
					{
						CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: false, currentLineContent, num3, lineLength, num4, contentElementHost, null);
					}
					else if (true == currentLineContent.DoubleStrikeThrough)
					{
						CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: false, currentLineContent, num3 - num5, lineLength, num4, contentElementHost, null);
						CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: false, currentLineContent, num3 + num5, lineLength, num4, contentElementHost, null);
					}
				}
			}
			if (!currentLineContent.UnderlineType.HasValue || currentLineContent.UnderlineType.Value == UnderlineType.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			string underlineTypeNumber = GetUnderlineTypeNumber(currentLineContent.UnderlineType.Value);
			double lineLength2 = GetLineLength(lineContents, currentLineContentsIndex, isForUnderline: true, currentLineContent);
			if (!(lineLength2 > 0.0))
			{
				return;
			}
			double offsetY = currentLineContent.OffsetY;
			offsetY = ((!currentLineContent.IsImage) ? (offsetY + currentLineContent.Height * 0.87) : (offsetY + (currentLineContent.Height - 0.13 * currentLineContent.FontSpecification.Height.LogicalPixels)));
			DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
			double strokeThickness = num;
			double num6 = ((Extent.LogicalToDevicePixelFactor == 1.0) ? 2.0 : 1.5);
			switch (underlineTypeNumber)
			{
			case "2":
			case "4":
				if (underlineTypeNumber == "4")
				{
					strokeThickness = num2;
				}
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, null);
				break;
			case "3":
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, null);
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY + num6, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, null);
				break;
			case "5":
			case "6":
				if (underlineTypeNumber == "6")
				{
					strokeThickness = num2;
				}
				doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, doubleCollection);
				break;
			case "7":
			case "8":
				if (underlineTypeNumber == "8")
				{
					strokeThickness = num2;
				}
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, doubleCollection);
				break;
			case "9":
			case "10":
				if (underlineTypeNumber == "10")
				{
					strokeThickness = num2;
				}
				doubleCollection.Add(6.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, doubleCollection);
				break;
			case "11":
			case "12":
				if (underlineTypeNumber == "12")
				{
					strokeThickness = num2;
				}
				doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, doubleCollection);
				break;
			case "13":
			case "14":
				if (underlineTypeNumber == "14")
				{
					strokeThickness = num2;
				}
				doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(4.0);
				CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(isUnderline: true, currentLineContent, offsetY, lineLength2, strokeThickness, contentElementHost, doubleCollection);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void CreateAndAddLineElementOrProxy(bool isUnderline, LineContentProxy lineContentProxy, double yOffset, double lineLength, double strokeThickness, IContentElementHost contentElementHost, DoubleCollection dashArray)
		{
			ColorInfo colorInfo = null;
			if (isUnderline)
			{
				colorInfo = ((null != lineContentProxy.UnderlineColor && lineContentProxy.UnderlineColor.Color.HasValue) ? lineContentProxy.UnderlineColor : lineContentProxy.Color);
			}
			double offsetX = lineContentProxy.OffsetX;
			double value = offsetX + lineLength;
			ColorInfo colorInfo2 = (isUnderline ? colorInfo : lineContentProxy.Color);
			Brush brushFromColorInfo = XamRichTextEditor.GetBrushFromColorInfo(colorInfo2, DocumentView.Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Foreground, LayoutBlockResolvedShading, lineContentProxy.Shading);
			ContentElementHost.LayoutProvider.UnderlineStrikethroughElementProxys.Add(new BorderElementProxy(Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(offsetX), Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(value), Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(yOffset), Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(yOffset), Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(strokeThickness), colorInfo2, brushFromColorInfo, dashArray, Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(TextAreaOffsetX), Extent.RoundToIntegralDevicePixel(TextAreaOffsetY), isVertical: false));
		}

		private double GetLineLength(List<LineContentProxy> lineContents, int currentLineContentsIndex, bool isForUnderline, LineContentProxy currentLineContent)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			string text = null;
			if (isForUnderline)
			{
				if (currentLineContent.ProcessedForUnderlineLength)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				text = GetUnderlineTypeNumber(currentLineContent.UnderlineType.Value);
			}
			else
			{
				if (currentLineContent.ProcessedForStrikethroughLength)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				flag = currentLineContent.StrikeThrough.Value;
				flag2 = currentLineContent.DoubleStrikeThrough.Value;
				if (!flag2 && !flag)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			int count = lineContents.Count;
			LineContentProxy lineContentProxy = currentLineContent;
			if (count == 1)
			{
				num = lineContents[0].ContentWidth;
			}
			else if (currentLineContent.IsInListNumberGroup)
			{
				if (currentLineContent != lineContents[0])
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				num = lineContents[0].ContentWidth;
			}
			else if (currentLineContentsIndex == count - 1)
			{
				num = lineContents[count - 1].ContentWidth;
			}
			else
			{
				for (int i = currentLineContentsIndex + 1; i < count; i++)
				{
					LineContentProxy lineContentProxy2 = lineContents[i];
					bool flag3 = false;
					if (isForUnderline)
					{
						flag3 = lineContentProxy2.UnderlineType.HasValue && lineContentProxy2.UnderlineType.Value != UnderlineType.None && text == GetUnderlineTypeNumber(lineContentProxy2.UnderlineType.Value);
					}
					else
					{
						bool? strikeThrough = lineContentProxy2.StrikeThrough;
						bool? doubleStrikeThrough = lineContentProxy2.DoubleStrikeThrough;
						flag3 = lineContentProxy2.FontSpecification.Bold == currentLineContent.FontSpecification.Bold && ((flag && strikeThrough.HasValue && strikeThrough.Value) || (flag2 && doubleStrikeThrough.HasValue && doubleStrikeThrough.Value));
					}
					if (flag3)
					{
						num += lineContentProxy2.OffsetX - lineContentProxy.OffsetX;
						if (i == count - 1)
						{
							num += lineContentProxy2.OverallWidth;
						}
						if (isForUnderline)
						{
							lineContentProxy2.ProcessedForUnderlineLength = true;
						}
						else
						{
							lineContentProxy2.ProcessedForStrikethroughLength = true;
						}
						lineContentProxy = lineContentProxy2;
						continue;
					}
					num += lineContentProxy2.OffsetX - lineContentProxy.OffsetX;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (isForUnderline)
			{
				currentLineContent.ProcessedForUnderlineLength = true;
			}
			else
			{
				currentLineContent.ProcessedForStrikethroughLength = true;
			}
			return num;
		}

		private string GetUnderlineTypeNumber(UnderlineType underlineType)
		{
			switch (underlineType)
			{
			case UnderlineType.Single:
			case UnderlineType.Words:
			case UnderlineType.Wave:
				return "2";
			case UnderlineType.Double:
			case UnderlineType.WavyDouble:
				return "3";
			case UnderlineType.Thick:
			case UnderlineType.WavyHeavy:
				return "4";
			case UnderlineType.Dotted:
				return "5";
			case UnderlineType.DottedHeavy:
				return "6";
			case UnderlineType.Dash:
				return "7";
			case UnderlineType.DashedHeavy:
				return "8";
			case UnderlineType.DashLong:
				return "9";
			case UnderlineType.DashLongHeavy:
				return "10";
			case UnderlineType.DotDash:
				return "11";
			case UnderlineType.DashDotHeavy:
				return "12";
			case UnderlineType.DotDotDash:
				return "13";
			case UnderlineType.DashDotDotHeavy:
				return "14";
			default:
				return "2";
			}
		}

		private void Init()
		{
			_element = new DocumentViewLineVisual(this);
			_element.Transform = new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0);
		}
	}
	internal class ImageLayoutNodeBinder
	{
		private ImageLayoutNode _imageLayoutNode;

		private ImageContainer _imageClippingHost;

		private ImageContainer _imageOuterContainer;

		private Image _image;

		private RichDocumentView _documentView;

		internal ImageLayoutNodeBinder(RichDocumentView documentView, ImageLayoutNode imageLayoutNode, ImageContainer imageClippingHost, ImageContainer imageOuterContainer, Image image)
		{
			_imageLayoutNode = imageLayoutNode;
			imageLayoutNode.PropertyChanged += imageLayoutNode_PropertyChanged;
			_imageClippingHost = imageClippingHost;
			_imageOuterContainer = imageOuterContainer;
			_image = image;
			_documentView = documentView;
			InitializeElementProperties();
		}

		internal void Dispose()
		{
			_imageLayoutNode.PropertyChanged -= imageLayoutNode_PropertyChanged;
			_imageLayoutNode = null;
			_imageClippingHost = null;
			_imageOuterContainer = null;
			_image = null;
			_documentView = null;
		}

		private void InitializeElementProperties()
		{
			_image.Width = _imageLayoutNode.ImageWidth;
			_image.Height = _imageLayoutNode.ImageHeight;
			_image.Source = _imageLayoutNode.ImageSource;
			_image.RenderTransform = _imageLayoutNode.RenderTransform;
			_imageOuterContainer.Width = _imageLayoutNode.ImageOuterAreaWidth;
			_imageOuterContainer.Height = _imageLayoutNode.ImageOuterAreaHeight;
			_imageClippingHost.Width = _imageLayoutNode.ImageCropAreaWidth;
			_imageClippingHost.Height = _imageLayoutNode.ImageCropAreaHeight;
			_imageClippingHost.RenderTransform = _imageLayoutNode.ImageCropAreaRenderTransform;
		}

		private void imageLayoutNode_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.PropertyName))
			{
				InitializeElementProperties();
			}
			else
			{
				switch (e.PropertyName)
				{
				case "ImageWidth":
					_image.Width = _imageLayoutNode.ImageWidth;
					break;
				case "ImageHeight":
					_image.Height = _imageLayoutNode.ImageHeight;
					break;
				case "ImageSource":
					_image.Source = _imageLayoutNode.ImageSource;
					break;
				case "RenderTransform":
					_image.RenderTransform = _imageLayoutNode.RenderTransform;
					break;
				case "ImageOuterAreaWidth":
					_imageOuterContainer.Width = _imageLayoutNode.ImageOuterAreaWidth;
					break;
				case "ImageOuterAreaHeight":
					_imageOuterContainer.Height = _imageLayoutNode.ImageOuterAreaHeight;
					break;
				case "ImageCropAreaWidth":
