					_imageClippingHost.Width = _imageLayoutNode.ImageCropAreaWidth;
					break;
				case "ImageCropAreaHeight":
					_imageClippingHost.Height = _imageLayoutNode.ImageCropAreaHeight;
					break;
				case "ImageCropAreaRenderTransform":
					_imageClippingHost.RenderTransform = _imageLayoutNode.ImageCropAreaRenderTransform;
					break;
				}
			}
			_documentView.RefreshLayersLayout();
		}
	}
	public class ScrollManager
	{
		private ScrollBar _verticalScrollBar;

		private ScrollBar _horizontalScrollBar;

		private bool _ignoreOffsetChange;

		private bool _verticalScrollbarValueChangedInternally;

		internal RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; private set; }

		internal ScrollBar HorizontalScrollBar
		{
			get
			{
				return _horizontalScrollBar;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_horizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					_horizontalScrollBar.ValueChanged -= OnHorizontalScrollBarValueChanged;
				}
				_horizontalScrollBar = value;
				_horizontalScrollBar.ValueChanged += OnHorizontalScrollBarValueChanged;
			}
		}

		internal XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; private set; }

		internal ScrollBar VerticalScrollBar
		{
			get
			{
				return _verticalScrollBar;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_verticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					_verticalScrollBar.ValueChanged -= OnVerticalScrollBarValueChanged;
				}
				_verticalScrollBar = value;
				_verticalScrollBar.ValueChanged += OnVerticalScrollBarValueChanged;
			}
		}

		internal ScrollManager(RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
		}

		internal bool GetIsHorizontalScrollBarNeeded(bool adjustForVerticalScrollBarWidth = false)
		{
			if (_horizontalScrollBar == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ComputeHorizontalScrollBarMaximum(adjustForVerticalScrollBarWidth) > 0.0;
		}

		internal bool GetIsVerticalScrollBarNeeded(bool adjustForHorizontalScrollBarHeight = false)
		{
			if (_verticalScrollBar == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ComputeVerticalScrollBarMaximum(adjustForHorizontalScrollBarHeight) > 0.0;
		}

		internal void OnOffsetChanged()
		{
			if (!_ignoreOffsetChange)
			{
				UpdateScrollBarValues();
			}
		}

		internal void ScrollDown(int numberOfPixelsToScroll)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			documentView.VerticalViewOffset = Math.Min(VerticalScrollBar.Maximum, documentView.VerticalViewOffset + (double)numberOfPixelsToScroll);
		}

		internal void ScrollLeft(double pixelsToScroll)
		{
			HorizontalScrollBar.Value = Math.Max(0.0, HorizontalScrollBar.Value - pixelsToScroll);
		}

		internal void ScrollPageDown()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (!documentView.IsLastDocumentLineInView)
			{
				documentView.VerticalViewOffset += VerticalScrollBar.LargeChange;
			}
		}

		internal void ScrollPageUp()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (!documentView.IsFirstDocumentLineInView)
			{
				documentView.VerticalViewOffset -= VerticalScrollBar.LargeChange;
			}
		}

		internal void ScrollRight(double pixelsToScroll)
		{
			double val = HorizontalScrollBar.Value + pixelsToScroll;
			HorizontalScrollBar.Value = Math.Min(val, HorizontalScrollBar.Maximum);
		}

		internal void ScrollUp(int numberOfPixelsToScroll)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			documentView.VerticalViewOffset = Math.Max(0.0, documentView.VerticalViewOffset - (double)numberOfPixelsToScroll);
		}

		internal void UpdateScrollBarRanges()
		{
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = DocumentView.Presenter.TextArea;
			if (textArea == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ScrollBar horizontalScrollBar = HorizontalScrollBar;
			ScrollBar verticalScrollBar = VerticalScrollBar;
			if (RichTextEditor != null)
			{
				if (horizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					horizontalScrollBar.SmallChange = 14.0;
					horizontalScrollBar.LargeChange = textArea.ActualWidth * 0.9;
					horizontalScrollBar.Minimum = 0.0;
					horizontalScrollBar.Maximum = ComputeHorizontalScrollBarMaximum();
					horizontalScrollBar.ViewportSize = textArea.ActualWidth;
				}
				if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					verticalScrollBar.SmallChange = 16.0;
					verticalScrollBar.LargeChange = textArea.ActualHeight;
					verticalScrollBar.Minimum = 0.0;
					verticalScrollBar.Maximum = ComputeVerticalScrollBarMaximum();
					verticalScrollBar.ViewportSize = textArea.ActualHeight;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateScrollBarValues()
		{
			UpdateScrollBarRanges();
			if (HorizontalScrollBar != null)
			{
				HorizontalScrollBar.Value = DocumentView.HorizontalViewOffset;
			}
			if (VerticalScrollBar != null)
			{
				_verticalScrollbarValueChangedInternally = true;
				VerticalScrollBar.Value = DocumentView.VerticalViewOffset;
				_verticalScrollbarValueChangedInternally = false;
			}
		}

		private double ComputeHorizontalScrollBarMaximum(bool adjustForVerticalScrollBarWidth = false)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			double num = 0.0;
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = documentView.Presenter.TextArea;
			if (textArea != null)
			{
				num = textArea.ActualWidth;
			}
			double num2 = (adjustForVerticalScrollBarWidth ? _verticalScrollBar.ActualWidth : 0.0);
			return documentView.MaximumHorizontalScrollExtent - (num - num2) + documentView.Caret.Width;
		}

		private double ComputeVerticalScrollBarMaximum(bool adjustForHorizontalScrollBarHeight = false)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			double num = 0.0;
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = documentView.Presenter.TextArea;
			if (textArea != null)
			{
				num = textArea.ActualHeight;
			}
			double num2 = (adjustForHorizontalScrollBarHeight ? _horizontalScrollBar.ActualHeight : 0.0);
			return documentView.MaximumVerticalScrollExtent - (num - num2);
		}

		private void OnHorizontalScrollBarValueChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			_ignoreOffsetChange = true;
			documentView.HorizontalViewOffset = Math.Max(0.0, e.NewValue);
			_ignoreOffsetChange = false;
		}

		private void OnVerticalScrollBarValueChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (!_verticalScrollbarValueChangedInternally)
			{
				documentView.OnVerticalViewOffsetChangedByUser();
			}
			_ignoreOffsetChange = true;
			documentView.VerticalViewOffset = Math.Max(0.0, e.NewValue);
			_ignoreOffsetChange = false;
		}
	}
	internal class ViewKeyboardHandler
	{
		private CharacterSettings _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace;

		private CharacterSettings _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert;

		private string _runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert;

		private readonly char TABCHAR = '\t';

		private readonly char ESCAPECHAR = '\u001b';

		internal CharacterSettings CharacterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace => _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace;

		internal CharacterSettings CharacterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert => _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert;

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; set; }

		private XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; set; }

		internal ViewKeyboardHandler(RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
		}

		internal void OnKeyDown(ref KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(RichTextEditor))
			{
				return;
			}
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			_ = documentView.Caret;
			ViewSelectionManager selectionManager = documentView.SelectionManager;
			Selection selection = documentView.Selection;
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			RichTextDocument document = richTextEditor.Document;
			bool isReadOnlyResolved = richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved;
			richTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage(string.Concat("@@@@   In ViewKeyboardHandler.OnKeyDown. Key = '", e.Key, "'"), ((IImeEditor)documentView.Presenter).DebugId);
			if (documentView.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition)
			{
				return;
			}
			NotifySelectionManagerOfModifierKeyStateChangeIfNecessary(e);
			switch (e.Key)
			{
			case Key.Back:
			case Key.Return:
			case Key.Escape:
				if (e.Key == Key.Escape && documentView.CloseContextMenuIfOpen())
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				else if ((Key.Return != e.Key || richTextEditor.AcceptsReturn) && document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved)
				{
					string text = "";
					if (e.Key == Key.Return)
					{
						text = "\r\n";
					}
					else if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
					{
						text = new string('\u001b', 1);
					}
					else if (e.Key == Key.Back)
					{
						text = new string('\b', 1);
					}
					ProcessText(text);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.Up:
			case Key.Down:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (e.Key == Key.Up)
				{
					if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift))
					{
						selection.SelectUp();
					}
					else if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.None))
					{
						selection.MoveUp();
					}
				}
				else if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift))
				{
					selection.SelectDown();
				}
				else if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.None))
				{
					selection.MoveDown();
				}
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			case Key.Left:
			case Key.Right:
			{
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				bool flag9 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
				bool flag10 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift);
				bool flag11 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
				if (e.Key == Key.Left)
				{
					if (flag9)
					{
						selection.MoveToPreviousWord();
					}
					else if (flag10)
					{
						selection.SelectPreviousCharacter();
					}
					else if (flag11)
					{
						selection.SelectPreviousWord();
					}
					else
					{
						selection.MoveToPreviousCharacter();
					}
				}
				else if (flag9)
				{
					selection.MoveToNextWord();
				}
				else if (flag10)
				{
					selection.SelectNextCharacter();
				}
				else if (flag11)
				{
					selection.SelectNextWord();
				}
				else
				{
					selection.MoveToNextCharacter();
				}
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			}
			case Key.Prior:
			case Key.Next:
			{
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				bool flag = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
				bool flag2 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift);
				bool flag3 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
				if (e.Key == Key.Prior)
				{
					if (flag2)
					{
						selectionManager.SelectPageUp();
					}
					else if (flag)
					{
						selectionManager.MoveToTopOfPage();
					}
					else if (flag3)
					{
						selectionManager.SelectToTopOfPage();
					}
					else
					{
						selectionManager.MovePageUp();
					}
				}
				else if (flag2)
				{
					selectionManager.SelectPageDown();
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					selectionManager.MoveToBottomOfPage();
				}
				else if (flag3)
				{
					selectionManager.SelectToBottomOfPage();
				}
				else
				{
					selectionManager.MovePageDown();
				}
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			}
			case Key.Delete:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved)
				{
					if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.None))
					{
						selection.DeleteCurrentCharacter();
					}
					else if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
					{
						selection.DeleteToEndOfCurrentWord();
					}
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.Home:
			{
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				bool num = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
				bool flag7 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift);
				bool flag8 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
				if (num)
				{
					selection.MoveToStartOfDocument();
				}
				else if (flag7)
				{
					selection.SelectToStartOfLine();
				}
				else if (flag8)
				{
					selection.SelectToStartOfDocument();
				}
				else
				{
					if (selection.Length > 0)
					{
						selection.Collapse(SelectionCollapseDirection.Start);
					}
					selection.MoveToStartOfLine();
				}
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			}
			case Key.End:
			{
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				bool flag4 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
				bool flag5 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift);
				bool flag6 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
				if (flag4 && !flag5)
				{
					if (selectionManager.Selection.Length > 0)
					{
						selectionManager.Selection.Collapse(SelectionCollapseDirection.End);
					}
					selection.MoveToEndOfDocument();
					documentView.ScrollManager.VerticalScrollBar.Value = documentView.ScrollManager.VerticalScrollBar.Maximum;
				}
				else if (!flag4 && flag5)
				{
					selection.SelectToEndOfLine();
				}
				else if (flag6)
				{
					selection.SelectToEndOfDocument();
					documentView.ScrollManager.VerticalScrollBar.Value = documentView.ScrollManager.VerticalScrollBar.Maximum;
				}
				else
				{
					if (selection.Length > 0)
					{
						selection.Collapse(SelectionCollapseDirection.End);
						if (documentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAfterParagraphMark(selection.Start))
						{
							int start = Math.Max(0, selection.Start - 1);
							selection.SetRange(start, 0);
						}
					}
					selection.MoveToEndOfLine();
				}
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			}
			case Key.Tab:
			{
				bool acceptsTab = richTextEditor.AcceptsTab;
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && acceptsTab)
				{
					e.Handled = ProcessText(new string(new char[1] { TABCHAR }));
				}
				break;
			}
			case Key.Insert:
				if (document == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (!isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift))
				{
					selection.Paste();
					e.Handled = true;
					break;
				}
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selection.Copy();
					e.Handled = true;
					break;
				}
				if (richTextEditor.TextUpdateMode == RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert)
				{
					richTextEditor.TextUpdateMode = RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Overwrite;
				}
				else
				{
					richTextEditor.TextUpdateMode = RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert;
				}
				e.Handled = true;
				break;
			case Key.D8:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift) && document != null)
				{
					if (richTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode == HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DisplayAllHiddenSymbols)
					{
						richTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode = HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DoNotDisplay;
					}
					else
					{
						richTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode = HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DisplayAllHiddenSymbols;
					}
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.A:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift) && document != null)
				{
					selection.ToggleAllCapsFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				else if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null)
				{
					selection.SelectAll();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.Z:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selectionManager.Undo();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.Y:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selectionManager.Redo();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.C:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null)
				{
					selection.Copy();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.V:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selection.Paste();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.X:
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selection.Cut();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.B:
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selection.ToggleBoldFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.I:
				if (document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved && PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
				{
					selection.ToggleItalicFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.U:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null)
				{
					selection.ToggleSingleUnderlineFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.L:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null)
				{
					selection.AlignParagraphContentLeft();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.E:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null)
				{
					selection.AlignParagraphContentCenter();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.R:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null)
				{
					selection.AlignParagraphContentRight();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.J:
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && document != null && !isReadOnlyResolved)
				{
					selection.AlignParagraphContentJustified();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.K:
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift) && document != null)
				{
					selection.ToggleSmallCapsFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				break;
			case Key.OemPlus:
				ProcessPlusKey(e);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void OnKeyUp(ref KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			NotifySelectionManagerOfModifierKeyStateChangeIfNecessary(e);
		}

		internal void OnTextInput(ref TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = OnTextInput(e.Text);
		}

		internal bool OnTextInput(string text, CharacterSettings characterSettings = null)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			if (DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(richTextEditor))
			{
				return false;
			}
			RichTextDocument document = richTextEditor.Document;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) || document == null || richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return ProcessText(text, characterSettings);
		}

		internal void ResetCachedCharacterSettingsFromLastBackspace()
		{
			_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
		}

		internal void SimulateBackspace()
		{
			ProcessText(new string(new char[1] { '\b' }));
		}

		private bool GetIsNextOrCurrentCharacterTab(int offset, RichTextDocument document)
		{
			int num = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(offset - 1, document);
			LayoutManagerBase layoutManager = DocumentView.LayoutManager;
			ParagraphLayoutNode paragraphAtDocumentOffset = layoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(offset);
			if (num != offset && layoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(num) == paragraphAtDocumentOffset && document.GetTextInSpan(new DocumentSpan(num, 1)) == "\t")
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (document.GetTextInSpan(new DocumentSpan(offset, 1)) == "\t")
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void NotifySelectionManagerOfModifierKeyStateChangeIfNecessary(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.LeftCtrl || e.Key == Key.RightCtrl || e.Key == Key.LeftShift || e.Key == Key.RightShift || e.Key == Key.LeftAlt || e.Key == Key.RightAlt)
			{
				DocumentView.SelectionManager.ModifierKeyStateChanged();
			}
		}

		private string PerformInsertParagraph(RichDocumentView documentView, RichTextDocument document, VersionedDocumentOffset documentOffset)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			Selection selection = documentView.Selection;
			RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
			richTextHitTestInfo.Update(selection.Start, documentView.VerticalViewOffset, delayUpdatesUntilAccessed: false);
			if (richTextHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode != null && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableRowMark)
			{
				EditorUndoTransaction editorUndoTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset, selection.GetArrayOfClonedRanges(), selection.IsSelectionInverted);
				document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(editorUndoTransaction);
				empty = null;
				TableRowNode tableRowNode = documentView.Document.InsertTableRow(richTextHitTestInfo.TableRowNode.GetDocumentOffset(), before: false, out empty);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty) && tableRowNode != null)
				{
					int documentOffset2 = tableRowNode.GetDocumentOffset();
					selection.SelectionAnchorOffset = documentOffset2;
					selection.Start = documentOffset2;
					selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(documentOffset2);
					editorUndoTransaction.Commit();
				}
				else
				{
					editorUndoTransaction.Rollback();
				}
				return empty;
			}
			DocumentSpan intersectSpan = new DocumentSpan(Math.Max(0, documentOffset - 1), 1);
			IEnumerable<RunNode> runs = document.GetRuns(intersectSpan);
			if (runs != null && runs.Count() > 0)
			{
				RunNode runNode = runs.First();
				bool flag = runNode.StyleId == "Hyperlink";
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = (flag ? runNode.Settings : runNode.SettingsResolved);
				_runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = runs.First().StyleId;
			}
			else
			{
				_runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = string.Empty;
			}
			return RichTextEditorUtilities.InsertParagraphHelper(documentView, document, documentOffset);
		}

		private void PerformListIndentOutdent(bool isShiftPressed, Selection selection)
		{
			if (isShiftPressed)
			{
				selection.DecreaseListIndent();
			}
			else
			{
				selection.IncreaseListIndent();
			}
		}

		private void PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(bool isShiftPressed, Selection selection)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			ViewSelectionManager selectionManager = documentView.SelectionManager;
			_ = selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineContent;
			RichTextDocument document = documentView.Document;
			int val = -1;
			if (documentView.LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(selection.Start, out var layoutBlock, out var line))
			{
				val = ((ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock).GetIndexOfLine(line);
			}
			EditorUndoTransaction editorUndoTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset, selection.GetArrayOfClonedRanges(), selection.IsSelectionInverted);
			document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(editorUndoTransaction);
			if (isShiftPressed)
			{
				selection.DecreaseIndent();
			}
			else
			{
				selection.IncreaseIndent();
			}
			documentView.LayoutManager.EnsureStatusIsComplete();
			val = Math.Min(val, ((ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock).LineCount);
			line = ((ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock).GetLineAtIndex(val);
			int documentOffsetOfRichTextLine = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetDocumentOffsetOfRichTextLine(line, (ParagraphLayoutNode)layoutBlock);
			if (documentOffsetOfRichTextLine != selection.Start && !selectionManager.SelectionStartsInList)
			{
				int num = selection.Start;
				int num2 = 0;
				while (num > documentOffsetOfRichTextLine && document.GetTextInSpan(new DocumentSpan(num - 1, 1)) == "\t")
				{
					num2++;
					num--;
				}
				if (documentOffsetOfRichTextLine != selection.Start - num2)
				{
					selection.MoveRange(documentOffsetOfRichTextLine - selection.Start);
				}
			}
			editorUndoTransaction.Commit();
		}

		private void ProcessPlusKey(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			if (richTextEditor.Document != null && !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved)
			{
				bool num = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
				bool flag = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control | ModifierKeys.Shift);
				if (num)
				{
					richTextEditor.Selection.ToggleSubscriptFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					richTextEditor.Selection.ToggleSuperscriptFormatting();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ProcessTab(bool isShiftPressed, Selection selection, RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			if (isShiftPressed)
			{
				ProcessShiftTab(selection.Start, documentView);
			}
			else
			{
				selection.InsertTab();
			}
		}

		private void ProcessTabInTableCell(bool isShiftPressed, Selection selection, RichDocumentView documentView, RichTextHitTestInfo hitTestInfo)
		{
			TableCellLayoutNode tableCellLayoutNode = null;
			if (hitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode == null)
			{
				if (hitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				tableCellLayoutNode = hitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode.GetLastCell();
			}
			else
			{
				tableCellLayoutNode = hitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode;
			}
			TableLayoutNode tableLayoutNode = hitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode;
			int columnCount = tableLayoutNode.ColumnCount;
			int count = tableLayoutNode.ChildNodes.Count;
			int row = tableCellLayoutNode.Row;
			int column = tableCellLayoutNode.Column;
			if (row == count - 1 && column == columnCount - 1)
			{
				RichTextDocument document = documentView.Document;
				EditorUndoTransaction editorUndoTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset, selection.GetArrayOfClonedRanges(), selection.IsSelectionInverted);
				document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(editorUndoTransaction);
				TableNode tableNode = tableLayoutNode.DocumentNode as TableNode;
				string error = null;
				TableRowNode tableRowNode = documentView.Document.InsertTableRow(tableNode.GetRow(count - 1).GetDocumentOffset(), before: false, out error);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(error) && tableRowNode != null)
				{
					int documentOffset = tableRowNode.GetDocumentOffset();
					selection.SelectionAnchorOffset = documentOffset;
					selection.Start = documentOffset;
					selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(documentOffset);
					editorUndoTransaction.Commit();
				}
				else
				{
					editorUndoTransaction.Rollback();
				}
				return;
			}
			int rowIndex = row;
			int index = column;
			if (isShiftPressed)
			{
				if (column == 0)
				{
					if (row != 0)
					{
						rowIndex = row - 1;
						index = columnCount - 1;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					index = column - 1;
				}
			}
			else if (column >= columnCount - 1)
			{
				if (row < count - 1)
				{
					rowIndex = row + 1;
					index = 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				index = column + 1;
			}
			if (tableLayoutNode.GetRowAtIndex(rowIndex).ChildNodes[index] is TableCellLayoutNode tableCellLayoutNode2)
			{
				selection.Collapse();
				DocumentSpan documentSpan = tableCellLayoutNode2.DocumentNode.GetDocumentSpan();
				selection.Start = documentSpan.Start;
				selection.SelectionAnchorOffset = documentSpan.Start;
				if (documentSpan.Length < 2)
				{
					selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(documentSpan.Start);
				}
				else
				{
					selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(Math.Max(documentSpan.Start, documentSpan.End) - 1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ProcessShiftTab(int documentOffset, RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			Point? point = documentView.LayoutPointFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset);
			if (!point.HasValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
			richTextHitTestInfo.Update(point.Value, documentView.VerticalViewOffset);
			if (richTextHitTestInfo.IsInTable)
			{
				return;
			}
			ParagraphLayoutNode paragraphLayoutNode = richTextHitTestInfo.ParagraphLayoutNode;
			if (paragraphLayoutNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int documentOffset2 = paragraphLayoutNode.DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset();
			if (documentOffset != documentOffset2 && documentOffset != documentOffset2 + paragraphLayoutNode.DocumentNode.PrefixOffsetCount)
			{
				int offset = documentOffset - 1;
				VersionedDocumentOffset caretDocumentOffset = new VersionedDocumentOffset(documentView.Document, offset);
				DocumentSpan span = new DocumentSpan(offset, 1);
				if (documentView.Document.GetTextInSpan(span) == "\t")
				{
					RichTextEditorUtilities.ReplaceTextHelper(documentView, documentView.Document, caretDocumentOffset, "", span);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ProcessText(string text, CharacterSettings characterSettings = null)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Caret caret = documentView.Caret;
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			RichTextDocument document = richTextEditor.Document;
			Selection selection = documentView.Selection;
			ViewSelectionManager selectionManager = documentView.SelectionManager;
			caret.TranslateDocumentOffsetToCurrentVersion(OffsetTrackingMode.Positive);
			VersionedDocumentOffset documentOffset = caret.DocumentOffset;
			bool flag = false;
			switch (text)
			{
			case "\r":
			case "\n":
			case "\r\n":
			case "\n\r":
				text = "\n";
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
			if (flag && !richTextEditor.AcceptsReturn)
			{
				return false;
			}
			char c = text[0];
			bool flag2 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift);
			bool flag3 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
			bool flag4 = flag && !flag2;
			bool flag5 = false;
			if (char.IsLetterOrDigit(c) || char.IsPunctuation(c) || char.IsSeparator(c) || char.IsSymbol(c) || flag || c == TABCHAR)
			{
				if (selection.Length > 0)
				{
					if (c == TABCHAR)
					{
						RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
						richTextHitTestInfo.Update(selection.Start, documentView.VerticalViewOffset, delayUpdatesUntilAccessed: false);
						if (richTextHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode != null || richTextHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode != null)
						{
							if (flag3)
							{
								selection.InsertTab();
							}
							else
							{
								ProcessTabInTableCell(flag2, selection, documentView, richTextHitTestInfo);
							}
						}
						else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtListIndicator)
						{
							if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfList)
							{
								PerformListIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
							}
							else
							{
								PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
							}
						}
						else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfList)
						{
							if (selectionManager.SelectionIncludesEntireLine || selectionManager.SelectionSpansLines)
							{
								PerformListIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
							}
							else
							{
								selection.SetSelectedText("", preserveSelection: false);
								PerformListIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
							}
						}
						else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsInList)
						{
							if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineContent)
							{
								if (selectionManager.SelectionIncludesEntireLine || selectionManager.SelectionSpansLines)
								{
									PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
								}
								else
								{
									selection.SetSelectedText("", preserveSelection: false);
									PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
								}
							}
							else if (selectionManager.SelectionSpansParagraphs)
							{
								selection.SetSelectedText("", preserveSelection: false);
								ProcessTab(flag2, selection, documentView);
							}
							else
							{
								if (selectionManager.SelectionEndsAfterParagraphMark)
								{
									selection.End--;
								}
								ProcessTab(flag2, selection, documentView);
							}
						}
						else if (selectionManager.SelectionIncludesEntireLine)
						{
							PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
						}
						else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineContent)
						{
							selection.SetSelectedText("", preserveSelection: false);
							PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
						}
						else
						{
							ProcessTab(flag2, selection, documentView);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (flag4)
						{
							VersionedDocumentOffset startVersioned = selection.StartVersioned;
							document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(null, null);
							selection.SetSelectedText("", preserveSelection: false);
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PerformInsertParagraph(documentView, document, startVersioned)))
							{
								document.UndoManager.CurrentTransaction.Commit();
							}
							else
							{
								document.UndoManager.CurrentTransaction.Rollback();
							}
							return true;
						}
						selection.SetSelectedText(text, preserveSelection: false);
					}
				}
				else if (c == TABCHAR)
				{
					RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo2 = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
					richTextHitTestInfo2.Update(selection.Start, documentView.VerticalViewOffset, delayUpdatesUntilAccessed: false);
					if (richTextHitTestInfo2.TableCellLayoutNode != null || richTextHitTestInfo2.TableRowLayoutNode != null)
					{
						if (flag3)
						{
							selection.InsertTab();
						}
						else
						{
							ProcessTabInTableCell(flag2, selection, documentView, richTextHitTestInfo2);
						}
					}
					else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfList)
					{
						PerformListIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
					}
					else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsInList)
					{
						if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineInList)
						{
							PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
						}
						else
						{
							ProcessTab(flag2, selection, documentView);
						}
					}
					else if (selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLine && !documentView.GetDoesParagraphAtOffsetHaveCustomTabStops(selection.Start) && documentView.GetDoesParagraphAtOffsetHaveContent(selection.Start))
					{
						if (GetIsNextOrCurrentCharacterTab(selection.Start, document))
						{
							ProcessTab(flag2, selection, documentView);
						}
						else
						{
							PerformParagraphIndentOutdent(flag2, selection);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						ProcessTab(flag2, selection, documentView);
					}
					flag5 = true;
				}
				else if (flag4)
				{
					if (!selectionManager.SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineInList)
					{
						PerformInsertParagraph(documentView, document, documentOffset);
						return true;
					}
					LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = documentView.LineFromDocumentOffset(caret.DocumentOffset);
					if (lineElementProxy != null && lineElementProxy.HasContent)
					{
						PerformInsertParagraph(documentView, document, documentOffset);
						return true;
					}
					ParagraphLayoutNode paragraphAtDocumentOffset = documentView.LayoutManager.GetParagraphAtDocumentOffset(caret.DocumentOffset);
					if (paragraphAtDocumentOffset != null && ((ParagraphNode)paragraphAtDocumentOffset.DocumentNode).ListLevel > 0)
					{
						selection.DecreaseIndent();
					}
					else
					{
						selection.ClearParagraphListStyle();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					documentView.VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
					RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode richDocumentViewTextUpdateMode = richTextEditor.TextUpdateMode;
					if (richDocumentViewTextUpdateMode == RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Overwrite && documentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOrEndOfLine(beginning: false, documentOffset))
					{
						richDocumentViewTextUpdateMode = RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert;
					}
					CharacterSettings characterSettings2 = _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace;
					if (characterSettings != null)
					{
						characterSettings2 = Infragistics.Documents.RichText.Utilities.MergeThreeLazy(null, characterSettings2, characterSettings);
					}
					if (richDocumentViewTextUpdateMode == RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert)
					{
						RichTextEditorUtilities.InsertTextHelper(documentView, document, documentOffset, text, (characterSettings2 != null) ? characterSettings2 : _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert, _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace == null, _runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert);
					}
					else
					{
						RichTextEditorUtilities.ReplaceTextHelper(documentView, document, documentOffset, text, new DocumentSpan(documentOffset, text.Length), (characterSettings2 != null) ? characterSettings2 : _characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert, _runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert);
					}
					if (_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace != null)
					{
						_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = null;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					documentView.HorizontalViewOffset = 0.0;
				}
				if (!flag5)
				{
					_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = null;
				}
				_runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = string.Empty;
				return true;
			}
			if (c == '\b')
			{
				DocumentSpan documentSpan = new DocumentSpan(RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(selection.Start, document), 0);
				if (!documentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfLineInList(selection.Start))
				{
					_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastBackspace = document.GetCommonCharacterSettings(documentSpan);
				}
				RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo3 = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
				richTextHitTestInfo3.Update(selection.Start, documentView.VerticalViewOffset);
				bool flag6 = true;
				if (richTextHitTestInfo3.TableCellLayoutNode != null && selection.Length == 0 && selection.Start == richTextHitTestInfo3.TableCellLayoutNode.DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset())
				{
					flag6 = false;
				}
				if (flag6)
				{
					if (richTextHitTestInfo3.ParagraphLayoutNode != null && selection.Length < 1)
					{
						int documentOffset2 = richTextHitTestInfo3.ParagraphLayoutNode.DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset();
						if (selection.Start == documentOffset2 || (selection.Start == documentOffset2 + 1 && selection.Start == document.RootNode.AggregateOffsetCount))
						{
							ParagraphSettings commonParagraphSettings = document.GetCommonParagraphSettings(documentSpan);
							ParagraphAlignment? paragraphAlignment = commonParagraphSettings.ParagraphAlignment;
							if (paragraphAlignment.HasValue)
							{
								string error;
								if (paragraphAlignment.Value == ParagraphAlignment.End)
								{
									commonParagraphSettings.ParagraphAlignment = ParagraphAlignment.Center;
									document.ApplyParagraphSettings(documentSpan, commonParagraphSettings, out error);
									flag6 = false;
								}
								else if (paragraphAlignment.Value == ParagraphAlignment.Center)
								{
									commonParagraphSettings.ParagraphAlignment = ParagraphAlignment.Start;
									document.ApplyParagraphSettings(documentSpan, commonParagraphSettings, out error);
									flag6 = false;
								}
							}
							if (flag6 && document.OutdentParagraphs(documentSpan))
							{
								flag6 = false;
							}
						}
					}
					if (flag6)
					{
						if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.None))
						{
							selection.DeletePreviousCharacter();
						}
						else if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control))
						{
							selection.DeleteToBeginningOfCurrentWord();
						}
					}
				}
				_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = null;
				_runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = string.Empty;
				return true;
			}
			_ = ESCAPECHAR;
			_characterSettingsInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = null;
			_runStyleIdInEffectBeforeLastParagraphInsert = string.Empty;
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal class ViewMouseHandler
	{
		private class MouseWheelHelper
		{
			private int? _mouseWheelDeltaMinimum;

			private int? _mouseWheelDeltaMaximum;

			private List<int> _mouseWheelDeltasSeen;

			internal MouseWheelHelper()
			{
				_mouseWheelDeltasSeen = new List<int>();
			}

			internal int GetPixelsToScrollForDelta(int delta)
			{
				delta = Math.Abs(delta);
				if (!_mouseWheelDeltasSeen.Contains(delta))
				{
					_mouseWheelDeltasSeen.Add(delta);
					_mouseWheelDeltasSeen.Sort();
				}
				if (!_mouseWheelDeltaMinimum.HasValue)
				{
					_mouseWheelDeltaMinimum = (_mouseWheelDeltaMaximum = delta);
				}
				else
				{
					if (delta < _mouseWheelDeltaMinimum)
					{
						_mouseWheelDeltaMinimum = delta;
					}
					if (delta > _mouseWheelDeltaMaximum)
					{
						_mouseWheelDeltaMaximum = delta;
					}
				}
				return (_mouseWheelDeltasSeen.IndexOf(delta) + 1) * 20;
			}
		}

		private Point _mouseDownPointInTextAreaCoordinates;

		private MouseWheelHelper _mouseWheelHelper;

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; set; }

		private XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; set; }

		internal ViewMouseHandler(RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			_mouseWheelHelper = new MouseWheelHelper();
		}

		internal void OnMouseDoubleClick(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.SelectionManager.OnMouseDoubleClick(e);
		}

		internal void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		internal void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.CursorHelper.HideTemplate();
		}

		internal void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			ViewSelectionManager selectionManager = documentView.SelectionManager;
			Selection selection = selectionManager.Selection;
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = documentView.Presenter.TextArea;
			_mouseDownPointInTextAreaCoordinates = e.GetPosition(textArea);
			bool flag = false;
			RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
			richTextHitTestInfo.Update(documentView.LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(_mouseDownPointInTextAreaCoordinates), _mouseDownPointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift) && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableColumnPrefixArea && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableRowPrefixArea && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellMarginAreaLeft && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellHorizontalResizeBorder && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableResizeCorner)
			{
				int? num = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(_mouseDownPointInTextAreaCoordinates, includeListNumbers: false, includeParagraphMarks: true);
				if (num.HasValue)
				{
					selection.EstablishSelectionForNavigatedOffset(num.Value, num.Value < selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset, null);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				if (richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellHorizontalResizeBorder && richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea != RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableResizeCorner)
				{
					documentView.Caret.ProcessMouseLeftButtonDownInView(documentView, e);
				}
				selectionManager.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		internal void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.SelectionManager.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		internal void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.SelectionManager.OnMouseMove(e);
		}

		internal void OnMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.SelectionManager.OnMouseRightButtonDown(e);
		}

		internal void OnMouseRightButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.SelectionManager.OnMouseRightButtonUp(e);
		}

		internal void OnMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			int pixelsToScrollForDelta = _mouseWheelHelper.GetPixelsToScrollForDelta(e.Delta);
			if (e.Delta < 0)
			{
				DocumentView.ScrollManager.ScrollDown(pixelsToScrollForDelta);
			}
			else
			{
				DocumentView.ScrollManager.ScrollUp(pixelsToScrollForDelta);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ViewSelectionManager : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private enum PageMoveCaretPositionOption
		{
			PositionCaretAtPreferredOffsetX,
			PositionCaretAtZeroOffsetX,
			PositionCaretAtMaxOffsetX
		}

		[Flags]
		internal enum MouseActivityType
		{
			None = 0,
			Select = 1,
			MultiSelect = 2,
			SelectTableColumn = 4,
			SelectTableRow = 8,
			SelectTableCell = 0x10,
			SelectInsideTableCell = 0x20,
			SelectBullet = 0x40,
			ResizeVerticalEdge = 0x80,
			ResizeHorizontalEdge = 0x100,
			ResizeRectangle = 0x200,
			Drag = 0x400
		}

		private struct ResizeConstraint
		{
			internal double MinX;

			internal double MaxX;

			internal double MinY;

			internal double MaxY;

			internal ResizeConstraint(double minX, double maxX, double minY, double maxY)
			{
				MinX = minX;
				MaxX = maxX;
				MinY = minY;
				MaxY = maxY;
			}
		}

		private DispatcherTimer _autoScrollTimer;

		private bool _isMouseDown;

		private Range _lastEventSelectionRange;

		private bool _receivedFirstMouseMove;

		private MouseEventArgs _lastMouseMoveEventArgs;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _lastMouseDownHitTestInfo;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _currentMouseDownHitTestInfo;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _currentMouseUpHitTestInfo;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo;

		private RichTextHitTestInfo _selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo;

		private Rect _mouseDownResizeRect;

		private MouseActivityType _currentMouseActivityType;

		private MouseActivityType _lastMouseActivityType;

		private ResizeConstraint _currentResizeConstraint;

		private ToolTip _hyperlinkToolTip;

		private HyperlinkNode _mouseDownHyperlinkNode;

		private HyperlinkToolTipPresenter _hyperlinkToolTipPresenter;

		private bool _selectionChangedSinceMouseDown;

		private bool _mouseMoveTriggeredByMouseCapture;

		private double _verticalResizeEdgeOffset;

		internal MouseActivityType CurrentMouseActivityType
		{
			get
			{
				return _currentMouseActivityType;
			}
			private set
			{
				_currentMouseActivityType = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("CurrentMouseActivityType");
			}
		}

		internal ToolTip HyperlinkToolTip
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hyperlinkToolTip == null)
				{
					_hyperlinkToolTip = new ToolTip();
				}
				return _hyperlinkToolTip;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsMouseDown => _isMouseDown;

		internal bool IsMouseDownInTable
		{
			get
			{
				if (_isMouseDown && CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo != null)
				{
					return CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal Selection Selection => DocumentView.Selection;

		internal bool SelectionEndsAfterParagraphMark => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAfterParagraphMark(Selection.End);

		internal bool SelectionEndsAtEndOfLine => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOrEndOfLine(beginning: false, Selection.End);

		internal bool SelectionIncludesEntireLine
		{
			get
			{
				if (SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineContent)
				{
					return SelectionEndsAtEndOfLine;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool SelectionSpansLines => !DocumentView.GetAreDocumentOffsetsOnSameLine(Selection.Start, Selection.End);

		internal bool SelectionSpansParagraphs => !DocumentView.GetAreDocumentOffsetsInSameParagraph(Selection.Start, Selection.End, includeParagraphMark: true);

		internal bool SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLine => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOrEndOfLine(beginning: true, Selection.Start, backupBeforeTabsIfBeginning: false);

		internal bool SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineContent => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOrEndOfLine(beginning: true, Selection.Start);

		internal bool SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfLineInList => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfLineInList(Selection.Start);

		internal bool SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfList => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfList(Selection.Start);

		internal bool SelectionStartsAtBeginningOfParagraph => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtBeginningOfParagraph(Selection.Start);

		internal bool SelectionStartsAtListIndicator => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetAtListIndicator(Selection.Start);

		internal bool SelectionStartsInList => DocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetInList(Selection.Start);

		internal int TotalMouseDownMouseMoves { get; private set; }

		private DispatcherTimer AutoScrollTimer
		{
			get
			{
				if (_autoScrollTimer == null)
				{
					_autoScrollTimer = new DispatcherTimer();
					_autoScrollTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100.0);
					_autoScrollTimer.Tick += OnAutoScrollTimerTick;
				}
				return _autoScrollTimer;
			}
		}

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; set; }

		private HyperlinkToolTipPresenter HyperlinkToolTipPresenter
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hyperlinkToolTipPresenter == null)
				{
					_hyperlinkToolTipPresenter = new HyperlinkToolTipPresenter();
				}
				return _hyperlinkToolTipPresenter;
			}
		}

		private bool IsMultiSelectActive
		{
			get
			{
				if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && Selection.Length > 0)
				{
					return RichTextEditor.IsMultiSelectEnabled;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private RichTextHitTestInfo CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentMouseDownHitTestInfo == null)
				{
					_currentMouseDownHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(DocumentView.LayoutManager);
				}
				return _currentMouseDownHitTestInfo;
			}
		}

		private RichTextHitTestInfo CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo == null)
				{
					_currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(DocumentView.LayoutManager);
				}
				return _currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo;
			}
		}

		private RichTextHitTestInfo CurrentMouseUpHitTestInfo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentMouseUpHitTestInfo == null)
				{
					_currentMouseUpHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(DocumentView.LayoutManager);
				}
				return _currentMouseUpHitTestInfo;
			}
		}

		private XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; set; }

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		internal ViewSelectionManager(RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			int version = documentView.Document.CurrentVersion.Version;
			if (selection.Range.StartVersioned.Version != null && selection.Range.StartVersioned.Version.Version == version && selection.Range.EndVersioned.Version != null && selection.Range.EndVersioned.Version.Version == version && documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset.Version != null && documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset.Version.Version == version)
			{
				selection.SetRange(documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset, documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset);
			}
			_lastEventSelectionRange = selection.Range.Clone();
			selection.PropertyChanged += OnSelectionPropertyChanged;
		}

		internal void ModifierKeyStateChanged()
		{
			if (CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo != null && _lastMouseMoveEventArgs != null)
			{
				DocumentView.SetCursor(CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.HitTestArea, _lastMouseMoveEventArgs);
			}
		}

		internal void MovePageDown()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: false, select: false, restrictToCurrentView: false);
		}

		internal void MovePageUp()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: true, select: false, restrictToCurrentView: false);
		}

		internal void MoveToBottomOfPage()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: false, select: false, restrictToCurrentView: true);
		}

		internal void MoveToTopOfPage()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: true, select: false, restrictToCurrentView: true);
		}

		internal void OnDispose()
		{
			if (_autoScrollTimer != null)
			{
				_autoScrollTimer.Stop();
			}
		}

		internal void OnMouseDoubleClick(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor == null || RichTextEditor.Document == null || CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.ParagraphListIndicator)
			{
				return;
			}
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Selection selection = documentView.Selection;
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = documentView.Presenter.TextArea;
			Point position = e.GetPosition(textArea);
			int? num = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(position);
			if (!num.HasValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo = new RichTextHitTestInfo(documentView.LayoutManager);
			richTextHitTestInfo.Update(documentView.LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(position), documentView.VerticalViewOffset);
			if (richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.ParagraphListIndicator || richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableColumnPrefixArea || richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableRowPrefixArea || richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellHorizontalResizeBorder || richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (richTextHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellMarginAreaLeft && richTextHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode != null)
			{
				selection.AddRowCellsToSelectionHelper(richTextHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode, richTextHitTestInfo.TableNode, processBackwards: false, !PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control));
				return;
			}
			if (IsMultiSelectActive && CurrentMouseActivityType == MouseActivityType.None)
			{
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.MultiSelect;
				selection.StartMultiSelection(num.Value);
			}
			selection.SelectCurrentWord();
			selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset = selection.Start;
		}

		internal void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor != null && RichTextEditor.Document != null)
			{
				ProcessMouseDownPoint(e);
				_mouseMoveTriggeredByMouseCapture = true;
				DocumentView.Presenter.CaptureMouse();
			}
		}

		internal void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor == null || RichTextEditor.Document == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_isMouseDown = false;
			TotalMouseDownMouseMoves = 0;
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Point position = e.GetPosition(documentView.Presenter.TextArea);
			CurrentMouseUpHitTestInfo.Update(documentView.LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(position), position);
			_ = CurrentMouseUpHitTestInfo;
			AutoScrollTimer.Stop();
			documentView.Presenter.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			if (CurrentMouseActivityType == MouseActivityType.MultiSelect)
			{
				Selection.EndMultiSelection();
			}
			if (_mouseDownHyperlinkNode != null && _mouseDownHyperlinkNode != null)
			{
				HyperlinkExecutingEventArgs e2 = new HyperlinkExecutingEventArgs(_mouseDownHyperlinkNode.Uri);
				RichTextEditor.RaiseHyperlinkExecuting(e2);
				if (!e2.Cancel)
				{
					InvokeUri(e2);
				}
			}
			_mouseDownHyperlinkNode = null;
			switch (CurrentMouseActivityType)
			{
			case MouseActivityType.ResizeVerticalEdge:
			{
				RichTextHitTestInfo currentMouseDownHitTestInfo2 = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo;
				TableCellLayoutNode tableCellLayoutNode = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo2.TableCellLayoutNode;
				TableLayoutNode tableLayoutNode3 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo2.TableLayoutNode;
				if (tableCellLayoutNode != null && tableLayoutNode3 != null)
				{
					RichTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
					Point textAreaPoint3 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo2.TextAreaPoint;
					double value4 = position.X - textAreaPoint3.X;
					tableLayoutNode3.ResizeColumn(tableCellLayoutNode.Column, tableCellLayoutNode.ColumnSpan, new Extent(value4, ExtentUnitType.LogicalPixels), !PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control));
				}
				documentView.VerticalResizeIndicatorIsVisible = false;
				if (RichTextEditor.Cursor == Cursors.SizeWE)
				{
					RichTextEditor.Cursor = null;
				}
				break;
			}
			case MouseActivityType.ResizeHorizontalEdge:
			{
				RichTextHitTestInfo currentMouseDownHitTestInfo = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo;
				TableRowLayoutNode tableRowLayoutNode = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode;
				TableLayoutNode tableLayoutNode2 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode;
				if (tableRowLayoutNode != null && tableLayoutNode2 != null)
				{
					RichTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
					Point textAreaPoint2 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint;
					double value3 = position.Y - textAreaPoint2.Y;
					currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode.ResizeRow(tableRowLayoutNode.Row, new Extent(value3, ExtentUnitType.LogicalPixels));
				}
				documentView.HorizontalResizeIndicatorIsVisible = false;
				if (RichTextEditor.Cursor == Cursors.SizeNS)
				{
					RichTextEditor.Cursor = null;
				}
				break;
			}
			case MouseActivityType.ResizeRectangle:
			{
				TableLayoutNode tableLayoutNode = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode;
				if (tableLayoutNode != null)
				{
					RichTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
					Point textAreaPoint = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint;
					double value = position.X - textAreaPoint.X;
					double value2 = position.Y - textAreaPoint.Y;
					CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode.ResizeTable(new Extent(value, ExtentUnitType.LogicalPixels), new Extent(value2, ExtentUnitType.LogicalPixels));
				}
				DocumentView.RectangleResizeIndicatorIsVisible = false;
				if (RichTextEditor.Cursor == Cursors.SizeNWSE)
				{
					RichTextEditor.Cursor = null;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			_lastMouseDownHitTestInfo = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Clone();
			_lastMouseActivityType = CurrentMouseActivityType;
			CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.None;
			if (documentView.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition && Selection.Length > 0)
			{
				int start = Selection.Start;
				int end = Selection.End;
				documentView.ForceImeCompositionEnd();
				Selection.SetRange(start, end);
			}
			ClearMouseDownHitTestInfo();
			ClearMouseMoveHitTestInfo();
			ClearMouseUpHitTestInfo();
		}

		internal void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_lastMouseMoveEventArgs = e;
			if (RichTextEditor == null || RichTextEditor.Document == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Point position = e.GetPosition(documentView.Presenter.TextArea);
			RichTextHitTestInfo richTextHitTestInfo = CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.Clone();
			CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.Update(documentView.LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(position), position);
			RichTextHitTestInfo currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo = CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			_ = documentView.VerticalViewOffset;
			if (currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.Hits != null)
			{
				ProcessMouseMoveForHyperlink(currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.Hits[0], e);
			}
			if (!_isMouseDown)
			{
				if (currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.HasHits)
				{
					documentView.SetCursor(currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.Hits[0].Area, e);
				}
				else
				{
					documentView.SetCursor(RichTextHitTestResultArea.Unknown, e);
				}
				return;
			}
			TotalMouseDownMouseMoves++;
			switch (CurrentMouseActivityType)
			{
			case MouseActivityType.ResizeVerticalEdge:
			case MouseActivityType.ResizeHorizontalEdge:
				if (CurrentMouseActivityType == MouseActivityType.ResizeVerticalEdge)
				{
					documentView.VerticalResizeIndicatorXOffset = Math.Max(_currentResizeConstraint.MinX, Math.Min(_currentResizeConstraint.MaxX, position.X + _verticalResizeEdgeOffset));
				}
				else
				{
					documentView.HorizontalResizeIndicatorYOffset = Math.Max(_currentResizeConstraint.MinY, Math.Min(_currentResizeConstraint.MaxY, position.Y));
				}
				break;
			case MouseActivityType.ResizeRectangle:
				documentView.RectangleResizeIndicatorRect = new Rect(_mouseDownResizeRect.Left, _mouseDownResizeRect.Top, Math.Max(_currentResizeConstraint.MinX, Math.Min(_currentResizeConstraint.MaxX, position.X)) - _mouseDownResizeRect.Left, Math.Max(_currentResizeConstraint.MinY, Math.Min(_currentResizeConstraint.MaxY, position.Y)) - _mouseDownResizeRect.Top);
				break;
			default:
			{
				if (_mouseMoveTriggeredByMouseCapture)
				{
					_mouseMoveTriggeredByMouseCapture = false;
					return;
				}
				RichTextHitTestInfo currentMouseDownHitTestInfo = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo;
				_ = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode;
				_ = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode;
				_ = currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.TableNode;
				int? num = selection.ProcessIntraTableSelection(SelectionActivityTypeFromMouseActivityType(CurrentMouseActivityType), currentMouseDownHitTestInfo, currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo, null, null);
				if (!num.HasValue)
				{
					break;
				}
				int value = num.Value;
				RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = documentView.Presenter.TextArea;
				if (textArea.ContainsPoint(position))
				{
					bool flag = CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.X < richTextHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.X;
					if (AutoScrollTimer.IsEnabled)
					{
						AutoScrollTimer.Stop();
					}
					int? num2 = null;
					if (currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.TableNode != null && CurrentMouseActivityType != MouseActivityType.SelectInsideTableCell && CurrentMouseActivityType != MouseActivityType.MultiSelect)
					{
						num2 = currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.TableRowNode.GetDocumentOffset() + currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.TableRowNode.AggregateOffsetCount;
					}
					else
					{
						LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = documentView.LineFromTextAreaOffsetY(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.Y);
						LineElementProxy lineElementProxy2 = documentView.LineFromTextAreaOffsetY(position.Y);
						bool flag2 = false;
						bool flag3 = false;
						if (lineElementProxy != null)
						{
							flag2 = position.Y >= lineElementProxy.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates.Top && position.Y <= lineElementProxy.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates.Bottom;
							flag3 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.X >= lineElementProxy.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates.Left - 3.0 && currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.X <= lineElementProxy.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates.Right + 3.0;
						}
						bool includeParagraphMarks = true;
						if (flag2 && !flag3 && selection.Start == lineElementProxy.GetLineEndDocumentOffset())
						{
							includeParagraphMarks = false;
						}
						else if (!SelectionSpansLines && lineElementProxy2 == null)
						{
							includeParagraphMarks = false;
						}
						if (currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.IsInTable)
						{
							documentView.Selection.Start = value;
							documentView.Selection.SelectionAnchorOffset = value;
						}
						num2 = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(position, includeListNumbers: true, includeParagraphMarks);
					}
					if (num2.HasValue)
					{
						selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num2.Value);
						if (!flag)
						{
							selection.ExtendSelectionToIncludeEntireLineIfNecessary(num2.Value >= value);
						}
					}
					if (_receivedFirstMouseMove)
					{
						documentView.Caret.PreferredCaretOffsetX = position.X;
					}
				}
				else if (((textArea.ContainsY(position) && !textArea.ContainsX(position)) || textArea.IsPointAbove(position) || textArea.IsPointBelow(position)) && !AutoScrollTimer.IsEnabled)
				{
					AutoScrollTimer.Start();
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			_receivedFirstMouseMove = true;
		}

		internal void OnMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView.GetDoesTextAreaContainMouseClickPoint(e))
			{
				DocumentView.CloseContextMenuIfOpen();
			}
		}

		internal void OnMouseRightButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (documentView.GetDoesTextAreaContainMouseClickPoint(e))
			{
				Selection selection = documentView.Selection;
				int? num = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(e.GetPosition(documentView.Presenter.TextArea));
				if (num.HasValue && !selection.ContainsOffset(num.Value))
				{
					selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset = num.Value;
					selection.SetRange(num.Value, num.Value);
				}
				DocumentView.ShowContextMenu(e);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		internal void ProcessMouseDownPoint(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			bool flag = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control);
			bool flag2 = PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Shift);
			_isMouseDown = true;
			_receivedFirstMouseMove = false;
			TotalMouseDownMouseMoves = 0;
			Point position = e.GetPosition(documentView.Presenter.TextArea);
			CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Update(documentView.LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(position), position);
			RichTextHitTestInfo currentMouseDownHitTestInfo = CurrentMouseDownHitTestInfo;
			ClearTableSelectionAnchorHitTestInfos(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea);
			if (currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Hits != null)
			{
				ProcessMouseDownForHyperlink(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Hits[0], e);
			}
			switch (currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea)
			{
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellHorizontalResizeBorder:
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder:
			{
				if (RichTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder)
				{
					CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.ResizeVerticalEdge;
					documentView.VerticalResizeIndicatorIsVisible = true;
					_verticalResizeEdgeOffset = GetVerticalResizeEdgeOffset(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo);
					documentView.VerticalResizeIndicatorXOffset = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.X + _verticalResizeEdgeOffset;
					RichTextEditor.Cursor = Cursors.SizeWE;
				}
				else if (currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellHorizontalResizeBorder)
				{
					CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.ResizeHorizontalEdge;
					documentView.HorizontalResizeIndicatorIsVisible = true;
					documentView.HorizontalResizeIndicatorYOffset = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.Y;
					RichTextEditor.Cursor = Cursors.SizeNS;
				}
				TableCellLayoutNode tableCellLayoutNode = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode;
				if (tableCellLayoutNode != null)
				{
					double num = 5.0;
					double num2 = double.MaxValue;
					bool flag3 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellVerticalResizeBorder;
					Extent? extent = (flag3 ? tableCellLayoutNode.ParentTable.GetColumnMinWidth(tableCellLayoutNode.Column, tableCellLayoutNode.ColumnSpan) : tableCellLayoutNode.ParentTable.GetRowMinHeight(tableCellLayoutNode.Row));
					Extent? extent2 = (flag3 ? tableCellLayoutNode.ParentTable.GetColumnMaxWidth(tableCellLayoutNode.Column, tableCellLayoutNode.ColumnSpan) : new Extent(99999.0, ExtentUnitType.LogicalPixels));
					if (extent.HasValue)
					{
						num = extent.Value.LogicalPixels;
					}
					if (extent2.HasValue)
					{
						num2 = extent2.Value.LogicalPixels;
					}
					_currentResizeConstraint = new ResizeConstraint(tableCellLayoutNode.BorderBlock.OverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels + num, tableCellLayoutNode.BorderBlock.OverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels + num2, tableCellLayoutNode.BorderBlock.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels - documentView.VerticalViewOffset + num, num2);
				}
				else
				{
					_currentResizeConstraint = new ResizeConstraint(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				}
				break;
			}
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableResizeCorner:
				if (!RichTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved)
				{
					CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.ResizeRectangle;
					documentView.RectangleResizeIndicatorIsVisible = true;
					TableLayoutNode tableLayoutNode = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableLayoutNode;
					if (tableLayoutNode != null && tableLayoutNode.OverallRect.HasValue)
					{
						Rect value = tableLayoutNode.OverallRect.Value;
						documentView.RectangleResizeIndicatorRect = (_mouseDownResizeRect = new Rect(value.Left, value.Top - documentView.VerticalViewOffset, value.Width, value.Height));
						double logicalPixels = tableLayoutNode.GetTableMinWidth().LogicalPixels;
						double num3 = double.MaxValue;
						double logicalPixels2 = tableLayoutNode.GetTableMinHeight().LogicalPixels;
						double maxY = double.MaxValue;
						_currentResizeConstraint = new ResizeConstraint(tableLayoutNode.OverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels + logicalPixels, tableLayoutNode.OverallOffsetX.LogicalPixels + num3, tableLayoutNode.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels - documentView.VerticalViewOffset + logicalPixels2, maxY);
						RichTextEditor.Cursor = Cursors.SizeNWSE;
					}
					else
					{
						_currentResizeConstraint = new ResizeConstraint(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
					}
				}
				break;
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableColumnPrefixArea:
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.SelectTableColumn;
				if (_lastMouseActivityType == MouseActivityType.SelectTableColumn && (flag || flag2))
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						selection.AddColumnCellsToSelectionHelper(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode, currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode);
					}
					else if (_selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo.TableNode == currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode)
					{
						int column = _selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode.Column;
						int column2 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode.Column;
						if (column != column2)
						{
							NormalizedStartEnd normalizedStartEnd4 = new NormalizedStartEnd(column, column2);
							bool processBackwards3 = column2 < column;
							selection.AddCellsToSelectionHelper(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode.GetCellsInColumns(normalizedStartEnd4.Start, normalizedStartEnd4.Count, includeMergedCells: true), processBackwards3, replaceEntireSelection: true);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (_lastMouseActivityType != MouseActivityType.SelectTableColumn || (!flag && !flag2))
					{
						_selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Clone();
					}
					TableCellNode[] array2 = selection.AddColumnCellsToSelectionHelper(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode, currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode, processBackwards: false, replaceEntireSelection: true).ToArray();
					selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset = array2[0].GetDocumentOffset();
				}
				break;
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableRowPrefixArea:
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.SelectTableRow;
				if (_lastMouseActivityType == MouseActivityType.SelectTableRow && (flag || flag2))
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						selection.AddRowCellsToSelectionHelper(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode, currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode);
					}
					else if (_selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo.TableNode == currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode)
					{
						int row = _selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode.Row;
						int row2 = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode.Row;
						if (row != row2)
						{
							NormalizedStartEnd normalizedStartEnd3 = new NormalizedStartEnd(row, row2);
							bool processBackwards2 = row2 < row;
							selection.AddCellsToSelectionHelper(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode.GetCellsInRows(normalizedStartEnd3.Start, normalizedStartEnd3.Count, includeMergedCells: true), processBackwards2, replaceEntireSelection: true);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (_lastMouseActivityType != MouseActivityType.SelectTableRow || (!flag && !flag2))
					{
						_selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Clone();
					}
					selection.AddRowCellsToSelectionHelper(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode, currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode, processBackwards: false, replaceEntireSelection: true);
					selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableRowLayoutNode.DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset();
				}
				break;
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellMarginAreaLeft:
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.SelectTableCell;
				if (_lastMouseActivityType == MouseActivityType.SelectTableCell && (flag || flag2))
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						selection.AddCellsToSelectionHelper(new TableCellNode[1] { currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellNode }, processBackwards: false);
					}
					else if (_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo.TableNode == currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableNode)
					{
						NormalizedStartEnd normalizedStartEnd = new NormalizedStartEnd(_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode.Column, currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode.Column);
						NormalizedStartEnd normalizedStartEnd2 = new NormalizedStartEnd(_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode.Row, currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode.Row);
						bool processBackwards = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.GetDocumentOffset() < _selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo.GetDocumentOffset();
						selection.AddCellsToSelectionFromRectangularArea(normalizedStartEnd2.Start, normalizedStartEnd2.Count, normalizedStartEnd.Start, normalizedStartEnd.Count, _selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo.TableNode, processBackwards, replaceEntireSelection: true);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (_lastMouseActivityType != MouseActivityType.SelectTableCell || (!flag && !flag2))
					{
						_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo = currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.Clone();
					}
					TableCellNode[] array = new TableCellNode[1] { currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellNode };
					selection.AddCellsToSelectionHelper(array, processBackwards: false, replaceEntireSelection: true);
					selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset = array[0].GetDocumentOffset();
				}
				break;
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.ParagraphListIndicator:
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.SelectBullet;
				selection.SelectListIndicatorAtPoint(documentView.LayoutPointFromTextAreaPoint(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint));
				break;
			default:
				PerformDefaultMouseDownSelectionLogic(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo, selection, documentView);
				break;
			}
			_selectionChangedSinceMouseDown = false;
			documentView.KeyboardHandler.ResetCachedCharacterSettingsFromLastBackspace();
		}

		internal void RaiseSelectionChangedEvents()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (_lastEventSelectionRange != null && (Selection.Range.Start != _lastEventSelectionRange.Start || Selection.Range.End != _lastEventSelectionRange.End))
			{
				documentView.RaiseSelectionChanged(new RichTextSelectionChangedEventArgs(documentView));
				RichTextEditor.RaiseSelectionChanged(new RichTextSelectionChangedEventArgs(documentView));
			}
			documentView.Caret.UpdateVisibility();
			_lastEventSelectionRange = Selection.Range.Clone();
		}

		public void Redo()
		{
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			if (richTextEditor.Document != null && !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved)
			{
				UndoManager undoManager = richTextEditor.Document.UndoManager;
				if (undoManager.CanRedo)
				{
					undoManager.Redo();
					CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void SelectPageDown()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: false, select: true, restrictToCurrentView: false);
		}

		internal void SelectPageUp()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: true, select: true, restrictToCurrentView: false);
		}

		internal void SelectToBottomOfPage()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: false, select: true, restrictToCurrentView: true);
		}

		internal void SelectToTopOfPage()
		{
			MovePageUpDownHelper(up: true, select: true, restrictToCurrentView: true);
		}

		public void Undo()
		{
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			if (richTextEditor.Document != null && !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved)
			{
				UndoManager undoManager = richTextEditor.Document.UndoManager;
				if (undoManager.CanUndo)
				{
					undoManager.Undo();
