					CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
				}
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateSelectionWithCaretLocation()
		{
			int offset = DocumentView.Caret.DocumentOffset.Offset;
			Selection.SetRange(offset, offset);
		}

		private void ClearTableSelectionAnchorHitTestInfos(RichTextHitTestResultArea currentHitTestArea)
		{
			switch (currentHitTestArea)
			{
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableColumnPrefixArea:
				_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				_selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				break;
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableRowPrefixArea:
				_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				_selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				break;
			case RichTextHitTestResultArea.TableCellMarginAreaLeft:
				_selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				_selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				break;
			default:
				_selectTableColumnAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				_selectTableRowAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				_selectTableCellAnchorHitTestInfo = null;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void ClearMouseDownHitTestInfo()
		{
			_currentMouseDownHitTestInfo = null;
		}

		private void ClearMouseMoveHitTestInfo()
		{
			_currentMouseMoveHitTestInfo = null;
		}

		private void ClearMouseUpHitTestInfo()
		{
			_currentMouseUpHitTestInfo = null;
		}

		private int GetPixelsToScrollFromDistance(double distance)
		{
			if (distance < 30.0)
			{
				return 10;
			}
			if (distance < 50.0)
			{
				return 20;
			}
			if (distance < 70.0)
			{
				return 40;
			}
			if (distance < 100.0)
			{
				return 80;
			}
			return 160;
		}

		private double GetVerticalResizeEdgeOffset(RichTextHitTestInfo hitTestInfo)
		{
			ILayoutBlock layoutBlock = hitTestInfo.TableCellLayoutNode;
			while (!(layoutBlock.ParentBlock is TableRowLayoutNode) || layoutBlock.ParentBlock == null)
			{
				layoutBlock = layoutBlock.ParentBlock;
			}
			if (layoutBlock != null && layoutBlock.BorderRect.HasValue)
			{
				return layoutBlock.BorderRect.Value.Right - hitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint.X - 1.0;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private void InvokeUri(HyperlinkExecutingEventArgs args)
		{
			try
			{
				InvokeUriImpl(args);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				RichTextEditorUtilities.DisplayErrorMessage(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MSG_Title_ExceptionWhileExecutingHyperlink"), RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("MSG_Body_ExceptionWhileExecutingHyperlink", ex.Message, args.Uri));
			}
		}

		[SecurityCritical]
		private static void InvokeUriImpl(HyperlinkExecutingEventArgs args)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(args.WorkingDirectory))
			{
				Process.Start(args.Uri);
				return;
			}
			Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(args.Uri)
			{
				WorkingDirectory = args.WorkingDirectory
			});
		}

		private void MovePageUpDownHelper(bool up, bool select, bool restrictToCurrentView)
		{
			if (DocumentView == null || DocumentView.Document == null || DocumentView.VisibleLinesInMiddleLayerCount < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Caret caret = documentView.Caret;
			bool flag = false;
			Rect empty = Rect.Empty;
			LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = null;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			LineElementProxy[] visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray = documentView.GetVisibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray();
			if (up)
			{
				if (restrictToCurrentView && (int)caret.DocumentOffset == documentView.FirstVisibleDocumentOffset)
				{
					return;
				}
				lineElementProxy = visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray[0];
				if (lineElementProxy == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				empty = lineElementProxy.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates;
				flag = !documentView.IsFirstDocumentLineInView;
			}
			else
			{
				if (restrictToCurrentView && (int)caret.DocumentOffset == documentView.LastVisibleDocumentOffset)
				{
					return;
				}
				lineElementProxy = visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray[visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray.Length - 1];
				if (lineElementProxy == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				empty = lineElementProxy.BoundsInTextAreaCoordinates;
				flag = !documentView.IsLastDocumentLineInView;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				PageMoveCaretPositionOption pageMoveCaretPositionOption = PageMoveCaretPositionOption.PositionCaretAtPreferredOffsetX;
				if (up && empty.Contains(caret.PixelLocation))
				{
					pageMoveCaretPositionOption = PageMoveCaretPositionOption.PositionCaretAtZeroOffsetX;
				}
				else if (!up && empty.Contains(caret.PixelLocation))
				{
					pageMoveCaretPositionOption = PageMoveCaretPositionOption.PositionCaretAtMaxOffsetX;
				}
				int num = 0;
				switch (pageMoveCaretPositionOption)
				{
				case PageMoveCaretPositionOption.PositionCaretAtZeroOffsetX:
					num = lineElementProxy.GetLineStartDocumentOffset();
					break;
				case PageMoveCaretPositionOption.PositionCaretAtMaxOffsetX:
					num = lineElementProxy.GetLineEndDocumentOffset();
					break;
				case PageMoveCaretPositionOption.PositionCaretAtPreferredOffsetX:
				{
					int? num2 = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(new Point(caret.PreferredCaretOffsetX, lineElementProxy.TextAreaOffsetY));
					if (num2.HasValue)
					{
						num = num2.Value;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
				if (select)
				{
					selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num);
				}
				else
				{
					caret.SetDocumentOffset(num, syncSelection: true);
				}
				documentView.EnsureLineContainingOffsetIsFullyInView(num);
				return;
			}
			Point oldCaretTextAreaPoint = new Point(caret.PreferredCaretOffsetX, caret.PixelLocation.Y);
			if (up)
			{
				documentView.ScrollManager.ScrollPageUp();
			}
			else
			{
				documentView.ScrollManager.ScrollPageDown();
			}
			EventHandler<RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs> layoutChanged = null;
			layoutChanged = delegate
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				LineElementProxy firstVisibleLine = null;
				flag2 = (up ? documentView.GetIsFirstVisibleLineTheFirstDocumentLine(out firstVisibleLine) : documentView.GetIsLastVisibleLineTheLastDocumentLine(out firstVisibleLine));
				int? num3 = null;
				num3 = ((!flag2 || firstVisibleLine == null) ? documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(oldCaretTextAreaPoint) : (up ? new int?(firstVisibleLine.GetLineStartDocumentOffset()) : new int?(firstVisibleLine.GetLineEndDocumentOffset())));
				if (num3.HasValue)
				{
					if (select)
					{
						selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num3.Value);
					}
					else if (num3.HasValue)
					{
						documentView.Caret.SetDocumentOffset(num3.Value, syncSelection: true);
					}
					documentView.EnsureLineContainingOffsetIsFullyInView(num3.Value);
					documentView.VerifyCaretIsInViewAfterNextLayout();
				}
				documentView.LayoutChanged -= layoutChanged;
			};
			documentView.LayoutChanged += layoutChanged;
		}

		private void OnSelectionPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.PropertyName)
			{
			case "Start":
				if (Selection.IsSelectionInverted)
				{
					DocumentView.Caret.SetDocumentOffset(Selection.Start, syncSelection: false);
				}
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
				break;
			case "End":
				if (!Selection.IsSelectionInverted)
				{
					DocumentView.Caret.SetDocumentOffset(Selection.End, syncSelection: false);
				}
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
				break;
			case "Length":
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
				break;
			case "Range":
				RaiseSelectionChangedEvents();
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
				break;
			case "ListIndicatorSelections":
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
				break;
			case "CharacterSettings":
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
				break;
			}
			UpdateCommandsStatus();
			_selectionChangedSinceMouseDown = true;
		}

		private void OnAutoScrollTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			int num = 1;
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = documentView.Presenter.TextArea;
			Selection selection = Selection;
			Point currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates = CurrentMouseMoveHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint;
			bool mouseAboveTextArea = textArea.IsPointAbove(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			bool mouseBelowTextArea = textArea.IsPointBelow(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			bool num2 = textArea.IsPointLeft(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			bool flag = textArea.IsPointRight(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			if (mouseAboveTextArea)
			{
				num = GetPixelsToScrollFromDistance(Math.Abs(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y));
				documentView.ScrollManager.ScrollUp(num);
			}
			else if (mouseBelowTextArea)
			{
				num = GetPixelsToScrollFromDistance(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y - textArea.ActualHeight);
				documentView.ScrollManager.ScrollDown(num);
			}
			if (num2)
			{
				num3 = 3.0 * Math.Abs(textArea.AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates).X);
				documentView.ScrollManager.ScrollLeft(num3);
				int? num5 = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(new Point(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.X - num3, currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y));
				if (num5.HasValue)
				{
					int num6 = (selection.IsSelectionInverted ? selection.Start : selection.End);
					if (num5.Value != num6)
					{
						selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num5.Value);
						selection.ExtendSelectionToIncludeEntireLineIfNecessary(selection.SelectionAnchorOffset >= num5.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				LineElementProxy lineElementProxy = documentView.LineFromTextAreaOffsetY(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y);
				if (lineElementProxy != null)
				{
					double num7 = lineElementProxy.OverallWidth - textArea.ActualWidth;
					if (num7 > 0.0 && documentView.HorizontalViewOffset < num7)
					{
						num4 = 3.0 * (textArea.AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates).X - textArea.ActualWidth);
						documentView.ScrollManager.ScrollRight(num4);
						int? num8 = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(new Point(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.X + num4, currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y));
						if (num8.HasValue)
						{
							selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num8.Value);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			EventHandler<RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs> layoutChanged = null;
			layoutChanged = delegate
			{
				if (AutoScrollTimer.IsEnabled && _isMouseDown)
				{
					LineElementProxy[] visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray = documentView.GetVisibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray();
					if (mouseAboveTextArea)
					{
						int? num9 = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(new Point(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.X, 0.0));
						if (num9.HasValue)
						{
							selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num9.Value);
						}
					}
					else if (mouseBelowTextArea)
					{
						int? num10 = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(new Point(currentMouseMovePointInTextAreaCoordinates.X, visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray[visibleLinesInTextLayerAsArray.Length - 1].TextAreaOffsetY));
						if (num10.HasValue)
						{
							selection.SetSelectionEndUsingSelectionAnchor(num10.Value);
						}
					}
				}
				documentView.LayoutChanged -= layoutChanged;
			};
			if (mouseAboveTextArea || mouseBelowTextArea)
			{
				documentView.LayoutChanged += layoutChanged;
			}
		}

		private void PerformDefaultMouseDownSelectionLogic(RichTextHitTestInfo currentMouseDownHitTestInfo, Selection selection, RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			if ((currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.Paragraph || currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.HitTestArea == RichTextHitTestResultArea.Hyperlink) && currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TableCellNode != null)
			{
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.SelectInsideTableCell;
			}
			else
			{
				CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.Select;
			}
			selection.ClearSelectedCellsAndRows();
			int? num = documentView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(currentMouseDownHitTestInfo.TextAreaPoint);
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				selection.SelectionStartReferenceOffset = num.Value;
				if (IsMultiSelectActive)
				{
					CurrentMouseActivityType = MouseActivityType.MultiSelect;
					selection.StartMultiSelection(num.Value);
				}
				else
				{
					selection.SetRange(num.Value, num.Value);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ProcessMouseDownForHyperlink(RichTextHitTestResult hitTestResult, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.SetCursor(hitTestResult.Area, e);
			if (PresentationUtilities.IsModifierPressed(ModifierKeys.Control) && hitTestResult.Area == RichTextHitTestResultArea.Hyperlink)
			{
				_mouseDownHyperlinkNode = hitTestResult.LayoutNode.DocumentNode as HyperlinkNode;
			}
			HyperlinkToolTip.IsOpen = false;
		}

		private void ProcessMouseMoveForHyperlink(RichTextHitTestResult hitTestResult, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_isMouseDown)
			{
				HyperlinkToolTip.IsOpen = false;
				if (_selectionChangedSinceMouseDown)
				{
					_mouseDownHyperlinkNode = null;
				}
			}
			else if (hitTestResult.Area == RichTextHitTestResultArea.Hyperlink)
			{
				if (hitTestResult.LayoutNode is HyperlinkLayoutNode hyperlinkLayoutNode)
				{
					HyperlinkNode hyperlinkNode = hyperlinkLayoutNode.DocumentNode as HyperlinkNode;
					_hyperlinkToolTipPresenter = null;
					HyperlinkToolTipPresenter.ToolTipText = hyperlinkNode.TooltipResolved;
					HyperlinkToolTipPresenter.ToolTipPrompt = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("TT_HyperlinkPrompt");
					HyperlinkToolTip.Content = HyperlinkToolTipPresenter;
					HyperlinkToolTip.PlacementTarget = RichTextEditor;
					HyperlinkToolTip.Placement = System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.PlacementMode.Mouse;
					HyperlinkToolTip.IsOpen = true;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				HyperlinkToolTip.IsOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private SelectionActivityType SelectionActivityTypeFromMouseActivityType(MouseActivityType mouseActivityType)
		{
			return mouseActivityType switch
			{
				MouseActivityType.MultiSelect => SelectionActivityType.MultiSelect, 
				MouseActivityType.Select => SelectionActivityType.Select, 
				MouseActivityType.SelectBullet => SelectionActivityType.SelectBullet, 
				MouseActivityType.SelectInsideTableCell => SelectionActivityType.SelectInsideTableCell, 
				MouseActivityType.SelectTableCell => SelectionActivityType.SelectTableCell, 
				MouseActivityType.SelectTableColumn => SelectionActivityType.SelectTableColumn, 
				MouseActivityType.SelectTableRow => SelectionActivityType.SelectTableRow, 
				_ => SelectionActivityType.None, 
			};
		}

		private void UpdateCommandsStatus()
		{
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}
	}

	internal static class RichTextEditorUtilities
	{
		internal static int AdjustDocumentOffsetForNewLine(int documentOffset, RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			if (documentView.LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset, out var _, out var line))
			{
				ILayoutBlock layoutBlock2;
				IRichTextLine line2;
				bool num = documentView.LayoutBlockLineFromDocumentOffset(documentOffset - 1, out layoutBlock2, out line2);
				ILayoutNodeWithLines layoutNodeWithLines = layoutBlock2 as ILayoutNodeWithLines;
				if (num && layoutNodeWithLines != null)
				{
					int num2 = GetDocumentOffsetOfRichTextLine(line2, layoutBlock2 as ParagraphLayoutNode) + layoutNodeWithLines.GetDocumentOffsetCountOfLine(line2, includeListNumber: false, includeNewLine: false);
					if (line != line2 && documentOffset == num2)
					{
						return documentOffset - 1;
					}
				}
			}
			return documentOffset;
		}

		internal static int AdjustDocumentOffsetForParagraphMark(int documentOffset, RichTextDocument document)
		{
			int num = 0;
			DocumentSpan intersectSpan = new DocumentSpan(Math.Max(0, documentOffset - 1), 0);
			foreach (ParagraphNode paragraph in document.GetParagraphs(intersectSpan))
			{
				int num2 = paragraph.GetDocumentOffset() + paragraph.AggregateOffsetCount;
				if (documentOffset == num2)
				{
					num = 1;
					break;
				}
			}
			return documentOffset - num;
		}

		internal static Point AdjustPointForYCusp(Point point)
		{
			return new Point(point.X, point.Y + 1.0);
		}

		internal static void CreateAndSetBindingOnElement(object sourceObject, FrameworkElement targetElement, string sourceProperty, DependencyProperty targetProperty, BindingMode bindingMode)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding(sourceProperty);
			binding.Source = sourceObject;
			binding.Mode = bindingMode;
			targetElement.SetBinding(targetProperty, binding);
		}

		internal static void DisplayErrorMessage(string title, string message)
		{
			MessageBox.Show(message, title, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand);
		}

		internal static void DisplayStopwatchResult(TimeSpan ts, string resultMessage)
		{
			_ = $"{ts.Hours:00}:{ts.Minutes:00}:{ts.Seconds:00}.{ts.Milliseconds / 10:00}";
		}

		internal static bool DoublesAreClose(double value1, double value2)
		{
			if (value1 == value2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			double num = (Math.Abs(value1) + Math.Abs(value2) + 10.0) * 2.220446049250313E-16;
			double num2 = value1 - value2;
			if (0.0 - num < num2)
			{
				return num > num2;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static int EnsureOffsetIsValid(int documentOffset, RichTextDocument document)
		{
			int num = documentOffset;
			int aggregateOffsetCount = document.RootNode.AggregateOffsetCount;
			if (num >= aggregateOffsetCount)
			{
				num = aggregateOffsetCount - 1;
			}
			return Math.Max(0, num);
		}

		internal static FontWeight FontWeightFromIsBold(bool isBold)
		{
			if (!isBold)
			{
				return FontWeights.Normal;
			}
			return FontWeights.Bold;
		}

		internal static FontStyle FontStyleFromIsItalic(bool isItalic)
		{
			if (!isItalic)
			{
				return FontStyles.Normal;
			}
			return FontStyles.Italic;
		}

		internal static double GetContentElementHostOffsetYAdjustment(IContentElementHost contentElementHost, RichDocumentView documentView, bool subtractVerticalViewOffset = true)
		{
			if (contentElementHost is ContentElementClippingHost contentElementClippingHost)
			{
				double num = contentElementClippingHost.LayoutBlock.OverallOffsetY.LogicalPixels;
				if (subtractVerticalViewOffset)
				{
					num -= documentView.VerticalViewOffset;
				}
				return num;
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		internal static int GetDocumentOffsetOfRichTextLine(IRichTextLine line, ParagraphLayoutNode paragraph)
		{
			int indexOfLine = paragraph.GetIndexOfLine(line);
			int num = paragraph.DocumentNode.GetDocumentOffset() + paragraph.DocumentNode.PrefixOffsetCount;
			for (int i = 0; i < indexOfLine; i++)
			{
				IRichTextLine lineAtIndex = paragraph.GetLineAtIndex(i);
				num += paragraph.GetDocumentOffsetCountOfLine(lineAtIndex, includeListNumber: false, includeNewLine: true);
			}
			return num;
		}

		internal static RichDocumentView GetDocumentViewForElement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			RichDocumentViewPresenter visualAncestor = PresentationUtilities.GetVisualAncestor<RichDocumentViewPresenter>(element, null);
			if (visualAncestor == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(GetString("LE_RichDocumentViewPresenterNotFound", element.GetType().Name));
			}
			return visualAncestor.DocumentView;
		}

		internal static bool GetIsTableCellEmpty(TableCellLayoutNode tableCellLayoutNode)
		{
			return tableCellLayoutNode.DocumentNode.AggregateOffsetCount < 2;
		}

		internal static Size GetNonNanSize(Size preferredSize, Size defaultSize)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateIsNotInfinity(defaultSize.Width);
			CoreUtilities.ValidateIsNotNan(defaultSize.Width);
			CoreUtilities.ValidateIsNotInfinity(defaultSize.Height);
			CoreUtilities.ValidateIsNotNan(defaultSize.Height);
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			num = ((!double.IsNaN(preferredSize.Width) && !double.IsInfinity(preferredSize.Width)) ? preferredSize.Width : defaultSize.Width);
			num2 = ((!double.IsNaN(preferredSize.Height) && !double.IsInfinity(preferredSize.Height)) ? preferredSize.Height : defaultSize.Height);
			return new Size(num, num2);
		}

			return global::SR.GetString(name);

		{
			return global::SR.GetString(name, args);
		}

		internal static string InsertParagraphHelper(RichDocumentView documentView, RichTextDocument document, VersionedDocumentOffset caretDocumentOffset)
		{
			documentView.RichTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
			documentView.VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			documentView.MakeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout();
			EditorUndoTransaction editorUndoTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, caretDocumentOffset, null, null);
			document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(editorUndoTransaction);
			int documentOffset = document.AdjustDocumentOffsetForInput(caretDocumentOffset.Offset);
			documentView.Caret.SetDocumentOffset(documentOffset, syncSelection: true);
			documentView.VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			string error = string.Empty;
			document.InsertParagraph(caretDocumentOffset, out error, emulateUI: true);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
			{
				editorUndoTransaction.Commit();
			}
			else
			{
				editorUndoTransaction.Rollback();
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
			return error;
		}

		internal static void InsertTextHelper(RichDocumentView documentView, RichTextDocument document, VersionedDocumentOffset caretDocumentOffset, string text, CharacterSettings characterSettings, bool mergeWithExistingSettings, string runStyleId = null)
		{
			documentView.RichTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
			documentView.VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			documentView.MakeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout();
			EditorUndoTransaction editorUndoTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, caretDocumentOffset, null, null);
			document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(editorUndoTransaction);
			int documentOffset = document.AdjustDocumentOffsetForInput(caretDocumentOffset.Offset);
			documentView.Caret.SetDocumentOffset(documentOffset, syncSelection: true);
			CharacterSettings characterSettings2 = Infragistics.Documents.RichText.Utilities.MergeThreeLazy(null, documentView.Selection.PendingCharacterSettings, characterSettings);
			string error = string.Empty;
			document.InsertText(caretDocumentOffset.Offset, text, out error, characterSettings2, runStyleId, mergeWithExistingSettings);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
			{
				editorUndoTransaction.Commit();
			}
			else
			{
				editorUndoTransaction.Rollback();
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		internal static void LogDebuggerError(int level, string category, string message)
		{
			if (Debugger.IsAttached && Debugger.IsLogging())
			{
				Debugger.Log(level, category, message);
			}
		}

		internal static string PrependHttpToAddressIfAppropriate(string address)
		{
			if (address.Length > 6 && address.Substring(0, 7).ToLower() != "http://" && address.Length > 7 && address.Substring(0, 8).ToLower() != "https://")
			{
				return "http://" + address;
			}
			return address;
		}

		internal static void ReplaceTextHelper(RichDocumentView documentView, RichTextDocument document, VersionedDocumentOffset caretDocumentOffset, string text, DocumentSpan span, CharacterSettings characterSettings = null, string runStyleId = null)
		{
			documentView.RichTextEditor.StartUndoLogging();
			int aggregateOffsetCount = document.RootNode.AggregateOffsetCount;
			_ = span.Length;
			if (span.End <= aggregateOffsetCount || aggregateOffsetCount - span.Start > 0)
			{
				documentView.VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
				documentView.MakeCaretPositionPreferredAfterNextLayout();
				EditorUndoTransaction editorUndoTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, caretDocumentOffset, null, null);
				document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(editorUndoTransaction);
				string error = string.Empty;
				document.ReplaceContents(span, text, out error);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(error))
				{
					editorUndoTransaction.Commit();
				}
				else
				{
					editorUndoTransaction.Rollback();
				}
				CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
			}
		}

		internal static void SetRectangleClipIfNecessary(FrameworkElement element, Size elementSize)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (element.Clip == null)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			else
			{
				Rect rect = ((RectangleGeometry)element.Clip).Rect;
				if (rect.Width != elementSize.Width || rect.Height != elementSize.Height)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				RectangleGeometry rectangleGeometry = new RectangleGeometry();
				rectangleGeometry.Rect = new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), elementSize);
				element.Clip = rectangleGeometry;
			}
		}

		internal static void StartUndoLogging(this XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor)
		{
			if (richTextEditor.Document != null && !richTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved && richTextEditor.IsUndoEnabled)
			{
				richTextEditor.Document.StartUndoLogging();
			}
		}

		internal static Extent ValidateMaxExtent(Extent extent)
		{
			if (extent > XamRichTextEditor.ExtentMax)
			{
				return XamRichTextEditor.ExtentMax;
			}
			return extent;
		}

		internal static Size ValidateMaxSizeForMeasure(Size size)
		{
			return new Size(ValidateMaxDoubleForMeasure(size.Width), ValidateMaxDoubleForMeasure(size.Height));
		}

		internal static double ValidateMaxDoubleForMeasure(double doubleValue)
		{
			if (double.IsInfinity(doubleValue))
			{
				return XamRichTextEditor.ExtentMax.LogicalPixels;
			}
			if (doubleValue > XamRichTextEditor.ExtentMax.LogicalPixels)
			{
				return XamRichTextEditor.ExtentMax.LogicalPixels;
			}
			return doubleValue;
		}
	}

		private class DocumentChangeInfo
		{
			internal RichTextDocument OldDocument { get; set; }

			internal RichTextDocument NewDocument { get; set; }

			internal DocumentContentChangedEventArgs NewDocumentContentChangedEventArgs { get; set; }

			internal DocumentChangeInfo(RichTextDocument oldDocument, RichTextDocument newDocument)
			{
				OldDocument = oldDocument;
				NewDocument = newDocument;
			}
		}

		private struct LayoutManagerCacheKey : IEquatable<LayoutManagerCacheKey>
		{
			private RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle _layoutStyle;

			private RichTextDocument _document;

			internal RichTextDocument Document => _document;

			internal LayoutManagerCacheKey(RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle layoutStyle, RichTextDocument document)
			{
				_layoutStyle = layoutStyle;
				_document = document;
			}

			public override int GetHashCode()
			{
				return _layoutStyle.GetHashCode() ^ _document.GetHashCode();
			}

			public override bool Equals(object obj)
			{
				if (obj is LayoutManagerCacheKey)
				{
					return this == (LayoutManagerCacheKey)obj;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public static bool operator ==(LayoutManagerCacheKey item1, LayoutManagerCacheKey item2)
			{
				if (item1._layoutStyle == item2._layoutStyle)
				{
					return item1._document == item2._document;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public static bool operator !=(LayoutManagerCacheKey item1, LayoutManagerCacheKey item2)
			{
				return !(item1 == item2);
			}

			public bool Equals(LayoutManagerCacheKey other)
			{
				return this == other;
			}
		}

			get
			{

				{
					return true;
				}

					return Document.IsReadOnly;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

			}
		}

		private bool HasFocus
		{
			get
			{
				if (!base.IsFocused && !base.IsKeyboardFocused)
				{
					return base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		Rect[] IRichTextEditor.ViewPorts
		{
			get
			{
				int count = Views.Count;
				Rect[] array = new Rect[count];
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					array[i] = Views[i].GetOverallLayoutRectOfTextLayerViewport();
				}
				return array;
			}
		}

		static XamRichTextEditor()
		{
			_formatProvidersCompletedPreJit = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, bool>();
			_formatProvidersPendingPreJit = new ConcurrentQueue<SerializationProviderBase>();
			AcceptsReturnProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("AcceptsReturn", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, OnAcceptsReturnPropertyChanged));
			AcceptsTabProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("AcceptsTab", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, OnAcceptsTabPropertyChanged));
			ActiveDocumentViewPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("ActiveDocumentView", typeof(RichDocumentView), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), null, OnActiveDocumentViewPropertyChanged);
			ActiveDocumentViewProperty = ActiveDocumentViewPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			AllowDocumentViewSplittingProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("AllowDocumentViewSplitting", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnAllowDocumentViewSplittingPropertyChanged));
			CaretPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("Caret", typeof(Caret), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), null, null);
			CaretProperty = CaretPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CaretColorProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("CaretColor", typeof(ColorInfo), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(ColorInfo.Automatic, null));
			ClipboardFormatsProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("ClipboardFormats", typeof(string), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(string.Empty, OnClipboardFormatsPropertyChanged));
			ClipboardSerializationProvidersProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("ClipboardSerializationProviders", typeof(List<SerializationProviderBase>), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(null, OnClipboardSerializationProvidersPropertyChanged));
			DocumentProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("Document", typeof(RichTextDocument), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(null, OnDocumentPropertyChanged));
			HiddenSymbolDisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("HiddenSymbolDisplayMode", typeof(HiddenSymbolDisplayMode), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DoNotDisplay, OnHiddenSymbolDisplayModePropertyChanged));
			HorizontalScrollBarVisibilityProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("HorizontalScrollBarVisibility", typeof(ScrollBarVisibility), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(ScrollBarVisibility.Auto));
			InactiveDocumentViewPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("InactiveDocumentView", typeof(RichDocumentView), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), null, null);
			InactiveDocumentViewProperty = InactiveDocumentViewPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsDocumentViewSplitProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("IsDocumentViewSplit", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsDocumentViewSplitPropertyChanged));
			IsMultiSelectEnabledProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("IsMultiSelectEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsMultiSelectEnabledPropertyChanged));
			IsReadOnlyProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("IsReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.FalseBox, OnIsReadOnlyPropertyChanged));
			IsUndoEnabledProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("IsUndoEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.TrueBox, OnIsUndoEnabledPropertyChanged));
			SelectionPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("Selection", typeof(Selection), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), null, null);
			SelectionProperty = SelectionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			SelectionHighlightActiveBrushProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("SelectionHighlightActiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(102, 51, 153, byte.MaxValue)), OnSelectionHighlightActiveBrushPropertyChanged));
			SelectionHighlightInactiveBrushProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("SelectionHighlightInactiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(102, 201, 218, 227)), OnSelectionHighlightInactiveBrushPropertyChanged));
			TextUpdateModeProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("TextUpdateMode", typeof(RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert, OnTextUpdateModePropertyChanged));
			VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("VerticalScrollBarVisibility", typeof(ScrollBarVisibility), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(ScrollBarVisibility.Auto));
			ZoomLevelProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("ZoomLevel", typeof(double), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(1.0, OnZoomLevelPropertyChanged));
			ZoomLevelAsPercentagePropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("ZoomLevelAsPercentage", typeof(int), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), 100, null);
			ZoomLevelAsPercentageProperty = ZoomLevelAsPercentagePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CustomMarginVersionPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterReadOnly("CustomMarginVersion", typeof(int), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), 0, null);
			CustomMarginVersionProperty = CustomMarginVersionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			DocumentBackgroundProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("DocumentBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(DefaultDocumentBackgroundBrush));
			HorizontalMiniSplitterVisibilityProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("HorizontalMiniSplitterVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed));
			HorizontalSplitterVisibilityProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("HorizontalSplitterVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(XamRichTextEditor), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamRichTextEditor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(XamRichTextEditor)));
			FocusWithinManager.RegisterType(typeof(XamRichTextEditor), delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (false.Equals(e.NewValue))
				{
					XamRichTextEditor xamRichTextEditor = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
					if (xamRichTextEditor.Document != null)
					{
						xamRichTextEditor.Document.OnEditorLostFocus();
					}
				}
			});
			InitializeHandlersForFrameworkCommands();
			InputMethod.IsInputMethodSuspendedProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamRichTextEditor), new PropertyMetadata(OnInputMethodPropertyChanged));
			InputMethod.IsInputMethodEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(XamRichTextEditor), new PropertyMetadata(OnInputMethodPropertyChanged));
			ThemeManager.RegisterControl(typeof(XamRichTextEditor));
		}

		public XamRichTextEditor()
		{
			try
			{
				_license = LicenseManager.Validate(typeof(XamRichTextEditor), this) as UltraLicense;
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
			}
			base.Loaded += XamRichTextEditor_Loaded;
			InitializeCommanding();
			ClipboardSerializationProviders = new List<SerializationProviderBase>
			{
				PlainTextSerializationProvider.Instance,
				ImageSerializationProvider.Instance
			};
			ClipboardFormats = string.Join(",", "Rich Text Format", "Embed Source", "HTML Format", "UnicodeText", "System.String", "Text", "DeviceIndependentBitmap");
			base.UseLayoutRounding = true;
			_ = FindReplaceManager;
			Document = new RichTextDocument();
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (_measuredWithInfiniteHeight != double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height))
			{
				_measuredWithInfiniteHeight = double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height);
				VerifyMiniSplitterVisibility();
				UpdateScrollBarVisibilityAllViews();
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width))
			{
				availableSize = new Size(ExtentMax.LogicalPixels, availableSize.Height);
				if (!_infiniteWidthMessageDisplayed)
				{
					RichTextEditorUtilities.LogDebuggerError(1, RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("DBG_LayoutCategory"), RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("DBG_InfiniteWidthMeasurement"));
					_infiniteWidthMessageDisplayed = true;
				}
			}
			TriggerAsyncDocumentChangeProcessing();
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			UpdateDpiFactor();
			_richDocumentViewLayoutPanel = GetTemplateChild("RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel") as RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel;
			if (_richDocumentViewLayoutPanel == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel", typeof(RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel).Name, typeof(XamRichTextEditor).Name));
			}
			VerifySplitterVisibility();
			VerifyMiniSplitterVisibility();
			_richDocumentViewLayoutPanel.Initialize(this);
			if (ActiveDocumentView != null && ActiveDocumentView.HasPresenter)
			{
				foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
				{
					if (view.HasPresenter)
					{
						view.Presenter.RemoveAllMarginPresenters();
						view.ProcessTemplateChange();
						RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel.RemoveViewPresenter(view.Presenter);
						view.ClearPresenter();
						RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel.AddViewPresenter(view.Presenter);
						view.Presenter.InvalidateMeasure();
						if (view.IsActive && view.Presenter != null)
						{
							view.Presenter.Focus();
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_isLoaded)
			{
				VerifySplitStatus();
			}
			EnsureVisualStates();
			List<SerializationProviderBase> clipboardSerializationProviders = ClipboardSerializationProviders;
			if (clipboardSerializationProviders == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (SerializationProviderBase item in clipboardSerializationProviders)
			{
				if (item != null)
				{
					string fullName = item.GetType().FullName;
					if (!_formatProvidersCompletedPreJit.ContainsKey(fullName))
					{
						_formatProvidersPendingPreJit.Enqueue(item);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(PreJitFormatProvider), DispatcherPriority.Background);
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new XamRichTextEditorAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnMouseLeftControl();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ShouldZoom())
			{
				if (e.Delta > 0)
				{
					ZoomLevel = GetNormalizedZoomLevelValue(ZoomLevel += 0.2);
				}
				else
				{
					ZoomLevel = GetNormalizedZoomLevelValue(ZoomLevel -= 0.2);
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseWheel(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!AcceptsTab && e.Key == Key.Tab && Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Shift)
			{
				IInputElement focusedElement = Keyboard.FocusedElement;
				if (MoveFocus(this, next: false) && focusedElement != Keyboard.FocusedElement)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnGotKeyboardFocus(e);
			if (e.NewFocus != this && e.NewFocus != ActiveDocumentView.Presenter)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnFocusReceived();
			}
			SetFocusToActiveViewPresenter();
		}

		protected override void OnLostKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnLostKeyboardFocus(e);
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnFocusLost();
			}
		}

		private static void OnAcceptsReturnPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private static void OnAcceptsTabPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private static void OnActiveDocumentViewPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor xamRichTextEditor = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
			if (e.NewValue != null)
			{
				xamRichTextEditor.Selection = (e.NewValue as RichDocumentView).Selection;
			}
			xamRichTextEditor.RaiseActiveDocumentViewChanged(new ActiveDocumentViewChangedEventArgs(e.OldValue as RichDocumentView, e.NewValue as RichDocumentView));
			if (e.OldValue != null)
			{
				(e.OldValue as RichDocumentView).OnDeactivated();
			}
			if (e.NewValue != null)
			{
				(e.NewValue as RichDocumentView).OnActivated();
				xamRichTextEditor.VerifyInactiveDocumentView();
			}
			else
			{
				xamRichTextEditor.Caret = null;
				xamRichTextEditor.Selection = null;
			}
			if (xamRichTextEditor._performingInitialViewActivation)
			{
				if (xamRichTextEditor.HasFocus)
				{
					xamRichTextEditor.SetFocusToActiveViewPresenter();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				xamRichTextEditor.SetFocusToActiveViewPresenter();
			}
			xamRichTextEditor._performingInitialViewActivation = false;
		}

		private static void OnAllowDocumentViewSplittingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((XamRichTextEditor)d).VerifyMiniSplitterVisibility();
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		private static void OnClipboardFormatsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
		}

		private static void OnClipboardSerializationProvidersPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
		}

		private static void OnDocumentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor xamRichTextEditor = d as XamRichTextEditor;
			DocumentChangeInfo documentChangeInfo = new DocumentChangeInfo(e.OldValue as RichTextDocument, e.NewValue as RichTextDocument);
			if (xamRichTextEditor._richDocumentViewLayoutPanel != null)
			{
				xamRichTextEditor.ProcessDocumentChange(documentChangeInfo);
			}
			else
			{
				xamRichTextEditor._pendingDocumentChangeInfo = documentChangeInfo;
				if (e.NewValue is RichTextDocument richTextDocument)
				{
					richTextDocument.ContentChanged += xamRichTextEditor.OnPendingNewDocumentContentChanged;
				}
			}
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		private static void OnHiddenSymbolDisplayModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor obj = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
			obj.ForceViewsToRebuildLineContentOnNextLayoutPass();
			obj.RefreshAllViews();
		}

		private static void OnIsDocumentViewSplitPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor xamRichTextEditor = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
			bool flag = true;
			if (xamRichTextEditor._richDocumentViewLayoutPanel == null)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (!xamRichTextEditor.IsLoaded || !xamRichTextEditor.IsInitialized)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				xamRichTextEditor.VerifySplitStatus();
				xamRichTextEditor.RaiseSplitStatusChanged(new SplitStatusChangedEventArgs((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue));
			}
		}

		private static void OnIsMultiSelectEnabledPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in ((XamRichTextEditor)d).Views)
			{
				view.OnIsMultiSelectEnabledChanged((bool)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnIsReadOnlyPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((XamRichTextEditor)d).NotifyViewsOfDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange();
		}

		private static void OnIsUndoEnabledPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamRichTextEditor xamRichTextEditor = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
			if (!(bool)e.NewValue && xamRichTextEditor.Document != null)
			{
				xamRichTextEditor.Document.StopUndoLogging();
			}
		}

		{
			((XamRichTextEditor)d).InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private static void OnSelectionHighlightInactiveBrushPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((XamRichTextEditor)d).InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private static void OnTextUpdateModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = (XamRichTextEditor)d;
		}

		private static void OnZoomLevelPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((XamRichTextEditor)d).ZoomLevelAsPercentage = (int)((double)e.NewValue * 100.0);
		}

		public int? GetPositionFromPoint(Point point, bool snapToText)
		{
			if (Views.Count < 1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			RichDocumentView closestView = GetClosestView(point);
			if (closestView == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Point validTextAreaPointFromControlPoint = GetValidTextAreaPointFromControlPoint(point, closestView);
			if (snapToText)
			{
				return closestView.DocumentOffsetFromTextAreaPoint(validTextAreaPointFromControlPoint);
			}
			return closestView.DocumentOffsetFromPointInDocumentText(validTextAreaPointFromControlPoint);
		}

		internal static void SetLayoutTargetWidth(XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor, WebLayoutManager layoutManager, double layoutTargetWidth, bool? forceLayoutComplete = null)
		{
			layoutManager.LayoutTargetWidth = layoutTargetWidth;
			if (!forceLayoutComplete.HasValue)
			{
				forceLayoutComplete = ShouldLayoutDocumentSynchronously(richTextEditor.Document);
			}
			if (forceLayoutComplete.Value)
			{
				layoutManager.ValidateStructure();
				layoutManager.EnsureStatusIsComplete();
			}
		}

		internal void ClearCaretLayoutInfoCacheInAllViews()
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.ClearCaretLayoutInfoCache();
			}
		}

		internal static void ForceLayoutComplete(XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor, double layoutTargetWidth = 100.0)
		{
			if (GetCompatibleLayoutManager(RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle.WebLayout, richTextEditor) is WebLayoutManager layoutManager)
			{
				SetLayoutTargetWidth(richTextEditor, layoutManager, layoutTargetWidth, true);
			}
		}

		internal static Brush GetBrushFromColorInfo(ColorInfo colorinfo, RichTextDocument document, AutomaticColorUsageType automaticColorUsageType, Shading? resolvedBackgroundShading = null, Shading? characterShading = null)
		{
			Color resolvedColor = (colorinfo ?? ColorInfo.Automatic).GetResolvedColor(document.RootNode, automaticColorUsageType, AutomaticForegroundColorUtilities.GetBackgroundShading(resolvedBackgroundShading, characterShading));
			Brush value = null;
			if (BrushCache.TryGetValue(resolvedColor, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = new SolidColorBrush(resolvedColor);
			value.Freeze();
			BrushCache.Add(resolvedColor, value);
			return value;
		}

		internal static Brush GetBrushFromHighlightColor(HighlightColor? highlightColor)
		{
			if (!highlightColor.HasValue)
			{
				return null;
			}
			HighlightColor valueOrDefault = highlightColor.GetValueOrDefault();
			Brush value = null;
			if (HighlightBrushCache.TryGetValue(valueOrDefault, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			Color? color = Infragistics.Documents.RichText.ColorUtilities.ResolveHighlightColor(highlightColor);
			value = ((!color.HasValue) ? null : new SolidColorBrush(color.Value));
			value?.Freeze();
			HighlightBrushCache.Add(valueOrDefault, value);
			return value;
		}

		internal static Brush GetBrushFromShading(Shading? shading, RichTextDocument document, AutomaticColorUsageType automaticColorUsageType)
		{
			if (!shading.HasValue)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Color resolvedColor = shading.Value.Background.GetResolvedColor(document, automaticColorUsageType);
			Brush value = null;
			if (BrushCache.TryGetValue(resolvedColor, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = new SolidColorBrush(resolvedColor);
			value.Freeze();
			BrushCache.Add(resolvedColor, value);
			return value;
		}

		internal static LayoutManagerBase GetCompatibleLayoutManager(RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle layoutStyle, RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			LayoutManagerCacheKey key = new LayoutManagerCacheKey(layoutStyle, documentView.Document);
			XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			LayoutManagerBase value = null;
			if (richTextEditor.LayoutManagerCache.TryGetValue(key, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (layoutStyle == RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle.WebLayout)
			{
				value = new WebLayoutManager(LayoutProvider.MetricsProvider, documentView.Document, 0.0, richTextEditor);
				richTextEditor.LayoutManagerCache.Add(key, value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		internal static LayoutManagerBase GetCompatibleLayoutManager(RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle layoutStyle, XamRichTextEditor richTextEditor)
		{
			RichTextDocument document = richTextEditor.Document;
			LayoutManagerCacheKey key = new LayoutManagerCacheKey(layoutStyle, document);
			LayoutManagerBase value = null;
			if (richTextEditor.LayoutManagerCache.TryGetValue(key, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (layoutStyle == RichDocumentViewLayoutStyle.WebLayout)
			{
				value = new WebLayoutManager(LayoutProvider.MetricsProvider, document, 0.0, richTextEditor);
				richTextEditor.LayoutManagerCache.Add(key, value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		internal double GetSmallestTextAreaWidthInAllViews(RichDocumentView documentView, double width)
		{
			if (Views.Count < 2)
			{
				return width;
			}
			RichDocumentView richDocumentView = ((Views[0] == documentView) ? Views[1] : Views[0]);
			if (richDocumentView.Presenter != null)
			{
				RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = richDocumentView.Presenter.TextArea;
				if (textArea != null)
				{
					if (textArea.ActualWidth > 0.0)
					{
						return Math.Min(width, textArea.ActualWidth);
					}
					if (textArea.DesiredSize.Width > 0.0)
					{
						return Math.Min(width, textArea.DesiredSize.Width);
					}
				}
			}
			return width;
		}

		internal void OnSplitOperationEnded()
		{
			_currentActiveSplitterType = CurrentActiveSplitterType.None;
			VerifySplitterVisibility();
			VerifyMiniSplitterVisibility();
		}

		internal void OnSplitOperationStarted(bool isMiniSplit)
		{
			_currentActiveSplitterType = ((!isMiniSplit) ? CurrentActiveSplitterType.Regular : CurrentActiveSplitterType.Mini);
		}

		internal void RefreshAllViews()
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.RefreshLayersLayout();
			}
		}

		internal void SetFocusToActiveViewPresenter()
		{
			if (ActiveDocumentView != null && ActiveDocumentView.Presenter != null && !ActiveDocumentView.Presenter.HasFocusResolved)
			{
				ActiveDocumentView.Presenter.ForceFocus();
			}
		}

		internal static bool ShouldLayoutDocumentSynchronously(RichTextDocument document)
		{
			if (document != null && document.AggregateOffsetCount < 1000)
			{
				DocumentBodyNode documentBodyNode = ((document.RootNode != null) ? document.RootNode.Body : null);
				if (documentBodyNode != null)
				{
					foreach (NodeBase childNode in documentBodyNode.ChildNodes)
					{
						if (childNode is TableNode)
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal bool ShouldZoom()
		{
			if (BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
			{
				return (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Alt) > ModifierKeys.None;
			}
			return (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) > ModifierKeys.None;
		}

		internal void UpdateDpiFactor()
		{
			Extent.LogicalToDevicePixelFactor = 1.0 / Infragistics.Windows.Utilities.GetDeviceMatrix(toDevice: true, this).M11;
		}

		internal void UpdateScrollBarVisibilityAllViews(bool async = false)
		{
			if (async)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
					{
						view.UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars();
					}
				});
				return;
			}
			foreach (RichDocumentView view2 in Views)
			{
				view2.UpdateVisibilityOfScrollBars();
			}
		}

		private void document_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.PropertyName == "IsReadOnly")
			{
				NotifyViewsOfDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange();
			}
			if (e.PropertyName == "RootNode")
			{
				UpdateDocumentBackground();
			}
		}

		private void document_ContentChanged(object sender, DocumentContentChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnDocumentContentChanged(sender, e);
			}
			RaiseDocumentContentChanged(e);
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
			if (e.ChangeType == DocumentChangeType.Settings)
			{
				UpdateDocumentBackground();
			}
		}

		internal void EnsureCorrectNumberOfViewsForCurrentSplitStatus()
		{
			if (Document == null)
			{
				if (Views.Count > 0)
				{
					RemoveAllViews();
				}
				return;
			}
			int num = ((!IsDocumentViewSplit) ? 1 : 2);
			ObservableCollection<RichDocumentView> views = Views;
			if (views.Count > num)
			{
				while (views.Count > num)
				{
					RichDocumentView richDocumentView = views[0];
					RemoveView(richDocumentView);
					richDocumentView.OnUnloaded();
					RaiseViewUnloaded(new ViewEventArgs(richDocumentView));
				}
				if (views.Count == 0)
				{
					ActiveDocumentView = null;
				}
				else if (!views.Contains(ActiveDocumentView))
				{
					ActiveDocumentView = views[0];
				}
			}
			else
			{
				while (views.Count < num)
				{
					RichDocumentView richDocumentView2 = new RichDocumentView(this);
					richDocumentView2.ViewLocation = GetNextViewLocation();
					views.Insert(0, richDocumentView2);
					RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel.AddViewPresenter(richDocumentView2.Presenter);
					if (views.Count > 1)
					{
						richDocumentView2.InitializeFrom(views[views.Count - 1]);
					}
					if (views.Count == 1)
					{
						_performingInitialViewActivation = true;
						richDocumentView2.Activate();
					}
					richDocumentView2.Presenter.InvalidateMeasure();
					richDocumentView2.Presenter.UpdateLayout();
					richDocumentView2.ScrollManager.UpdateScrollBarValues();
					richDocumentView2.ScrollManager.UpdateScrollBarRanges();
					if (views.Count == 1 && HasFocus)
					{
						richDocumentView2.Presenter.Focus();
					}
					richDocumentView2.OnLoaded();
					RaiseViewLoaded(new ViewEventArgs(richDocumentView2));
				}
			}
			if (views.Count == 1)
			{
				RichDocumentView richDocumentView3 = views[0];
				if (richDocumentView3 != null)
				{
					richDocumentView3.ViewLocation = RichDocumentViewLocation.Center;
				}
			}
			else if (views.Count > 1)
			{
				RichDocumentView richDocumentView4 = views[0];
				if (richDocumentView4 != null)
				{
					richDocumentView4.ViewLocation = RichDocumentViewLocation.Upper;
				}
				richDocumentView4 = views[1];
				if (richDocumentView4 != null)
				{
					richDocumentView4.ViewLocation = RichDocumentViewLocation.Lower;
				}
			}
		}

		private void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
		}

		private void _findReplaceManager_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.PropertyName == "HighlightMatchesCount")
			{
				ForceViewsToRebuildLineContentOnNextLayoutPass();
			}
			RefreshAllViews();
		}

		private void ForceViewsToRebuildLineContentOnNextLayoutPass()
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.ForceLayoutProvidersToRebuildLineContentOnNextLayoutPass();
			}
		}

		private RichDocumentView GetClosestView(Point testPoint)
		{
			if (Views.Count == 1)
			{
				return Views[0];
			}
			Point point = Views[1].Presenter.TextAreaHostGrid.TransformToVisual(this).Transform(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			if (testPoint.Y < point.Y)
			{
				return Views[0];
			}
			return Views[1];
		}

		private RichDocumentViewLocation GetNextViewLocation()
		{
			if (!IsDocumentViewSplit)
			{
				return RichDocumentViewLocation.Center;
			}
			if (Views.Count != 0)
			{
				return RichDocumentViewLocation.Lower;
			}
			return RichDocumentViewLocation.Upper;
		}

		private double GetNormalizedZoomLevelValue(double zoomValue)
		{
			return Math.Min(4.0, Math.Max(0.2, zoomValue));
		}

		private Point GetValidTextAreaPointFromControlPoint(Point testPointInControlCoordinates, RichDocumentView view)
		{
			RichDocumentViewTextArea textArea = view.Presenter.TextArea;
			Point point = TransformToVisual(textArea).Transform(testPointInControlCoordinates);
			if (textArea.ContainsPoint(point))
			{
				return point;
			}
			Grid textAreaHostGrid = view.Presenter.TextAreaHostGrid;
			double num = point.X;
			if (num < 0.0)
			{
				num = 0.0;
			}
			else if (num > textAreaHostGrid.ActualWidth)
			{
				num = textAreaHostGrid.ActualWidth - 1.0;
			}
			double num2 = point.Y;
			if (num2 < 0.0)
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			else if (num2 > textAreaHostGrid.ActualHeight)
			{
				num2 = textAreaHostGrid.ActualHeight - 1.0;
			}
			return new Point(num, num2);
		}

		private void HookDocumentEvents(RichTextDocument document)
		{
			if (document != null)
			{
				document.PropertyChanged += document_PropertyChanged;
				document.ContentChanged += document_ContentChanged;
			}
		}

		private void InitializeCommanding()
		{
			CommandSourceManager.RegisterCommandTarget(this);
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
		}

		private static void InitializeHandlersForFrameworkCommands()
		{
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Cut, RichTextEditorCommandType.Cut);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Copy, RichTextEditorCommandType.Copy);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Paste, RichTextEditorCommandType.Paste);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Undo, RichTextEditorCommandType.Undo);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Redo, RichTextEditorCommandType.Redo);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Delete, RichTextEditorCommandType.Delete);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.ContextMenu, RichTextEditorCommandType.ContextMenu);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.AlignCenter, RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentCenter);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.AlignJustify, RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentJustified);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.AlignLeft, RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentLeft);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.AlignRight, RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentRight);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.DecreaseFontSize, RichTextEditorCommandType.ShrinkFont);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.IncreaseFontSize, RichTextEditorCommandType.GrowFont);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.DecreaseIndentation, RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseIndentLevel);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.IncreaseIndentation, RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseIndentLevel);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.Delete, RichTextEditorCommandType.Delete);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.DeleteNextWord, RichTextEditorCommandType.DeleteNextWord);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.DeletePreviousWord, RichTextEditorCommandType.DeletePreviousWord);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveDownByLine, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveDown);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveUpByLine, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveUp);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveDownByPage, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveDownByPage);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveUpByPage, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveUpByPage);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveRightByCharacter, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToNextCharacter);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveLeftByCharacter, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToPreviousCharacter);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveRightByWord, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToNextWord);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveLeftByWord, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToPreviousWord);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveToDocumentStart, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToStartOfDocument);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveToDocumentEnd, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToEndOfDocument);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveToLineStart, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToStartOfLine);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.MoveToLineEnd, RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToEndOfLine);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectUpByLine, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectUp);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectDownByLine, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectDown);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectDownByPage, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectDownByPage);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectUpByPage, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectUpByPage);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectRightByCharacter, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectNextCharacter);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectLeftByCharacter, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectPreviousCharacter);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectToDocumentStart, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToStartOfDocument);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectToDocumentEnd, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToEndOfDocument);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectToLineStart, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToStartOfLine);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.SelectToLineEnd, RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToEndOfLine);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleBold, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleBoldFormatting);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleItalic, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleItalicFormatting);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleUnderline, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSingleUnderlineFormatting);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleInsert, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleInsert);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleSubscript, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSubscript);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleSuperscript, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSuperscript);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.Backspace, RichTextEditorCommandType.Backspace);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleBullets, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleBullets);
			RegisterClassCommandBinding(EditingCommands.ToggleNumbering, RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleNumbering);
		}

		private static void RegisterClassCommandBinding(RoutedUICommand applicationCommand, RichTextEditorCommandType richTextEditorCommandType)
		{
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(XamRichTextEditor), new CommandBinding(applicationCommand, delegate(object o, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				((XamRichTextEditor)o).HandleFrameworkCommandExecute(richTextEditorCommandType, e);
			}, delegate(object o, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				((XamRichTextEditor)o).HandleFrameworkCommandCanExecute(richTextEditorCommandType, e);
			}));
		}

		private void HandleFrameworkCommandExecute(RichTextEditorCommandType richTextEditorCommandType, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			new RichTextEditorCommand(richTextEditorCommandType).Execute(this);
		}

		private void HandleFrameworkCommandCanExecute(RichTextEditorCommandType richTextEditorCommandType, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RichTextEditorCommand richTextEditorCommand = new RichTextEditorCommand(richTextEditorCommandType);
			e.CanExecute = richTextEditorCommand.CanExecute(this);
			if (!e.CanExecute)
			{
				e.ContinueRouting = true;
			}
		}

		private void NotifyViewsOfDocumentChange(RichTextDocument oldDocument, RichTextDocument newDocument)
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnDocumentChanged(oldDocument, newDocument);
			}
		}

		private void OnPendingNewDocumentContentChanged(object sender, DocumentContentChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_pendingDocumentChangeInfo != null)
			{
				_pendingDocumentChangeInfo.NewDocumentContentChangedEventArgs = e;
				_pendingDocumentChangeInfo.NewDocument.ContentChanged -= OnPendingNewDocumentContentChanged;
			}
		}

		private void NotifyViewsOfDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange()
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange();
			}
		}

		private void NotifyViewsOfImeStateChange()
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.OnImeStateChanged();
			}
		}

		private void OnActualHeightChanged()
		{
			UpdateScrollBarVisibilityAllViews();
		}

		private static void OnInputMethodPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((XamRichTextEditor)d).NotifyViewsOfImeStateChange();
		}

		private void ProcessDocumentChange(DocumentChangeInfo documentChangeInfo)
		{
			EnsureCorrectNumberOfViewsForCurrentSplitStatus();
			NotifyViewsOfDocumentChange(documentChangeInfo.OldDocument, documentChangeInfo.NewDocument);
			UnhookDocumentEvents(documentChangeInfo.OldDocument);
			HookDocumentEvents(documentChangeInfo.NewDocument);
			RemoveCachedLayoutManagersForDocument(documentChangeInfo.OldDocument);
			RaiseDocumentChanged(new DocumentChangedEventArgs(documentChangeInfo.OldDocument, documentChangeInfo.NewDocument));
			CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
			_lastDocumentBackgroundResolved = null;
			UpdateDocumentBackground();
			if (!IsUndoEnabled && documentChangeInfo.NewDocument != null)
			{
				documentChangeInfo.NewDocument.StopUndoLogging();
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
				{
					view.Caret.InitializeLocation();
				}
			});
		}

		private void RemoveAllViews()
		{
			ObservableCollection<RichDocumentView> views = Views;
			for (int num = views.Count - 1; num > -1; num--)
			{
				RemoveView(views[num]);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveCachedLayoutManagersForDocument(RichTextDocument document)
		{
			if (document == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			WeakDictionary<LayoutManagerCacheKey, LayoutManagerBase> layoutManagerCache = LayoutManagerCache;
			List<LayoutManagerCacheKey> list = new List<LayoutManagerCacheKey>();
			foreach (LayoutManagerCacheKey key in layoutManagerCache.Keys)
			{
				if (key.Document == document)
				{
					list.Add(key);
				}
			}
			foreach (LayoutManagerCacheKey item in list)
			{
				layoutManagerCache[item].Dispose();
				layoutManagerCache.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveView(RichDocumentView view)
		{
			view.Presenter.RemoveAllMarginPresenters();
			RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel.RemoveViewPresenter(view.Presenter);
			Views.Remove(view);
		}

		private void TriggerAsyncDocumentChangeProcessing()
		{
			if (_pendingDocumentChangeInfo == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Action ProcessPendingChangeInfo = delegate
			{
				if (_pendingDocumentChangeInfo != null)
				{
					ProcessDocumentChange(_pendingDocumentChangeInfo);
					DocumentContentChangedEventArgs newDocumentContentChangedEventArgs = _pendingDocumentChangeInfo.NewDocumentContentChangedEventArgs;
					_pendingDocumentChangeInfo = null;
					if (newDocumentContentChangedEventArgs != null)
					{
						RaiseDocumentContentChanged(newDocumentContentChangedEventArgs);
					}
				}
			};
			if (_richDocumentViewLayoutPanel != null)
			{
				ProcessPendingChangeInfo();
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				ProcessPendingChangeInfo();
			});
		}

		private void UnhookDocumentEvents(RichTextDocument document)
		{
			if (document != null)
			{
				document.PropertyChanged -= document_PropertyChanged;
				document.ContentChanged -= document_ContentChanged;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDocumentBackground()
		{
			if (Document == null)
			{
				ClearValue(DocumentBackgroundProperty);
				_lastDocumentBackgroundResolved = null;
				return;
			}
			ColorInfo backgroundResolved = Document.BackgroundResolved;
			if (backgroundResolved != _lastDocumentBackgroundResolved)
			{
				DocumentBackground = GetBrushFromColorInfo(backgroundResolved, Document, AutomaticColorUsageType.Background);
				_lastDocumentBackgroundResolved = backgroundResolved;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateScrollbarRangesForAllViews(bool async = false)
		{
			foreach (RichDocumentView view in Views)
			{
				view.ScrollManager.UpdateScrollBarRanges();
			}
		}

		private void VerifyInactiveDocumentView()
		{
			ObservableCollection<RichDocumentView> views = Views;
			if (views.Count > 1)
			{
				if (views[0] != ActiveDocumentView)
				{
					InactiveDocumentView = views[0];
				}
				else if (views[1] != ActiveDocumentView)
				{
					InactiveDocumentView = views[1];
				}
				else
				{
					InactiveDocumentView = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				InactiveDocumentView = null;
			}
		}

		private void VerifyMiniSplitterVisibility()
		{
			if (AllowDocumentViewSplitting && !_measuredWithInfiniteHeight)
			{
				if (IsDocumentViewSplit)
				{
					if (_currentActiveSplitterType != CurrentActiveSplitterType.Mini)
					{
						HorizontalMiniSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					HorizontalMiniSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
			else if (_currentActiveSplitterType != CurrentActiveSplitterType.Mini)
			{
				HorizontalMiniSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		private void VerifySplitStatus()
		{
			if (_richDocumentViewLayoutPanel != null && base.IsInitialized)
			{
				if (!_splitStatusSetInternally)
				{
					RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel.HorizontalSplitterPosition = 0.5;
				}
				_splitStatusSetInternally = false;
				VerifySplitterVisibility();
				VerifyMiniSplitterVisibility();
				EnsureCorrectNumberOfViewsForCurrentSplitStatus();
				CommandSourceManager.NotifyCanExecuteChanged(typeof(RichTextEditorCommand));
			}
		}

		private void VerifySplitterVisibility()
		{
			if (IsDocumentViewSplit)
			{
				HorizontalSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else if (_currentActiveSplitterType != CurrentActiveSplitterType.Regular)
			{
				HorizontalSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		private void _views_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			VerifyInactiveDocumentView();
		}

		private void XamRichTextEditor_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_isLoaded = true;
			base.Loaded -= XamRichTextEditor_Loaded;
			_actualHeightPropertyTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(this, "ActualHeight", OnActualHeightChanged);
		}

		private static RichTextDocument CreateDocForPreJittingFormatProviders()
		{
			RichTextDocument richTextDocument = new RichTextDocument();
			richTextDocument.AppendText("ABC", out var _);
			return richTextDocument;
		}

		private void PreJitFormatProvider()
		{
			if (!_formatProvidersPendingPreJit.TryDequeue(out var result))
			{
				return;
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(PreJitFormatProvider), DispatcherPriority.Background);
			string fullName = result.GetType().FullName;
			if (!_formatProvidersCompletedPreJit.TryAdd(fullName, value: false) || result is ImageSerializationProvider)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_docForPreJittingFormatProviders == null)
			{
				_docForPreJittingFormatProviders = CreateDocForPreJittingFormatProviders();
			}
			try
			{
				using MemoryStream stream = new MemoryStream();
				_docForPreJittingFormatProviders.Save(result, stream);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private static bool MoveFocus(IInputElement src, bool next)
		{
			return MoveFocus(src, (!next) ? FocusNavigationDirection.Previous : FocusNavigationDirection.Next);
		}

		private static bool MoveFocus(IInputElement src, FocusNavigationDirection direction)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = src as FrameworkElement;
			TraversalRequest request = new TraversalRequest(direction);
			if (frameworkElement != null)
			{
				return frameworkElement.MoveFocus(request);
			}
			if (src is FrameworkContentElement frameworkContentElement)
			{
				return frameworkContentElement.MoveFocus(request);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnActiveDocumentViewChanged(ActiveDocumentViewChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			this.ActiveDocumentViewChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		internal void RaiseActiveDocumentViewChanged(ActiveDocumentViewChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnActiveDocumentViewChanged(args);
		}

		}

		}

		}

		protected virtual void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuOpeningEventArgs args)
		{

		}

		}

		{

		}

		}

		protected virtual void OnDocumentChanged(DocumentChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			this.DocumentChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		internal void RaiseDocumentChanged(DocumentChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnDocumentChanged(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDocumentContentChanged(DocumentContentChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			this.DocumentContentChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		internal void RaiseDocumentContentChanged(DocumentContentChangedEventArgs args)

		}

		protected virtual void OnHyperlinkExecuting(HyperlinkExecutingEventArgs args)
		{
			this.HyperlinkExecuting?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		{

		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged(RichTextSelectionChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			this.SelectionChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		{

		}

		{
			this.SplitStatusChanged?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		{

		}

		{
			this.ViewLoaded?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		{
