		}

		protected virtual void OnViewUnloaded(ViewEventArgs args)
		{
			this.ViewUnloaded?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		internal void RaiseViewUnloaded(ViewEventArgs args)
		{
			OnViewUnloaded(args);
		}

		object ICommandTarget.GetParameter(CommandSource source)
		{
			if (source.Command is RichTextEditorCommand)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		bool ICommandTarget.SupportsCommand(ICommand command)
		{
			if (command is CommandBase { CommandSource: not null } commandBase && commandBase.CommandSource.Parameter != null)
			{
				return commandBase.CommandSource.Parameter == this;
			}
			return command is RichTextEditorCommand;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Controls.Editors.Primitives
{
	public class RichTextEditorCommand : CommandBase
	{
		private RichTextEditorCommandType _commandType;

		public RichTextEditorCommand(RichTextEditorCommandType commandType)
		{
			_commandType = commandType;
		}

		public override bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (parameter is XamRichTextEditor xamRichTextEditor)
			{
				if (xamRichTextEditor.Document == null || xamRichTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				bool isReadOnlyResolved = xamRichTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved;
				ViewSelectionManager selectionManager = xamRichTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView.SelectionManager;
				Selection selection = selectionManager.Selection;
				RichTextDocument document = xamRichTextEditor.Document;
				switch (_commandType)
				{
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Cut:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						return selectionManager.Selection.Length > 0;
					}
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Copy:
					return selectionManager.Selection.Length > 0;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Paste:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						return selection.CanPaste();
					}
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Undo:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						return document.UndoManager.CanUndo;
					}
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Redo:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						return document.UndoManager.CanRedo;
					}
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SplitEditArea:
					return !xamRichTextEditor.IsDocumentViewSplit;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveSplitFromEditArea:
					return xamRichTextEditor.IsDocumentViewSplit;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Delete:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DeleteNextWord:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						int aggregateOffsetCount = document.AggregateOffsetCount;
						if (aggregateOffsetCount > 0)
						{
							return (int)xamRichTextEditor.Caret.DocumentOffset < aggregateOffsetCount;
						}
						return false;
					}
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToNextCharacter:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToNextWord:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectDown:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectDownByPage:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectNextCharacter:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectNextWord:
				{
					int aggregateOffsetCount2 = document.AggregateOffsetCount;
					if (aggregateOffsetCount2 > 0)
					{
						return (int)xamRichTextEditor.Caret.DocumentOffset < aggregateOffsetCount2;
					}
					return false;
				}
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DeletePreviousWord:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						if (document.AggregateOffsetCount > 0)
						{
							return (int)xamRichTextEditor.Caret.DocumentOffset > 0;
						}
						return false;
					}
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToPreviousCharacter:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToPreviousWord:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectUp:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectUpByPage:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectPreviousCharacter:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectPreviousWord:
					if (document.AggregateOffsetCount > 0)
					{
						return (int)xamRichTextEditor.Caret.DocumentOffset > 0;
					}
					return false;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseListIndentLevel:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseListIndentLevel:
					if (!isReadOnlyResolved)
					{
						return xamRichTextEditor.ActiveDocumentView.GetIsDocumentOffsetInList(selection.Start);
					}
					return false;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.GrowFont:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ShrinkFont:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseIndentLevel:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseIndentLevel:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseParagraphOrListIndentLevel:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseParagraphOrListIndentLevel:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplyBoldFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveBoldFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleBoldFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplyItalicFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveItalicFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleItalicFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplySingleStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplyDoubleStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveSingleStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveDoubleStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSingleStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleDoubleStrikethroughFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSingleUnderlineFormatting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentLeft:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentRight:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentCenter:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentJustified:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleInsert:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSubscript:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSuperscript:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Backspace:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleBullets:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleNumbering:
					return !isReadOnlyResolved;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AllowEditAreaSplitting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DoNotAllowEditAreaSplitting:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DisplayHiddenSymbols:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.HideHiddenSymbols:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectAll:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ContextMenu:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveDown:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveUp:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveDownByPage:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveUpByPage:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToStartOfDocument:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToEndOfDocument:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToStartOfLine:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToEndOfLine:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToStartOfDocument:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToEndOfDocument:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToStartOfLine:
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToEndOfLine:
					return true;
				}
				if (base.CommandSource != null)
				{
					base.CommandSource.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			if (parameter is XamRichTextEditor { Document: var document, ActiveDocumentView: var activeDocumentView } xamRichTextEditor)
			{
				if (document == null || activeDocumentView == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				ViewSelectionManager selectionManager = activeDocumentView.SelectionManager;
				Selection selection = selectionManager.Selection;
				switch (_commandType)
				{
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Cut:
					selection.Cut();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Copy:
					selection.Copy();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Paste:
					selection.Paste();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Undo:
					selectionManager.Undo();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Redo:
					selectionManager.Redo();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SplitEditArea:
					xamRichTextEditor.IsDocumentViewSplit = true;
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveSplitFromEditArea:
					xamRichTextEditor.IsDocumentViewSplit = false;
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AllowEditAreaSplitting:
					xamRichTextEditor.AllowDocumentViewSplitting = true;
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DoNotAllowEditAreaSplitting:
					xamRichTextEditor.AllowDocumentViewSplitting = false;
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.HideHiddenSymbols:
					xamRichTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode = HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DoNotDisplay;
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DisplayHiddenSymbols:
					xamRichTextEditor.HiddenSymbolDisplayMode = HiddenSymbolDisplayMode.DisplayAllHiddenSymbols;
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.GrowFont:
					selection.GrowFont();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ShrinkFont:
					selection.ShrinkFont();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseIndentLevel:
					selection.IncreaseIndent();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseIndentLevel:
					selection.DecreaseIndent();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseListIndentLevel:
					selection.IncreaseListIndent();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseListIndentLevel:
					selection.DecreaseListIndent();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.IncreaseParagraphOrListIndentLevel:
					selection.IncreaseParagraphOrListIndent();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.DecreaseParagraphOrListIndentLevel:
					selection.DecreaseParagraphOrListIndent();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplyBoldFormatting:
					selection.ApplyBoldFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveBoldFormatting:
					selection.RemoveBoldFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleBoldFormatting:
					selection.ToggleBoldFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplyItalicFormatting:
					selection.ApplyItalicFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveItalicFormatting:
					selection.RemoveItalicFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleItalicFormatting:
					selection.ToggleItalicFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplySingleStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.ApplySingleStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ApplyDoubleStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.ApplyDoubleStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.RemoveStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveSingleStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.RemoveSingleStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.RemoveDoubleStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.RemoveStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSingleStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.ToggleSingleStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleDoubleStrikethroughFormatting:
					selection.ToggleDoubleStrikethroughFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSingleUnderlineFormatting:
					selection.ToggleSingleUnderlineFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentLeft:
					selection.AlignParagraphContentLeft();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentRight:
					selection.AlignParagraphContentRight();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentCenter:
					selection.AlignParagraphContentCenter();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.AlignParagraphContentJustified:
					selection.AlignParagraphContentJustified();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectAll:
					selection.SelectAll();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Delete:
					selection.DeleteCurrentCharacter();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ContextMenu:
					activeDocumentView.ShowContextMenu();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveUp:
					selection.MoveUp();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveDown:
					selection.MoveDown();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveUpByPage:
					selectionManager.MovePageUp();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveDownByPage:
					selectionManager.MovePageDown();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToNextCharacter:
					selection.MoveToNextCharacter();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToPreviousCharacter:
					selection.MoveToPreviousCharacter();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToNextWord:
					selection.MoveToNextWord();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToPreviousWord:
					selection.MoveToPreviousWord();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToStartOfDocument:
					selection.MoveToStartOfDocument();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToEndOfDocument:
					selection.MoveToEndOfDocument();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToStartOfLine:
					selection.MoveToStartOfLine();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.MoveToEndOfLine:
					selection.MoveToEndOfLine();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectUp:
					selection.SelectUp();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectDown:
					selection.SelectDown();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectUpByPage:
					selectionManager.SelectPageUp();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectDownByPage:
					selectionManager.SelectPageDown();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectNextCharacter:
					selection.SelectNextCharacter();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectPreviousCharacter:
					selection.SelectPreviousCharacter();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectNextWord:
					selection.SelectNextWord();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectPreviousWord:
					selection.SelectPreviousWord();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToStartOfDocument:
					selection.SelectToStartOfDocument();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToEndOfDocument:
					selection.SelectToEndOfDocument();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToStartOfLine:
					selection.SelectToStartOfLine();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.SelectToEndOfLine:
					selection.SelectToEndOfLine();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleInsert:
					xamRichTextEditor.TextUpdateMode = ((xamRichTextEditor.TextUpdateMode == RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert) ? RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Overwrite : RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert);
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSubscript:
					selection.ToggleSubscriptFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleSuperscript:
					selection.ToggleSuperscriptFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.Backspace:
					activeDocumentView.KeyboardHandler.SimulateBackspace();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleBullets:
					selection.ToggleBulletFormatting();
					break;
				case RichTextEditorCommandType.ToggleNumbering:
					selection.ToggleNumberFormatting();
					break;
				}
				if (base.CommandSource != null)
				{
					base.CommandSource.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			base.Execute(parameter);
		}
	}
	public class RichTextEditorCommandSource : CommandSource
	{
		public RichTextEditorCommandType CommandType { get; set; }

		protected override ICommand ResolveCommand()
		{
			return new RichTextEditorCommand(CommandType);
		}
	}
	public class AdornmentLayer : Panel
	{
		private AdornmentLayerInfo _adornmentLayerInfo;

		private List<AdornmentInfo> _adornments;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey AdornmentInfoPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("AdornmentInfo", typeof(AdornmentInfo), typeof(AdornmentLayer), null, null);

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty AdornmentInfoProperty = AdornmentInfoPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSizePropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSize", typeof(bool), typeof(AdornmentLayer), KnownBoxes.FalseBox, null);

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSizeProperty = ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSizePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; private set; }

		public string Key => _adornmentLayerInfo.Key;

		internal AdornmentLayerInfo AdornmentLayerInfo => _adornmentLayerInfo;

		internal XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; private set; }

		private List<AdornmentInfo> Adornments
		{
			get
			{
				if (_adornments == null)
				{
					_adornments = new List<AdornmentInfo>();
				}
				return _adornments;
			}
		}

		internal AdornmentLayer(RichDocumentView documentView, AdornmentLayerInfo adornmentLayerInfo)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(adornmentLayerInfo, "adornmentLayerInfo");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			_adornmentLayerInfo = adornmentLayerInfo;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			int count = children.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[i];
				AdornmentInfo adornmentInfo = GetAdornmentInfo(uIElement);
				Rect finalRect = ((adornmentInfo == null) ? ((!ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSize(uIElement)) ? new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), uIElement.DesiredSize) : new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), finalSize)) : ((!ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSize(uIElement)) ? new Rect(adornmentInfo.PixelLocation, uIElement.DesiredSize) : new Rect(adornmentInfo.PixelLocation, finalSize)));
				uIElement.Arrange(finalRect);
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (Key == "BackgroundContent")
			{
				base.Background = RichTextEditor.Background;
			}
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			int count = children.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				children[i].Measure(availableSize);
			}
			return availableSize;
		}

		internal static AdornmentInfo GetAdornmentInfo(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (AdornmentInfo)d.GetValue(AdornmentInfoProperty);
		}

		internal static bool ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSize(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (bool)d.GetValue(ShouldArrangeAdornmentWithFinalSizeProperty);
		}

		public AdornmentInfo AddAdornment(UIElement adornmentElement, Point adornmentLocation, object userData)
		{
			AdornmentInfo adornmentInfo = new AdornmentInfo
			{
				Element = adornmentElement,
				PixelLocation = adornmentLocation,
				UserData = userData
			};
			adornmentElement.SetValue(AdornmentInfoPropertyKey, adornmentInfo);
			base.Children.Add(adornmentElement);
			Adornments.Add(adornmentInfo);
			adornmentInfo.PropertyChanged += adornmentInfo_PropertyChanged;
			return adornmentInfo;
		}

		public bool RemoveAdornment(AdornmentInfo adornmentInfo)
		{
			if (Adornments.Contains(adornmentInfo))
			{
				base.Children.Remove(adornmentInfo.Element);
				Adornments.Remove(adornmentInfo);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void adornmentInfo_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.PropertyName == "PixelLocation")
			{
				InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}
	}
	internal class BackgroundContentAdornmentLayer : ContentAdornmentLayerBase
	{
		internal override LayoutPlane LayoutPlane => LayoutPlane.Background;

		internal BackgroundContentAdornmentLayer(RichDocumentView documentView, AdornmentLayerInfo adornmentLayerInfo)
			: base(documentView, adornmentLayerInfo)
		{
		}
	}
	internal abstract class ContentAdornmentLayerBase : AdornmentLayer, IContentElementHost
	{
		private VisualCollection _childVisuals;

		private BorderRenderingSurface _borderRenderingSurface;

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount => _childVisuals.Count + 1;

		internal abstract LayoutPlane LayoutPlane { get; }

		private LayoutProvider LayoutProvider => base.DocumentView.GetLayoutProviderForContentElementHost(this, LayoutPlane);

		bool IContentElementHost.IsRootContentElementHost => true;

		LayoutPlane IContentElementHost.LayoutPlane => LayoutPlane;

		LayoutProvider IContentElementHost.LayoutProvider => LayoutProvider;

		internal ContentAdornmentLayerBase(RichDocumentView documentView, AdornmentLayerInfo adornmentLayerInfo)
			: base(documentView, adornmentLayerInfo)
		{
			_childVisuals = new VisualCollection(this);
			_borderRenderingSurface = new BorderRenderingSurface(this);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			Size result = LayoutProvider.ArrangeContentElements(finalSize);
			if (LayoutPlane == LayoutPlane.Middle)
			{
				UpdateCaretAndSelectionSnapshotPoints();
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs args = new RichDocumentViewLayoutChangedEventArgs();
					base.DocumentView.RaiseLayoutChanged(args);
				});
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > _childVisuals.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			if (index == _childVisuals.Count)
			{
				return _borderRenderingSurface;
			}
			return _childVisuals[index];
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size size = LayoutProvider.CreateAndMeasureContentElements(availableSize);
			_borderRenderingSurface.InvalidateVisual();
			_borderRenderingSurface.Measure(size);
			return size;
		}

		private void UpdateCaretAndSelectionSnapshotPoints()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = base.DocumentView;
			if (documentView != null)
			{
				documentView.Caret.TranslateDocumentOffsetToCurrentVersion(OffsetTrackingMode.Positive);
				documentView.Selection.EnsureAllRangesAreCurrent(raisePropertyChangeNotification: false);
			}
		}

		void IContentElementHost.AddChild(DependencyObject child)
		{
			_childVisuals.Add(child as Visual);
		}

		void IContentElementHost.InsertChild(int index, DependencyObject child)
		{
			_childVisuals.Insert(index, child as Visual);
		}

		void IContentElementHost.ClearChildren()
		{
			_childVisuals.Clear();
		}

		void IContentElementHost.RemoveChild(DependencyObject child)
		{
			_childVisuals.Remove(child as Visual);
		}

		IEnumerable<Visual> IContentElementHost.GetLineVisuals()
		{
			VisualCollection.Enumerator enumerator = _childVisuals.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				yield return enumerator.Current;
			}
			yield return _borderRenderingSurface;
		}
	}

		internal ForegroundContentAdornmentLayer(RichDocumentView documentView, AdornmentLayerInfo adornmentLayerInfo)
			: base(documentView, adornmentLayerInfo)

	internal class MiddleContentAdornmentLayer : ContentAdornmentLayerBase

		internal MiddleContentAdornmentLayer(RichDocumentView documentView, AdornmentLayerInfo adornmentLayerInfo)
			: base(documentView, adornmentLayerInfo)
		{
		}
	}
	internal class BorderRenderingSurface : Control
	{
		private WeakDictionary<string, Pen> _penCache;

		private Point _lastWindowOffset;

		private IContentElementHost ContentElementHost { get; set; }

		internal BorderRenderingSurface(IContentElementHost contentElementHost)
		{
			ContentElementHost = contentElementHost;
			_penCache = new WeakDictionary<string, Pen>(manageKeysAsWeakReferences: false, manageValuesAsWeakReferences: true, 10);
			base.LayoutUpdated += OnLayoutUpdated;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width) || double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height))
			{
				return new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			return RichTextEditorUtilities.GetNonNanSize(availableSize, availableSize);
		}

		protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			base.OnRender(drawingContext);
			LayoutProvider layoutProvider = ContentElementHost.LayoutProvider;
			ProcessBorderElementProxys(layoutProvider.BorderElementProxysVertical, drawingContext);
			ProcessBorderElementProxys(layoutProvider.BorderElementProxysHorizontal, drawingContext);
			ProcessBorderElementProxys(layoutProvider.UnderlineStrikethroughElementProxys, drawingContext);
		}

		private void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Point windowOffset = GetWindowOffset();
			windowOffset = new Point(Extent.CalculateDpiOffsetAdjustment(windowOffset.X, alwaysRoundUp: true), Extent.CalculateDpiOffsetAdjustment(windowOffset.Y, alwaysRoundUp: true));
			if (windowOffset != _lastWindowOffset)
			{
				_lastWindowOffset = windowOffset;
				InvalidateVisual();
			}
		}

		private void ProcessBorderElementProxys(List<BorderElementProxy> borderElementProxys, DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			foreach (BorderElementProxy borderElementProxy in borderElementProxys)
			{
				Brush stroke = borderElementProxy.Stroke;
				double strokeThickness = borderElementProxy.StrokeThickness;
				DoubleCollection strokeDashArray = borderElementProxy.StrokeDashArray;
				string key = stroke.ToString() + strokeThickness + ((strokeDashArray != null) ? strokeDashArray.ToString() : "");
				if (!_penCache.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
				{
					value = new Pen();
					value.Brush = borderElementProxy.Stroke;
					value.Thickness = strokeThickness;
					if (borderElementProxy.StrokeDashArray != null)
					{
						value.DashStyle = new DashStyle(borderElementProxy.StrokeDashArray, 0.0);
					}
					else
					{
						value.DashStyle = DashStyles.Solid;
					}
					value.Freeze();
					_penCache.Add(key, value);
				}
				double num = strokeThickness / 2.0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				if (borderElementProxy.IsVertical)
				{
					num2 = 0.0 - num;
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = 0.0 - num;
				}
				num2 -= _lastWindowOffset.X;
				num3 -= _lastWindowOffset.Y;
				drawingContext.DrawLine(point0: new Point(SnapDouble(borderElementProxy.X1InTextAreaCoords) - num2, SnapDouble(borderElementProxy.Y1InTextAreaCoords) - num3), point1: new Point(SnapDouble(borderElementProxy.X2InTextAreaCoords) - num2, SnapDouble(borderElementProxy.Y2InTextAreaCoords) - num3), pen: value);
			}
		}

		private Point GetWindowOffset()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = ContentElementHost.LayoutProvider.DocumentView;
			Window window = Window.GetWindow(documentView.RichTextEditor);
			FrameworkElement presenter = documentView.Presenter;
			if (window != null && presenter.IsDescendantOf(window))
			{
				return presenter.TransformToAncestor(window).Transform(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			}
			return new Point(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		private double SnapDouble(double value)
		{
			return value + Extent.CalculateDpiOffsetAdjustment(value, alwaysRoundUp: true);
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "BackgroundRect", Type = typeof(Rectangle))]
	public class RichTextCaretPresenter : ContentControl
	{
		private const string BackgroundRectName = "BackgroundRect";

		private PropertyValueTracker _textUpdateModeTracker;

		public Caret Caret { get; private set; }

		static RichTextCaretPresenter()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichTextCaretPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(RichTextCaretPresenter)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichTextCaretPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		internal RichTextCaretPresenter(Caret caret)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(caret, "caret");
			Caret = caret;
			_textUpdateModeTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(Caret.RichTextEditor, DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(XamRichTextEditor.TextUpdateModeProperty), UpdateCaretModeStates);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			UpdateCaretModeStates();
		}

		private void UpdateCaretModeStates()
		{
			switch (Caret.RichTextEditor.TextUpdateMode)
			{
			case RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Insert:
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "InsertMode", useTransitions: false);
				break;
			case RichDocumentViewTextUpdateMode.Overwrite:
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "OverwriteMode", useTransitions: false);
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ContentElementClippingHost : Panel, IContentElementHost
	{
		private bool _isInitialized;

		private VisualCollection _childVisuals;

		private BorderRenderingSurface _borderRenderingSurface;

		protected override int VisualChildrenCount => _childVisuals.Count + 1;

		internal ILayoutBlock LayoutBlock { get; private set; }

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; set; }

		private LayoutProvider LayoutProvider { get; set; }

		bool IContentElementHost.IsRootContentElementHost => false;

		LayoutPlane IContentElementHost.LayoutPlane => LayoutProvider.LayoutPlane;

		LayoutProvider IContentElementHost.LayoutProvider => LayoutProvider;

		internal ContentElementClippingHost(ILayoutBlock layoutBlock)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutBlock, "layoutBlock");
			LayoutBlock = layoutBlock;
			_childVisuals = new VisualCollection(this);
			_borderRenderingSurface = new BorderRenderingSurface(this);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			return LayoutProvider.ArrangeContentElements(finalSize);
		}

		protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > _childVisuals.Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			if (index == _childVisuals.Count)
			{
				return _borderRenderingSurface;
			}
			return _childVisuals[index];
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size size = LayoutProvider.CreateAndMeasureContentElements(availableSize);
			_borderRenderingSurface.InvalidateVisual();
			_borderRenderingSurface.Measure(size);
			return RichTextEditorUtilities.GetNonNanSize(availableSize, size);
		}

		internal void Initialize(LayoutProvider layoutProvider)
		{
			if (!_isInitialized)
			{
				CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutProvider, "layoutProvider");
				LayoutProvider = layoutProvider;
				DocumentView = layoutProvider.DocumentView;
				_isInitialized = true;
			}
		}

		void IContentElementHost.AddChild(DependencyObject child)
		{
			_childVisuals.Add(child as Visual);
		}

		void IContentElementHost.InsertChild(int index, DependencyObject child)
		{
			_childVisuals.Insert(index, child as Visual);
		}

		void IContentElementHost.ClearChildren()
		{
			_childVisuals.Clear();
		}

		void IContentElementHost.RemoveChild(DependencyObject child)
		{
			_childVisuals.Remove(child as Visual);
		}

		IEnumerable<Visual> IContentElementHost.GetLineVisuals()
		{
			VisualCollection.Enumerator enumerator = _childVisuals.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				yield return enumerator.Current;
			}
			yield return _borderRenderingSurface;
		}
	}
