	internal class ImageContainer : Panel
	{
		internal ImageContainer(bool clipToBounds)
		{
			base.ClipToBounds = clipToBounds;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Arrange(new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), child.DesiredSize));
			}
			return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size availableSize2 = new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Measure(availableSize2);
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}
	}
	public class RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel : Panel
	{
		private const string HorizontalSplitterName = "HorizontalSplitter";

		private RichDocumentViewSplitter _horizontalSplitter;

		private XamRichTextEditor _richTextEditor;

		private double _horizontalSplitterPosition;

		private RichDocumentViewSplitter _dragOperationSplitter;

		private double _dragOperationOverallExtent;

		private double _dragOperationStartingSplitPosition;

		private double _dragOperationCumulativeDelta;

		private const double MIN_SPLITTER_PERCENTAGE = 0.003;

		internal double HorizontalSplitterPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return _horizontalSplitterPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				_horizontalSplitterPosition = value;
			}
		}

		private XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor => _richTextEditor;

		public RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel()
		{
			HorizontalSplitterPosition = 0.5;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			ObservableCollection<RichDocumentView> views = RichTextEditor.Views;
			if (views.Count < 1)
			{
				return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
			}
			if (views.Count == 1)
			{
				views[0].Presenter.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, finalSize.Width, finalSize.Height));
			}
			else
			{
				double height = _horizontalSplitter.DesiredSize.Height;
				double num = 0.0;
				double val = finalSize.Height * HorizontalSplitterPosition - height;
				val = Math.Max(0.0, val);
				views[0].Presenter.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, num, finalSize.Width, val));
				num += val;
				_horizontalSplitter.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, num, finalSize.Width, height));
				num += height;
				views[1].Presenter.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, num, finalSize.Width, Math.Max(0.0, finalSize.Height - val - height)));
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			ObservableCollection<RichDocumentView> views = RichTextEditor.Views;
			if (views.Count < 1)
			{
				return base.MeasureOverride(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetNonNanSize(availableSize, new Size(0.0, 0.0)));
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			bool flag = _dragOperationSplitter != null;
			if (flag)
			{
				availableSize = new Size(availableSize.Width, _dragOperationOverallExtent);
			}
			if (views.Count == 1)
			{
				views[0].Presenter.Measure(availableSize);
				num = views[0].Presenter.DesiredSize.Width;
				num2 = views[0].Presenter.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			else
			{
				_horizontalSplitter.Measure(new Size(1.0, availableSize.Height));
				double height = _horizontalSplitter.DesiredSize.Height;
				double val = availableSize.Height * HorizontalSplitterPosition - height;
				val = Math.Max(0.0, val);
				double val2 = availableSize.Height - val - height;
				val2 = Math.Max(0.0, val2);
				if (!double.IsNaN(val))
				{
					views[0].Presenter.Measure(new Size(availableSize.Width, val));
				}
				if (!double.IsNaN(val2))
				{
					views[1].Presenter.Measure(new Size(availableSize.Width, val2));
				}
				num = Math.Max(views[0].Presenter.DesiredSize.Width, views[1].Presenter.DesiredSize.Width);
				num2 = views[0].Presenter.DesiredSize.Height + views[1].Presenter.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			return new Size(num, flag ? _dragOperationOverallExtent : num2);
		}

		internal void AddViewPresenter(RichDocumentViewPresenter viewPresenter)
		{
			base.Children.Add(viewPresenter);
		}

		internal void Initialize(XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor)
		{
			_richTextEditor = RichTextEditor;
			_horizontalSplitter = FindName("HorizontalSplitter") as RichDocumentViewSplitter;
			if (_horizontalSplitter == null)
			{
				_horizontalSplitter = new RichDocumentViewSplitter(this)
				{
					Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
					Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed
				};
				base.Children.Add(_horizontalSplitter);
				BindSplitterVisibility(_horizontalSplitter, XamRichTextEditor.HorizontalSplitterVisibilityProperty);
			}
		}

		internal void OnMiniSplitterDragStarted(RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter miniSplitter, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			_dragOperationCumulativeDelta = 0.0;
			_dragOperationSplitter = _horizontalSplitter;
			_dragOperationOverallExtent = base.ActualHeight;
			_richTextEditor.OnSplitOperationStarted(isMiniSplit: true);
			_dragOperationStartingSplitPosition = 0.0;
			HorizontalSplitterPosition = 0.0;
			_richTextEditor.HorizontalSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			miniSplitter.Thumb.DragDelta += OnThumbDragDelta;
			miniSplitter.Thumb.DragCompleted += OnThumbDragCompleted;
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		internal void OnSplitterDragStarted(RichDocumentViewSplitter splitter, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			_dragOperationCumulativeDelta = 0.0;
			_dragOperationSplitter = splitter;
			_dragOperationOverallExtent = base.ActualHeight;
			_richTextEditor.OnSplitOperationStarted(isMiniSplit: false);
			_dragOperationStartingSplitPosition = HorizontalSplitterPosition;
			splitter.Thumb.DragDelta += OnThumbDragDelta;
			splitter.Thumb.DragCompleted += OnThumbDragCompleted;
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		internal void RemoveViewPresenter(RichDocumentViewPresenter viewPresenter)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(viewPresenter);
		}

		private void ApplyDeltaToSplitterPosition(double splitterThickness, ref double splitterPosition, double deltaInPixels)
		{
			deltaInPixels = GetCumulativeDelta(deltaInPixels);
			double num = Math.Abs(deltaInPixels) / _dragOperationOverallExtent;
			double y = _dragOperationSplitter.TranslatePoint(new Point(splitterThickness, splitterThickness), this).Y;
			double num2 = Math.Max(0.0, y / _dragOperationOverallExtent);
			if (deltaInPixels < 0.0)
			{
				splitterPosition = Math.Max(0.0, num2 - num);
			}
			else
			{
				splitterPosition = Math.Min(1.0, num2 + num);
			}
		}

		private void BindSplitterVisibility(RichDocumentViewSplitter splitter, DependencyProperty sourceVisibilityProperty)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding(DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(sourceVisibilityProperty));
			binding.Source = _richTextEditor;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			splitter.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding);
		}

		private double GetCumulativeDelta(double changeValue)
		{
			_dragOperationCumulativeDelta = changeValue;
			return _dragOperationCumulativeDelta;
		}

		private void OnThumbDragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (HorizontalSplitterPosition == 0.0)
			{
				_richTextEditor.HorizontalSplitterVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			((Thumb)sender).DragDelta -= OnThumbDragDelta;
			((Thumb)sender).DragCompleted -= OnThumbDragCompleted;
			_dragOperationSplitter = null;
			_richTextEditor.OnSplitOperationEnded();
			_richTextEditor.UpdateScrollBarVisibilityAllViews();
		}

		private void OnThumbDragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			ApplyDeltaToSplitterPosition(_horizontalSplitter.DesiredSize.Height, ref _horizontalSplitterPosition, e.VerticalChange);
			_richTextEditor.IsDocumentViewSplitInternal = _horizontalSplitterPosition > 0.0;
			InvalidateMeasure();
			_richTextEditor.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				_richTextEditor.UpdateScrollBarVisibilityAllViews();
			});
		}
	}
	public class RichDocumentViewMarginPanel : Panel
	{
		private class MarginComparer : IComparer<UIElement>
		{
			private bool _ascending;

			internal MarginComparer(bool ascending)
			{
				_ascending = ascending;
			}

			int IComparer<UIElement>.Compare(UIElement x, UIElement y)
			{
				int marginPosition = RichDocumentViewPresenter.GetMarginPosition(x);
				int marginPosition2 = RichDocumentViewPresenter.GetMarginPosition(y);
				if (_ascending)
				{
					return marginPosition - marginPosition2;
				}
				return marginPosition2 - marginPosition;
			}
		}

		private RichDocumentView _documentView;

		private XamRichTextEditor _richTextEditor;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LocationProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("Location", typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginLocation), typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Left));

		public RichDocumentViewMarginLocation Location
		{
			get
			{
				return (RichDocumentViewMarginLocation)GetValue(LocationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LocationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView
		{
			get
			{
				if (_documentView == null)
				{
					_documentView = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetDocumentViewForElement(this);
				}
				return _documentView;
			}
		}

		private Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				switch (Location)
				{
				case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Left:
				case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Right:
					return Orientation.Vertical;
				case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Top:
				case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Bottom:
					return Orientation.Horizontal;
				default:
					return Orientation.Horizontal;
				}
			}
		}

		private XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor
		{
			get
			{
				if (_richTextEditor == null)
				{
					_richTextEditor = DocumentView.RichTextEditor;
				}
				return _richTextEditor;
			}
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor == null)
			{
				return finalSize;
			}
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			if (children.Count < 1)
			{
				return finalSize;
			}
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement item in children)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				if (Location == RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Left)
				{
					list.Sort(new MarginComparer(ascending: false));
				}
				else
				{
					list.Sort(new MarginComparer(ascending: true));
				}
				double num = 0.0;
				foreach (UIElement item2 in list)
				{
					item2.Arrange(new Rect(num, 0.0, item2.DesiredSize.Width, finalSize.Height));
					num += item2.DesiredSize.Width;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (Location == RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Top)
				{
					list.Sort(new MarginComparer(ascending: false));
				}
				else
				{
					list.Sort(new MarginComparer(ascending: true));
				}
				double num2 = 0.0;
				foreach (UIElement item3 in list)
				{
					item3.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, num2, finalSize.Width, item3.DesiredSize.Height));
					num2 += item3.DesiredSize.Height;
				}
			}
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (RichTextEditor == null)
			{
				return availableSize;
			}
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			if (children.Count < 1)
			{
				return default(Size);
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				foreach (UIElement item in children)
				{
					item.Measure(availableSize);
					num += item.DesiredSize.Width;
					num2 = Math.Max(num2, item.DesiredSize.Height);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (UIElement item2 in children)
				{
					item2.Measure(availableSize);
					num = Math.Max(num, item2.DesiredSize.Width);
					num2 += item2.DesiredSize.Height;
				}
			}
			return new Size(num, num2);
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "Thumb", Type = typeof(Thumb))]
	public class RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter : Control
	{
		private const string ThumbName = "Thumb";

		private Thumb _thumb;

		private RichDocumentView _documentView;

		private XamRichTextEditor _richTextEditor;

		internal Thumb Thumb => _thumb;

		private RichDocumentView DocumentView
		{
			get
			{
				if (_documentView == null)
				{
					_documentView = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetDocumentViewForElement(this);
				}
				return _documentView;
			}
		}

		private RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel LayoutPanel => RichTextEditor.RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel;

		private XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor
		{
			get
			{
				if (_richTextEditor == null)
				{
					_richTextEditor = DocumentView.RichTextEditor;
				}
				return _richTextEditor;
			}
		}

		static RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter)));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_thumb = GetTemplateChild("Thumb") as Thumb;
			if (_thumb != null)
			{
				_thumb.DragStarted += OnThumbDragStarted;
			}
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Horizontal", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void OnThumbDragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				LayoutPanel.OnMiniSplitterDragStarted(this, e);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TextArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewTextArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TextAreaBorder", Type = typeof(Border))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TextAreaHostGrid", Type = typeof(Grid))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "VerticalScrollBarArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "HorizontalScrollBarArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "LeftMarginArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TopMarginArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "RightMarginArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "BottomMarginArea", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TextAreaLayoutTransformer", Type = typeof(LayoutTransformer))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TextAreaScaleTransform", Type = typeof(ScaleTransform))]
	public class RichDocumentViewPresenter : Control, IImeEditor
	{
		private const string TextAreaName = "TextArea";

		private const string TextAreaBorderName = "TextAreaBorder";

		private const string TextAreaHostGridName = "TextAreaHostGrid";

		private const string VerticalScrollBarAreaName = "VerticalScrollBarArea";

		private const string HorizontalScrollBarAreaName = "HorizontalScrollBarArea";

		private const string LeftMarginAreaName = "LeftMarginArea";

		private const string TopMarginAreaName = "TopMarginArea";

		private const string RightMarginAreaName = "RightMarginArea";

		private const string BottomMarginAreaName = "BottomMarginArea";

		private const string TextAreaLayoutTransformerName = "TextAreaLayoutTransformer";

		private const string TextAreaScaleTransformName = "TextAreaScaleTransform";

		private RichDocumentViewTextArea _textArea;

		private Border _textAreaBorder;

		private Grid _textAreaHostGrid;

		private RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea _verticalScrollBarArea;

		private RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea _horizontalScrollBarArea;

		private RichDocumentViewMarginPanel _leftMarginArea;

		private RichDocumentViewMarginPanel _topMarginArea;

		private RichDocumentViewMarginPanel _rightMarginArea;

		private RichDocumentViewMarginPanel _bottomMarginArea;

		private PropertyValueTracker _customMarginVersionTracker;

		private PropertyValueTracker _zoomLevelTracker;

		private ScaleTransform _textAreaScaleTransform;

		private LayoutTransformer _textAreaLayoutTransformer;

		private bool _hasFocus;

		private ImeEditorHelper _imeEditorHelper;

		private EditorUndoTransaction _currentCompositionTransaction;

		private CharacterSettings _pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollbarIntersectionBrushProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey MarginDefinitionPropertyKey;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty MarginDefinitionProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyPropertyKey MarginPositionPropertyKey;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty MarginPositionProperty;

		public Brush ScrollbarIntersectionBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (Brush)GetValue(ScrollbarIntersectionBrushProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ScrollbarIntersectionBrushProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; private set; }

		internal bool HasFocusResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hasFocus || base.IsFocused || base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
				{
					return DocumentView.RichTextEditor.IsKeyboardFocusWithin;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea HorizontalScrollBarArea => _horizontalScrollBarArea;

		internal XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; private set; }

		internal RichDocumentViewTextArea TextArea => _textArea;

		internal Grid TextAreaHostGrid => _textAreaHostGrid;

		internal RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea VerticalScrollBarArea => _verticalScrollBarArea;

		string IImeEditor.DebugId => "XamRichTextEditor";

		FrameworkElement IImeEditor.EditorElement => this;

		ImeMessageHookMode IImeEditor.ImeMessageHookMode => ImeMessageHookMode.ImeMessageHookModeAutomaticOnKeyboardFocus;

		ImeEditorHelperState IImeEditor.ImeEditorHelperState => DocumentView.ImeEditorHelperState;

		Rect IImeEditor.PreferredCompositionWindowRect => GetPreferredCompositionWindowRect();

		int IImeEditor.SelectionLength => DocumentView.Selection.Length;

		int IImeEditor.SelectionStart => DocumentView.Selection.Start;

		int IImeEditor.SelectionEnd => DocumentView.Selection.End;

		static RichDocumentViewPresenter()
		{
			ScrollbarIntersectionBrushProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("ScrollbarIntersectionBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Black), null));
			MarginDefinitionPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("MarginDefinition", typeof(MarginBase), typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter), null, null);
			MarginDefinitionProperty = MarginDefinitionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			MarginPositionPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyUtilities.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("MarginPosition", typeof(int), typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter), 0, null);
			MarginPositionProperty = MarginPositionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter)));
			Style style = new Style();
			style.Seal();
			FrameworkElement.FocusVisualStyleProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(style));
		}

		internal RichDocumentViewPresenter(RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			_customMarginVersionTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(RichTextEditor, DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(XamRichTextEditor.CustomMarginVersionProperty), OnCustomMarginVersionChanged);
			_zoomLevelTracker = new PropertyValueTracker(RichTextEditor, DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(XamRichTextEditor.ZoomLevelProperty), OnZoomLevelChanged);
			UpdateImeEditorHelperStatus();
			BindImeAttachedPropertiesToRichTextEditor();
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.CreateAdornmentGeneratorsIfNecessary();
			}
			_textArea = GetTemplateChild("TextArea") as RichDocumentViewTextArea;
			if (_textArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "TextArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewTextArea).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_textArea.Initialize(DocumentView);
			_textAreaBorder = GetTemplateChild("TextAreaBorder") as Border;
			if (_textAreaBorder == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "TextAreaBorder", typeof(Border).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_textAreaHostGrid = GetTemplateChild("TextAreaHostGrid") as Grid;
			if (_textAreaHostGrid != null)
			{
				_textAreaHostGrid.ClipToBounds = true;
			}
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.Source = RichTextEditor;
			binding.Path = new PropertyPath("DocumentBackground");
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			_textAreaHostGrid.SetBinding(Panel.BackgroundProperty, binding);
			_verticalScrollBarArea = GetTemplateChild("VerticalScrollBarArea") as RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea;
			if (_verticalScrollBarArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "VerticalScrollBarArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_horizontalScrollBarArea = GetTemplateChild("HorizontalScrollBarArea") as RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea;
			if (_horizontalScrollBarArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "HorizontalScrollBarArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_leftMarginArea = GetTemplateChild("LeftMarginArea") as RichDocumentViewMarginPanel;
			if (_leftMarginArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "LeftMarginArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_topMarginArea = GetTemplateChild("TopMarginArea") as RichDocumentViewMarginPanel;
			if (_topMarginArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "TopMarginArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_rightMarginArea = GetTemplateChild("RightMarginArea") as RichDocumentViewMarginPanel;
			if (_rightMarginArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "RightMarginArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_bottomMarginArea = GetTemplateChild("BottomMarginArea") as RichDocumentViewMarginPanel;
			if (_bottomMarginArea == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "BottomMarginArea", typeof(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_textAreaLayoutTransformer = GetTemplateChild("TextAreaLayoutTransformer") as LayoutTransformer;
			if (_textAreaLayoutTransformer == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "TextAreaLayoutTransformer", typeof(LayoutTransformer).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			_textAreaScaleTransform = GetTemplateChild("TextAreaScaleTransform") as ScaleTransform;
			if (_textAreaScaleTransform == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "TextAreaScaleTransform", typeof(ScaleTransform).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewPresenter).Name));
			}
			binding = new Binding();
			binding.Source = RichTextEditor;
			binding.Path = new PropertyPath("DocumentBackground");
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			SetBinding(Control.BackgroundProperty, binding);
			SyncZoomLevel();
			CreateBuiltInMargins();
			VerifyCustomMargins();
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		protected override void OnGotFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnGotFocus(e);
			_hasFocus = true;
			DocumentView?.Caret.UpdateVisibility();
			if (TextArea != null)
			{
				TextArea.AdornmentLayersManager.InvalidateAllLayers();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.KeyboardHandler.OnKeyDown(ref e);
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyUp(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.KeyboardHandler.OnKeyUp(ref e);
			}
			base.OnKeyUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnLostFocus(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnLostFocus(e);
			_hasFocus = false;
			DocumentView?.Caret.UpdateVisibility();
			if (TextArea != null)
			{
				TextArea.AdornmentLayersManager.InvalidateAllLayers();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseEnter(e);
			}
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseLeave(e);
			}
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (documentView != null)
			{
				if (e.ClickCount == 2)
				{
					documentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseDoubleClick(e);
				}
				else
				{
					documentView.Activate();
					ForceFocus();
					documentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
				}
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			documentView.Activate();
			ForceFocus();
			documentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseRightButtonDown(e);
			base.OnMouseRightButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseRightButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseRightButtonUp(e);
			base.OnMouseRightButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null)
			{
				DocumentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseMove(e);
			}
			base.OnMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DocumentView != null && !RichTextEditor.ShouldZoom())
			{
				DocumentView.MouseHandler.OnMouseWheel(e);
			}
			base.OnMouseWheel(e);
		}

		protected override void OnTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			DocumentView.KeyboardHandler.OnTextInput(ref e);
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   In RichDocumentViewPresenter.OnTextInput. Text = '" + e.Text + "'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
			base.OnTextInput(e);
		}

		internal static MarginBase GetMarginDefinition(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (MarginBase)d.GetValue(MarginDefinitionProperty);
		}

		internal static int GetMarginPosition(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (int)d.GetValue(MarginPositionProperty);
		}

		internal void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
		}

		internal void ForceFocus()
		{
			Focus();
			Keyboard.Focus(this);
		}

		internal void ForceImeCompositionEnd()
		{
			if (_imeEditorHelper != null && DocumentView.ImeEditorHelperState.IsInComposition)
			{
				_imeEditorHelper.CompleteComposition();
			}
		}

		internal double GetMarginAreaHeight(RichDocumentViewMarginLocation marginLocation)
		{
			return marginLocation switch
			{
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Left => _leftMarginArea.ActualHeight, 
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Top => _topMarginArea.ActualHeight, 
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Right => _rightMarginArea.ActualHeight, 
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Bottom => _bottomMarginArea.ActualHeight, 
				_ => 0.0, 
			};
		}

		internal double GetMarginAreaWidth(RichDocumentViewMarginLocation marginLocation)
		{
			return marginLocation switch
			{
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Left => _leftMarginArea.ActualWidth, 
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Top => _topMarginArea.ActualWidth, 
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Right => _rightMarginArea.ActualWidth, 
				RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Bottom => _bottomMarginArea.ActualWidth, 
				_ => 0.0, 
			};
		}

		internal void OnCursorChanged(Cursor newCursor)
		{
			base.Cursor = newCursor;
			if (_textAreaBorder != null)
			{
				_textAreaBorder.Cursor = newCursor;
			}
		}

		internal void OnDocumentReadOnlyStatusChange()
		{
			UpdateImeEditorHelperStatus();
		}

		internal void OnImeStateChanged()
		{
			UpdateImeEditorHelperStatus();
		}

		internal void RemoveAllMarginPresenters()
		{
			RemoveMarginPresentersFromMarginPanel(_leftMarginArea, includeBuiltInMarginPresenters: true);
			RemoveMarginPresentersFromMarginPanel(_topMarginArea, includeBuiltInMarginPresenters: true);
			RemoveMarginPresentersFromMarginPanel(_rightMarginArea, includeBuiltInMarginPresenters: true);
			RemoveMarginPresentersFromMarginPanel(_bottomMarginArea, includeBuiltInMarginPresenters: true);
		}

		private void AddMarginToPanel(MarginBase margin, FrameworkElement marginPresenter)
		{
			marginPresenter.SetValue(MarginDefinitionPropertyKey, margin);
			switch (margin.Location)
			{
			case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Left:
				_leftMarginArea.Children.Add(marginPresenter);
				break;
			case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Top:
				_topMarginArea.Children.Add(marginPresenter);
				break;
			case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Right:
				_rightMarginArea.Children.Add(marginPresenter);
				break;
			case RichDocumentViewMarginLocation.Bottom:
				_bottomMarginArea.Children.Add(marginPresenter);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void BindBuiltInMarginVisibility(FrameworkElement marginPresenter, DependencyProperty property)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding(DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(property));
			binding.Source = RichTextEditor;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			marginPresenter.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding);
		}

		private void BindImeAttachedPropertiesToRichTextEditor()
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.Path = new PropertyPath(InputLanguageManager.InputLanguageProperty);
			binding.Source = DocumentView.RichTextEditor;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			SetBinding(InputLanguageManager.InputLanguageProperty, binding);
			binding = new Binding();
			binding.Path = new PropertyPath(InputMethod.PreferredImeStateProperty);
			binding.Source = DocumentView.RichTextEditor;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			SetBinding(InputMethod.PreferredImeStateProperty, binding);
		}

		private void CreateBuiltInMargins()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			if (documentView != null)
			{
				foreach (MarginBase builtInMargin in documentView.BuiltInMargins)
				{
					FrameworkElement marginPresenter = builtInMargin.CreatePresenter(DocumentView);
					AddMarginToPanel(builtInMargin, marginPresenter);
				}
			}
			SortAllMarginPositions();
		}

		private bool GetIsImeEnabledOnControl()
		{
			if (!InputMethod.GetIsInputMethodSuspended(RichTextEditor))
			{
				return InputMethod.GetIsInputMethodEnabled(RichTextEditor);
			}
			return false;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private Rect GetPreferredCompositionWindowRect()
		{
			Point pixelLocation = RichTextEditor.Caret.PixelLocation;
			Point point = new Point(pixelLocation.X, pixelLocation.Y);
			Rect result = new Rect(point.X, point.Y, 100.0, 100.0);
			PresentationSource presentationSource = PresentationSource.FromVisual(this);
			Visual rootVisual = presentationSource.RootVisual;
			if (rootVisual != null)
			{
				Point point2 = TransformToAncestor(rootVisual).Transform(new Point(result.Left, result.Top));
				result = new Rect(point2.X, point2.Y, result.Width, result.Height);
				Matrix transformToDevice = presentationSource.CompositionTarget.TransformToDevice;
				result.Transform(transformToDevice);
				return result;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private Rect GetTransformedRectFromTextAreaPoints(Point topLeft, Point bottomRight, Rect defaultRect)
		{
			Rect result = defaultRect;
			PresentationSource presentationSource = PresentationSource.FromVisual(this);
			if (presentationSource != null)
			{
				Visual rootVisual = presentationSource.RootVisual;
				if (rootVisual != null)
				{
					GeneralTransform generalTransform = TransformToAncestor(rootVisual);
					generalTransform.TryTransform(topLeft, out topLeft);
					generalTransform.TryTransform(bottomRight, out bottomRight);
					HwndSource obj = PresentationSource.FromVisual(this) as HwndSource;
					topLeft = obj.CompositionTarget.TransformToDevice.Transform(topLeft);
					bottomRight = obj.CompositionTarget.TransformToDevice.Transform(bottomRight);
					result.X = topLeft.X;
					result.Y = topLeft.Y;
					result.Width = Math.Max(100.0, bottomRight.X - topLeft.X);
					result.Height = Math.Max(30.0, bottomRight.Y - topLeft.Y);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void OnCustomMarginVersionChanged()
		{
			VerifyCustomMargins();
		}

		private void OnZoomLevelChanged()
		{
			SyncZoomLevel();
		}

		private void RemoveMarginPresentersFromMarginPanel(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel marginPanel, bool includeBuiltInMarginPresenters)
		{
			if (marginPanel == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			UIElementCollection children = marginPanel.Children;
			for (int num = children.Count - 1; num > -1; num--)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = children[num];
				MarginBase marginDefinition = GetMarginDefinition(uIElement);
				if (marginDefinition != null && (!marginDefinition.IsBuiltInMargin || includeBuiltInMarginPresenters))
				{
					marginDefinition.OnPresenterDiscarded(uIElement as FrameworkElement);
					children.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SortAllMarginPositions()
		{
			SortMarginPositionsInLocation(_leftMarginArea);
			SortMarginPositionsInLocation(_topMarginArea);
			SortMarginPositionsInLocation(_rightMarginArea);
			SortMarginPositionsInLocation(_bottomMarginArea);
		}

		private void SortMarginPositionsInLocation(RichDocumentViewMarginPanel marginPanel)
		{
			UIElementCollection children = marginPanel.Children;
			int count = children.Count;
			List<ISupportPositioning> list = new List<ISupportPositioning>(count);
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				MarginBase marginDefinition = GetMarginDefinition(children[i]);
				if (marginDefinition != null)
				{
					list.Add(marginDefinition);
				}
			}
			PositionSorter.Sort(list, out var isValid);
			if (!isValid)
			{
				RichTextEditorUtilities.LogDebuggerError(1, RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("DBG_LayoutCategory"), RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("DBG_InvalidISupportPositioningInfo"));
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
			{
				MarginBase marginDefinition2 = GetMarginDefinition(children[j]);
				int num = list.IndexOf(marginDefinition2);
				(children[j] as FrameworkElement).SetValue(MarginPositionPropertyKey, num);
			}
		}

		private void SyncZoomLevel()
		{
			_textAreaScaleTransform.ScaleX = RichTextEditor.ZoomLevelResolved;
			_textAreaScaleTransform.ScaleY = RichTextEditor.ZoomLevelResolved;
		}

		private void UpdateImeEditorHelperStatus()
		{
			bool isImeEnabledOnControl = GetIsImeEnabledOnControl();
			bool isReadOnlyResolved = RichTextEditor.IsReadOnlyResolved;
			if (isImeEnabledOnControl && !isReadOnlyResolved)
			{
				if (_imeEditorHelper == null)
				{
					_imeEditorHelper = new ImeEditorHelper(this);
				}
			}
			else if (_imeEditorHelper != null)
			{
				_imeEditorHelper.Dispose();
				_imeEditorHelper = null;
			}
		}

		private void VerifyCustomMargins()
		{
		}

		void IImeEditor.DeleteRange(int start, int length)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			RichTextEditorUtilities.ReplaceTextHelper(span: new DocumentSpan(start, length), caretDocumentOffset: new VersionedDocumentOffset(documentView.Document, start), documentView: documentView, document: documentView.Document, text: "", characterSettings: _pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition);
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.DeleteRange Start= '" + start + "', Length='" + length + "'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
		}

		void IImeEditor.EnsureCaretIsVisible()
		{
			DocumentView.VerifyLineWithCaretIsFullyInViewAfterNextLayout();
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.EnsureCaretIsVisible", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
		}

		Rect IImeEditor.GetCandidateWindowExclusionRect(int compositionStartOffset, int compositionEndOffset)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			Rect rect = new Rect(new Size(100.0, 30.0));
			compositionStartOffset = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(compositionStartOffset, documentView.Document);
			compositionEndOffset = RichTextEditorUtilities.EnsureOffsetIsValid(compositionEndOffset, documentView.Document);
			Point? point = documentView.TextAreaPointFromDocumentOffset(compositionStartOffset);
			if (!point.HasValue)
			{
				return rect;
			}
			Point value = point.Value;
			value.X = Math.Max(0.0, value.X - documentView.HorizontalViewOffset);
			Point bottomRight = rect.BottomRight;
			CaretLayoutInfo? caretLayoutInfo = documentView.GetCaretLayoutInfo(compositionEndOffset);
			if (caretLayoutInfo.HasValue)
			{
				bottomRight = new Point(caretLayoutInfo.Value.OverallTopLeftCaretPoint.X, caretLayoutInfo.Value.OverallTopLeftCaretPoint.Y + caretLayoutInfo.Value.CaretHeight);
			}
			bottomRight.X = Math.Max(0.0, bottomRight.X - documentView.HorizontalViewOffset);
			return GetTransformedRectFromTextAreaPoints(value, bottomRight, rect);
		}

		void IImeEditor.InsertText(int offset, string text, bool advanceCaret, ImeClauseAdornmentStyle adornmentStyle)
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			VersionedDocumentOffset caretDocumentOffset = new VersionedDocumentOffset(documentView.Document, offset);
			CharacterSettings characterSettings = Infragistics.Documents.RichText.Utilities.MergeThreeLazy(null, documentView.Selection.PendingCharacterSettings, _pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition) ?? new CharacterSettings();
			switch (adornmentStyle)
			{
			case ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.DottedLine:
				characterSettings.UnderlineType = UnderlineType.Dotted;
				break;
			case ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.SolidLine:
				characterSettings.UnderlineType = UnderlineType.Single;
				break;
			case ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.SolidThickLine:
				characterSettings.UnderlineType = UnderlineType.Thick;
				break;
			case ImeClauseAdornmentStyle.WavyLine:
				characterSettings.UnderlineType = UnderlineType.Wave;
				break;
			}
			if (offset == documentView.Selection.Start)
			{
				documentView.KeyboardHandler.OnTextInput(text, characterSettings);
			}
			else
			{
				RichTextEditorUtilities.InsertTextHelper(documentView, documentView.Document, caretDocumentOffset, text, characterSettings, mergeWithExistingSettings: true);
			}
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.InsertText Offset= '" + offset + "', Text='" + text + ", Style: '" + Enum.GetName(typeof(ImeClauseAdornmentStyle), adornmentStyle) + "', AdvanceCaret='" + advanceCaret.ToString() + "'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
		}

		void IImeEditor.OnCompositionStarting()
		{
			RichDocumentView documentView = DocumentView;
			RichTextDocument document = documentView.Document;
			_currentCompositionTransaction = new EditorUndoTransaction(document, documentView, documentView.Caret.DocumentOffset, null, null, suppressExecuteActions: true);
			CharacterSettings pendingCharacterSettings = documentView.Selection.PendingCharacterSettings;
			if (pendingCharacterSettings != null)
			{
				if (_pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition == null)
				{
					_pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition = new CharacterSettings();
				}
				ApplyCharacterSettings(pendingCharacterSettings, _pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition);
			}
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.OnCompositionStarting - Starting Transaction'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
			document.UndoManager.StartTransaction(_currentCompositionTransaction);
		}

		private void ApplyCharacterSettings(CharacterSettings source, CharacterSettings target)
		{
			if (source.Bold.HasValue && source.Bold.Value != target.Bold)
			{
				target.Bold = source.Bold;
			}
			if (source.Italics.HasValue && source.Italics.Value != target.Italics)
			{
				target.Italics = source.Italics;
			}
			if (source.UnderlineType.HasValue && source.UnderlineType.Value != target.UnderlineType)
			{
				target.UnderlineType = source.UnderlineType;
			}
			if (source.UnderlineType.HasValue && source.UnderlineType.Value != target.UnderlineType)
			{
				target.UnderlineType = source.UnderlineType;
			}
			if (source.StrikeThrough.HasValue && source.StrikeThrough.Value != target.StrikeThrough)
			{
				target.StrikeThrough = source.StrikeThrough;
			}
			if (source.DoubleStrikeThrough.HasValue && source.DoubleStrikeThrough.Value != target.DoubleStrikeThrough)
			{
				target.DoubleStrikeThrough = source.DoubleStrikeThrough;
			}
			if (source.FontSize.HasValue)
			{
				Extent value = source.FontSize.Value;
				Extent? fontSize = target.FontSize;
				if (value != fontSize)
				{
					target.FontSize = source.FontSize;
				}
			}
			if (source.FontSettings != null && source.FontSettings != target.FontSettings)
			{
				target.FontSettings = source.FontSettings;
			}
			if (source.Color != null && source.Color != target.Color)
			{
				target.Color = source.Color;
			}
			if (source.UnderlineColor != null && source.UnderlineColor != target.UnderlineColor)
			{
				target.UnderlineColor = source.UnderlineColor;
			}
			if (source.HighlightColor.HasValue && source.HighlightColor != target.HighlightColor)
			{
				target.HighlightColor = source.HighlightColor;
			}
		}

		void IImeEditor.OnCompositionEnding(bool compositionDiscarded)
		{
			if (_currentCompositionTransaction == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!_currentCompositionTransaction.IsClosed)
			{
				if (compositionDiscarded)
				{
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.OnCompositionEnding - Rolling Back Transaction'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
					_currentCompositionTransaction.Rollback();
				}
				else
				{
					ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.OnCompositionEnding - Committing Transaction'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
					_currentCompositionTransaction.Commit();
				}
			}
			_currentCompositionTransaction = null;
			_pendingCharacterSettingsForComposition = null;
		}

		Tuple<int, int> IImeEditor.SetSelection(int start, int length)
		{
			Selection selection = DocumentView.Selection;
			selection.SetRange(start, start + length);
			ImeDebugHelpers.WriteImeDebugMessage("@@@@   IImeEditor.SetSelection Start= '" + start + "', Length='" + length + "'", ((IImeEditor)this).DebugId);
			return new Tuple<int, int>(selection.Start, selection.Length);
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "MiniSplitter", Type = typeof(RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "ScrollBar", Type = typeof(ScrollBar))]
	public class RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea : Control
	{
		private const string MiniSplitterName = "MiniSplitter";

		private const string ScrollBarName = "ScrollBar";

		private RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter _miniSplitter;

		private ScrollBar _scrollBar;

		private RichDocumentView _documentView;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollBarVisibilityProperty;

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ScrollBarVisibility ScrollBarVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (ScrollBarVisibility)GetValue(ScrollBarVisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ScrollBarVisibilityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal RichDocumentView DocumentView => _documentView;

		internal RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter MiniSplitter => _miniSplitter;

		internal ScrollBar ScrollBar => _scrollBar;

		internal XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor => _documentView.RichTextEditor;

		static RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.OrientationVerticalBox, OnOrientationPropertyChanged));
			ScrollBarVisibilityProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("ScrollBarVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.VisibilityCollapsedBox));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_documentView = RichTextEditorUtilities.GetDocumentViewForElement(this);
			_miniSplitter = GetTemplateChild("MiniSplitter") as RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter;
			if (_miniSplitter == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "MiniSplitter", typeof(RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea).Name));
			}
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				BindMiniSplitterVisibility(_miniSplitter, XamRichTextEditor.HorizontalMiniSplitterVisibilityProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				_miniSplitter.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			ScrollManager scrollManager = DocumentView.ScrollManager;
			_scrollBar = GetTemplateChild("ScrollBar") as ScrollBar;
			if (_scrollBar == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(RichTextEditorUtilities.GetString("LE_MissingTemplatePart", "ScrollBar", typeof(ScrollBar).Name, typeof(RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea).Name));
			}
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				scrollManager.VerticalScrollBar = _scrollBar;
			}
			else
			{
				scrollManager.HorizontalScrollBar = _scrollBar;
			}
			scrollManager.UpdateScrollBarRanges();
			scrollManager.UpdateScrollBarValues();
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.Source = DocumentView;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			binding.Path = ((Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? new PropertyPath("HorizontalScrollBarVisibility") : new PropertyPath("VerticalScrollBarVisibility"));
			SetBinding(ScrollBarVisibilityProperty, binding);
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private static void OnOrientationPropertyChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(obj as RichDocumentViewScrollBarArea).EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private void BindMiniSplitterVisibility(RichDocumentViewMiniSplitter splitter, DependencyProperty sourceVisibilityProperty)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding(DependencyPropertyUtilities.GetName(sourceVisibilityProperty));
			binding.Source = RichTextEditor;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			splitter.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding);
		}

		private void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Horizontal", useTransitions: false);
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "Thumb", Type = typeof(Thumb))]
	public class RichDocumentViewSplitter : Control
	{
		private const string ThumbName = "Thumb";

		private Thumb _thumb;

		private RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel _layoutPanel;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal Thumb Thumb => _thumb;

		static RichDocumentViewSplitter()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyUtilities.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(RichDocumentViewSplitter), DependencyPropertyUtilities.CreateMetadata(KnownBoxes.OrientationVerticalBox, OnOrientationPropertyChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(RichDocumentViewSplitter)));
		}

		internal RichDocumentViewSplitter(RichDocumentViewLayoutPanel layoutPanel)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(layoutPanel, "layoutPanel");
			_layoutPanel = layoutPanel;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_thumb = GetTemplateChild("Thumb") as Thumb;
			if (_thumb != null)
			{
				_thumb.DragStarted += OnThumbDragStarted;
			}
			EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private static void OnOrientationPropertyChanged(DependencyObject obj, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(obj as RichDocumentViewSplitter).EnsureVisualStates();
		}

		private void EnsureVisualStates()
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Horizontal", useTransitions: false);
			}
		}

		private void OnThumbDragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			_layoutPanel.OnSplitterDragStarted(this, e);
		}
	}
	public class RichDocumentViewTextArea : Panel
	{
		private RichDocumentViewPresenter _documentViewPresenter;

		private AdornmentLayersManager _layersManager;

		private const double AUTOSCROLLAREA_LEFTEDGE_EXTENT = 35.0;

		private const double AUTOSCROLLAREA_TOPEDGE_EXTENT = 35.0;

		private const double AUTOSCROLLAREA_RIGHTEDGE_EXTENT = 35.0;

		private const double AUTOSCROLLAREA_BOTTOMEDGE_EXTENT = 35.0;

		internal AdornmentLayersManager AdornmentLayersManager
		{
			get
			{
				if (_layersManager == null)
				{
					_layersManager = new AdornmentLayersManager(this);
				}
				return _layersManager;
			}
		}

		internal RichDocumentView DocumentView { get; private set; }

		internal XamRichTextEditor RichTextEditor { get; private set; }

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			int count = children.Count;
			Rect finalRect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, finalSize.Width, finalSize.Height);
			AdornmentLayer adornmentLayer = AdornmentLayersManager.GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo.BackgroundContent);
			adornmentLayer?.Arrange(finalRect);
			AdornmentLayer adornmentLayer2 = AdornmentLayersManager.GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo.MiddleContent);
			adornmentLayer2?.Arrange(finalRect);
			AdornmentLayer adornmentLayer3 = AdornmentLayersManager.GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo.ForegroundContent);
			adornmentLayer3?.Arrange(finalRect);
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (children[i] != adornmentLayer && children[i] != adornmentLayer2 && children[i] != adornmentLayer3)
				{
					children[i].Arrange(finalRect);
				}
			}
			RichTextEditor.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				DocumentView.ScrollManager.UpdateScrollBarRanges();
			});
			return finalSize;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			UIElementCollection children = base.Children;
			int count = children.Count;
			Size size = availableSize;
			if (AdornmentLayersManager.GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo.MiddleContent) is ContentAdornmentLayerBase contentAdornmentLayerBase)
			{
				contentAdornmentLayerBase.InvalidateMeasure();
				contentAdornmentLayerBase.Measure(availableSize);
				size = contentAdornmentLayerBase.DesiredSize;
				AdornmentLayer adornmentLayer = AdornmentLayersManager.GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo.BackgroundContent);
				adornmentLayer.InvalidateMeasure();
				adornmentLayer.Measure(availableSize);
				AdornmentLayer adornmentLayer2 = AdornmentLayersManager.GetAdornmentLayer(AdornmentLayerInfo.ForegroundContent);
				adornmentLayer2.InvalidateMeasure();
				adornmentLayer2.Measure(availableSize);
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					if (children[i] != adornmentLayer && children[i] != contentAdornmentLayerBase && children[i] != adornmentLayer2)
					{
						children[i].InvalidateMeasure();
						children[i].Measure(size);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				size = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			return size;
		}

		internal Point AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(Point pointIntextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return new Point(pointIntextAreaCoordinates.X - DocumentView.HorizontalViewOffset, pointIntextAreaCoordinates.Y);
		}

		internal bool ContainsPoint(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			double horizontalViewOffset = DocumentView.HorizontalViewOffset;
			return new Rect(horizontalViewOffset, 0.0, base.ActualWidth, base.ActualHeight).Contains(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
		}

		internal bool ContainsX(Point pointRelativeToTextArea)
		{
			double horizontalViewOffset = DocumentView.HorizontalViewOffset;
			if (pointRelativeToTextArea.X >= horizontalViewOffset)
			{
				return pointRelativeToTextArea.X <= base.ActualWidth;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool ContainsY(Point pointRelativeToTextArea)
		{
			_ = DocumentView.HorizontalViewOffset;
			if (pointRelativeToTextArea.Y >= 0.0)
			{
				return pointRelativeToTextArea.Y <= base.ActualHeight;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void Initialize(RichDocumentView documentView)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView, "documentView");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.Presenter, "documentView.Presenter");
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(documentView.RichTextEditor, "documentView.RichTextEditor");
			DocumentView = documentView;
			_documentViewPresenter = documentView.Presenter;
			RichTextEditor = documentView.RichTextEditor;
			AdornmentLayersManager.VerifyLayers();
			documentView.OnTextAreaInitialized();
		}

		internal bool IsPointAbove(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return pointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y < 0.0;
		}

		internal bool IsPointBelow(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return pointInTextAreaCoordinates.Y > base.ActualHeight;
		}

		internal bool IsPointLeft(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(pointInTextAreaCoordinates).X < 0.0;
		}

		internal bool IsPointRight(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			return AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(pointInTextAreaCoordinates).X > base.ActualWidth;
		}

		internal bool IsPointInAnyAutoScrollArea(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			if (GetBottomAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(pointInTextAreaCoordinates) == -1.0 && GetLeftAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(pointInTextAreaCoordinates) == -1.0 && GetRightAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(pointInTextAreaCoordinates) == -1.0)
			{
				return GetTopAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(pointInTextAreaCoordinates) != -1.0;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal double GetBottomAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			Point point = AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			if (point.Y <= base.ActualHeight && point.Y >= base.ActualHeight - 35.0)
			{
				return (35.0 - (base.ActualHeight - point.Y)) / 35.0;
			}
			return -1.0;
		}

		internal double GetLeftAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			Point point = AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			if (point.X <= 35.0 && point.X >= 0.0)
			{
				return (35.0 - point.X) / 35.0;
			}
			return -1.0;
		}

		internal double GetRightAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			Point point = AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			if (point.X <= base.ActualWidth && point.X >= base.ActualWidth - 35.0)
			{
				return (35.0 - (base.ActualWidth - point.X)) / 35.0;
			}
			return -1.0;
		}

		internal double GetTopAutoScrollAreaPenetrationPct(Point pointInTextAreaCoordinates)
		{
			Point point = AdjustTextAreaPointForHorizontalViewOffset(pointInTextAreaCoordinates);
			if (point.Y <= 35.0 && point.Y >= 0.0)
			{
				return (35.0 - point.Y) / 35.0;
			}
			return -1.0;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Controls.Primitives
{
	internal class CustomCursorHelper
	{
		private class CursorAdorner : Adorner
		{
			private UIElement _element;

			private TranslateTransform _transform;

			public double OffsetX
			{
				get
				{
					return _transform.X;
				}
				set
				{
					_transform.X = value;
				}
			}

			public double OffsetY
			{
				get
				{
					return _transform.Y;
				}
				set
				{
					_transform.Y = value;
				}
			}

			protected override int VisualChildrenCount => 1;

			public CursorAdorner(FrameworkElement adornedElement, ContentControl cursorControl)
				: base(adornedElement)
			{
				_element = new Decorator
				{
					Child = cursorControl,
					HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
					VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
				};
				_transform = new TranslateTransform();
				_element.RenderTransform = _transform;
				AddVisualChild(_element);
			}

			protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
			{
				if (index == 0)
				{
					return _element;
				}
				return base.GetVisualChild(index);
			}

			protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
			{
				_element.Arrange(new Rect(finalSize));
				return finalSize;
			}

			protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
			{
				_element.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
				return _element.DesiredSize;
			}
		}

		private enum CursorSource
		{
			OriginalElement,
			Fallback,
			CustomCursorTemplate,
			CustomCursorFile
		}

		private static readonly object UnsetValue = new object();

		private object _oldLocalValue = UnsetValue;

		private Cursor _customCursor;

		private Cursor _fallbackCursor;

		private FrameworkElement _element;

		private CursorSource _currentSource;

		private DataTemplate _cursorTemplate;

		private ContentControl _cursorControl;

		private double? _horizontalOffset;

		private double? _verticalOffset;

		private bool _watchElementEvents;

		private ToolTip _toolTip;

		private AdornerLayer _adornerLayer;

		private CursorAdorner _adorner;

		private CursorSource CurrentSource => _currentSource;

		public DataTemplate CursorTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return _cursorTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_cursorTemplate != value)
				{
					_cursorTemplate = value;
					VerifyCursorSource();
				}
			}
		}

		public Cursor CustomCursor
		{
			get
			{
				return _customCursor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_customCursor != value)
				{
					_customCursor = value;
					VerifyCursorSource();
				}
			}
		}

		public Cursor FallbackCursor
		{
			get
			{
				return _fallbackCursor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _fallbackCursor)
				{
					_fallbackCursor = value;
					VerifyCursorSource();
				}
			}
		}

		public double? HorizontalOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return _horizontalOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				_horizontalOffset = value;
			}
		}

		private bool NeedsToShowCustomTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				if (_currentSource == CursorSource.CustomCursorTemplate)
				{
					if (_toolTip == null || _toolTip.IsOpen)
					{
						if (_adorner != null)
						{
							return _adorner.Parent == null;
						}
						return false;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public double? VerticalOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return _verticalOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				_verticalOffset = value;
			}
		}

		internal CustomCursorHelper(FrameworkElement element, bool watchElementEvents = true)
		{
			CoreUtilities.ValidateNotNull(element, "element");
			_element = element;
			_watchElementEvents = watchElementEvents;
		}

		internal void Clear()
		{
			FallbackCursor = null;
			CursorTemplate = null;
			CustomCursor = null;
		}

		internal void HideTemplate()
		{
			if (_toolTip != null)
			{
				_toolTip.IsOpen = false;
				_toolTip.StaysOpen = false;
			}
			if (_adorner != null)
			{
				_adornerLayer.Remove(_adorner);
			}
		}

		internal bool Initialize(Cursor customCursor, DataTemplate cursorTemplate, Cursor fallbackCursor)
		{
			CursorSource currentSource = _currentSource;
			_customCursor = customCursor;
			_cursorTemplate = cursorTemplate;
			_fallbackCursor = fallbackCursor;
			VerifyCursorSource();
			if (currentSource == _currentSource)
			{
				return NeedsToShowCustomTemplate;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void ProcessMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_currentSource == CursorSource.CustomCursorTemplate)
			{
				if (NeedsToShowCustomTemplate)
				{
					ShowTemplate();
				}
				InitializeOffset(e);
			}
		}

		internal void ShowTemplate()
		{
			if (_currentSource != CursorSource.CustomCursorTemplate)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_toolTip != null)
			{
				_toolTip.IsOpen = true;
				if (_toolTip.Parent is Popup popup)
				{
					popup.PopupAnimation = PopupAnimation.None;
				}
			}
			if (_adorner != null && _adorner.Parent == null)
			{
				_adornerLayer.Add(_adorner);
			}
			_cursorControl.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
		}

		private void CacheOriginalCursor()
		{
			if (_oldLocalValue == UnsetValue)
			{
				BindingExpression bindingExpression = _element.GetBindingExpression(FrameworkElement.CursorProperty);
				if (bindingExpression != null)
				{
					_oldLocalValue = bindingExpression;
				}
				else
				{
					_oldLocalValue = _element.ReadLocalValue(FrameworkElement.CursorProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		private void HookMouseEvents(bool hook)
		{
			if (hook && _watchElementEvents)
			{
				_element.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseEnterEvent, new MouseEventHandler(OnElementMouseEnter), handledEventsToo: true);
				_element.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseMoveEvent, new MouseEventHandler(OnElementMouseMove), handledEventsToo: true);
				_element.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeaveEvent, new MouseEventHandler(OnElementMouseLeave), handledEventsToo: true);
			}
			else
			{
				_element.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseEnterEvent, new MouseEventHandler(OnElementMouseEnter));
				_element.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseMoveEvent, new MouseEventHandler(OnElementMouseMove));
				_element.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeaveEvent, new MouseEventHandler(OnElementMouseLeave));
			}
		}

		private void InitializeCursorTemplate()
		{
			if (_cursorControl == null)
			{
				ContentControl contentControl = new ContentControl();
				contentControl.Margin = default(Thickness);
				contentControl.Padding = default(Thickness);
				contentControl.BorderThickness = default(Thickness);
				contentControl.IsHitTestVisible = false;
				_cursorControl = contentControl;
			}
			bool flag = true;
			if (BrowserInteropHelper.IsBrowserHosted)
			{
				if (_adornerLayer == null)
				{
					_adornerLayer = AdornerLayer.GetAdornerLayer(_element);
				}
				if (_adornerLayer != null && _adorner == null)
				{
					HookMouseEvents(hook: true);
					_adorner = new CursorAdorner(_element, _cursorControl);
				}
				flag = _adorner != null;
			}
			else if (_toolTip == null)
			{
				HookMouseEvents(hook: true);
				ToolTip toolTip = (_toolTip = new ToolTip());
				FrameworkElementFactory visualTree = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ContentPresenter));
				ControlTemplate controlTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(ToolTip))
				{
					VisualTree = visualTree
				};
				controlTemplate.Seal();
				toolTip.Template = controlTemplate;
				toolTip.Margin = default(Thickness);
				toolTip.Padding = default(Thickness);
				toolTip.Placement = System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.PlacementMode.Relative;
				toolTip.PlacementTarget = _element;
				toolTip.HasDropShadow = false;
				toolTip.Content = _cursorControl;
				toolTip.Opened += OnToolTipOpened;
			}
			_cursorControl.ContentTemplate = _cursorTemplate;
			if (flag)
			{
				_element.Cursor = Cursors.None;
			}
		}

		private void InitializeOffset(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			Point position = e.GetPosition(_element);
			double num = _horizontalOffset ?? ((_cursorControl != null) ? (_cursorControl.DesiredSize.Width / 2.0) : 0.0);
			double num2 = _verticalOffset ?? ((_cursorControl != null) ? (_cursorControl.DesiredSize.Height / 2.0) : 0.0);
			position.X -= num;
			position.Y -= num2;
			position.X = Math.Floor(position.X + 0.5);
			position.Y = Math.Floor(position.Y + 0.5);
			if (_toolTip != null)
			{
				_toolTip.HorizontalOffset = position.X;
				_toolTip.VerticalOffset = position.Y;
			}
			if (_adorner != null)
			{
				_adorner.OffsetX = position.X;
				_adorner.OffsetY = position.Y;
			}
		}

		private void OnElementLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_element.Loaded -= OnElementLoaded;
			VerifyCursorSource();
		}

		private void OnElementMouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			InitializeOffset(e);
			ShowTemplate();
		}

		private void OnElementMouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			HideTemplate();
		}

		private void OnElementMouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (NeedsToShowCustomTemplate)
			{
				ShowTemplate();
			}
			InitializeOffset(e);
		}

		private void OnToolTipOpened(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ToolTip)sender).StaysOpen = true;
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG_TEMPLATE")]
		private void Output(string message, params object[] args)
		{
		}

		private void RemoveCursorTemplate()
		{
			HookMouseEvents(hook: false);
			HideTemplate();
			if (_cursorControl != null)
			{
				_cursorControl = null;
			}
			if (_toolTip != null)
			{
				_toolTip.Opened -= OnToolTipOpened;
				_toolTip = null;
			}
			if (_adornerLayer != null)
			{
				_adornerLayer.Remove(_adorner);
				_adorner = null;
				_adornerLayer = null;
			}
		}

		private void RestoreOriginalCursor()
		{
			if (_oldLocalValue != UnsetValue)
			{
				object oldLocalValue = _oldLocalValue;
				_oldLocalValue = UnsetValue;
				if (oldLocalValue == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					_element.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.CursorProperty);
				}
				else if (oldLocalValue is BindingExpressionBase)
				{
					BindingExpressionBase bindingExpressionBase = oldLocalValue as BindingExpressionBase;
					_element.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.CursorProperty, bindingExpressionBase.ParentBindingBase);
				}
				else
				{
					_element.Cursor = oldLocalValue as Cursor;
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetCurrentSource(CursorSource source)
		{
			_currentSource = source;
			switch (source)
			{
			case CursorSource.OriginalElement:
				RemoveCursorTemplate();
				RestoreOriginalCursor();
				break;
			case CursorSource.CustomCursorFile:
				CacheOriginalCursor();
				RemoveCursorTemplate();
				_element.Cursor = _customCursor;
				break;
			case CursorSource.CustomCursorTemplate:
				CacheOriginalCursor();
				InitializeCursorTemplate();
				break;
			case CursorSource.Fallback:
				CacheOriginalCursor();
				RemoveCursorTemplate();
				_element.Cursor = _fallbackCursor;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void VerifyCursorSource()
		{
			CursorSource currentSource;
			if (_element.IsLoaded)
			{
				currentSource = ((_customCursor != null) ? CursorSource.CustomCursorFile : ((_cursorTemplate != null) ? CursorSource.CustomCursorTemplate : ((_fallbackCursor != null) ? CursorSource.Fallback : CursorSource.OriginalElement)));
			}
			else
			{
				_element.Loaded -= OnElementLoaded;
				_element.Loaded += OnElementLoaded;
				currentSource = CursorSource.OriginalElement;
			}
			SetCurrentSource(currentSource);
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "TransformRoot", Type = typeof(Grid))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "Presenter", Type = typeof(ContentPresenter))]
	public sealed class LayoutTransformer : ContentControl
	{
		private const string TransformRootName = "TransformRoot";

		private const string PresenterName = "Presenter";

		public LayoutTransformer()
		{
			base.DefaultStyleKey = typeof(LayoutTransformer);
			base.IsTabStop = false;
		}
	}
}
